### [1] [LCM: Lossless Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050)
*Clint Ehrlich,Theodore Blackman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LCM（Lossless Context Management）的确定性LLM记忆架构，通过递归上下文压缩和递归任务分解两种机制，在长上下文任务上超越了Claude Code等前沿编码智能体，实现了无损状态检索和终止保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有LLM在长上下文任务中记忆管理不足的问题，超越传统LLM和具备本地文件系统访问能力的前沿编码智能体（如Claude Code）的性能表现。

Method: LCM将符号递归分解为两种确定性、引擎管理的机制：(1) 递归上下文压缩——构建层级摘要DAG，压缩旧消息的同时保留指向每个原始消息的无损指针；(2) 递归任务分解——使用引擎管理的并行原语（如LLM-Map）替代模型编写的循环，类似于编程语言设计中从GOTO到结构化控制流的转变。

Result: 在OOLONG长上下文评测基准上，采用LCM增强的编码智能体Volt（基于Opus 4.6）在所有上下文长度（32K到1M tokens）上均取得比Claude Code更高的得分。

Conclusion: LCM既是对递归语言模型（RLM）范式的验证也是扩展，证明了递归上下文操作可以超越传统LLM和具备本地文件系统访问能力的前沿编码智能体；虽然牺牲了最大灵活性，但换来了终止保证、短任务零成本连续性以及所有先前状态的无损可检索性。

Abstract: We introduce Lossless Context Management (LCM), a deterministic architecture for LLM memory that outperforms Claude Code on long-context tasks. When benchmarked using Opus 4.6, our LCM-augmented coding agent, Volt, achieves higher scores than Claude Code on the OOLONG long-context eval, including at every context length between 32K and 1M tokens.
  LCM may be considered both a vindication and extension of the recursive paradigm pioneered by Recursive Language Models (RLMs). Our results demonstrate that recursive context manipulation can outperform not just conventional LLMs, but frontier coding agents with native file-system access.
  LCM departs from RLM by decomposing symbolic recursion into two deterministic, engine-managed mechanisms: recursive context compression, in which a hierarchical summary DAG automatically compacts older messages while retaining lossless pointers to every original; and recursive task partitioning, in which engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map replace model-written loops. This trade-off, analogous to the move from GOTO to structured control flow in program-ming language design, sacrifices maximal flexibility for termination guarantees, zero-cost continuity on short tasks, and lossless retrievability of all prior state.

</details>


### [2] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），通过在强调TD方法中引入对辅助中心化递归的正则化，解决了离策略TD学习中稳定性、投影几何与方差控制之间的结构性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略TD学习在函数逼近中面临稳定性、投影几何与方差控制的结构性权衡。强调TD（ETD）通过后续强调改善了离策略投影几何，但后续迹具有高方差。本文旨在通过Bellman误差中心化重新审视这一权衡，并解决朴素中心化强调扩展中出现的正定性破坏问题。

Method: 提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），该方法保留后续迹，仅对辅助中心化递归进行正则化，相当于将耦合关键矩阵的右下角块从1提升到1+c。推导了RETD核心矩阵，并在保守充分正则化条件下证明收敛性。

Result: 在诊断性线性离策略预测任务上的实验表明，RETD避免了朴素中心化强调学习的不稳定性，保留了有利的强调几何特性，且正则化参数c在诊断任务中展现出稳健的中间区间。

Conclusion: RETD通过正则化中心化递归有效解决了离策略TD学习中的稳定性-几何-方差权衡问题，在保持强调投影几何优势的同时克服了高方差和不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [3] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ANDRE，一种基于注意力的可微神经符号规则提取框架，通过可微的注意力驱动逻辑算子替代规则模板和模糊算子，在概率和噪声数据上实现准确、稳定的一阶逻辑规则学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号和神经符号ILP方法难以扩展到含噪声和概率性设置：经典ILP依赖离散组合搜索，对不确定性脆弱；可微ILP依赖预定义规则模板或不准确的模糊算子，存在梯度消失或逻辑结构近似差的问题。

Method: ANDRE通过注意力驱动的可微合取和析取算子近似逻辑min-max语义，替代规则模板和逻辑算子；支持在规则内软性选择、取反或排除谓词，在连续规则空间中进行优化。

Result: 在经典ILP基准、大规模知识库和含概率谓词与噪声监督的合成数据集上，ANDRE取得竞争性或更优的预测性能，并在不确定性下可靠恢复正确符号规则；对中等标签噪声保持鲁棒，在规则提取质量和稳定性上显著优于现有可微ILP方法。

Conclusion: ANDRE通过注意力机制实现了可微ILP中符号结构与概率推理的有效结合，为噪声和概率场景下的可解释规则学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [4] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro$^2$Assist是一个基于AR眼镜的步骤感知主动助手，通过多模态数据感知用户运动状态，提取程序性上下文，持续推理用户需求并提供及时辅助。在程序动作理解准确率上超越基线21%以上，主动时机准确率最高达基线的2.29倍，用户研究中90%认为有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统主要提供响应式指导（由用户查询触发）或有限的主动辅助（仅针对孤立的短期事件），无法支持长期的多步骤程序性任务。因此需要一种能持续跟踪细粒度任务进度、推理用户演变状态的主动辅助系统。

Method: 利用AR眼镜采集多模态数据实现基于运动的感知；从多尺度时间动态和任务特定专家知识中提取面向步骤的程序上下文；基于感官输入和程序上下文进行持续推理以推断用户需求，并在AR眼镜上显示及时辅助。

Result: 在公开来源数据集和真实AR眼镜测试台数据集上，Pro$^2$Assist的程序动作理解准确率超越最佳基线21%以上，主动时机准确率达基线的2.29倍；20人用户研究中90%认为有用。

Conclusion: Pro$^2$Assist在程序性任务辅助中有效且实用，尤其在长期多步骤任务的主动辅助方面具有显著优势，未来可应用于日常生活的AR辅助场景。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [5] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率非一致性信号（PIS）驱动的神经符号问答框架，将非结构化文本显式提升为事件图与区间约束，从而分离语义提取与符号推理。实验表明，当提供正确结构表示时，时间推理准确率达100%（4000/4000），零假阳/假阴；在噪声注入设定下准确率75.1%并支持确定性故障定位。核心发现：时间推理瓶颈并非推理能力缺陷，而是文本到事件的结构对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"大语言模型在复杂时间推理任务上表现脆弱源于自回归逻辑演绎能力不足"的主流叙事，主张真正的瓶颈在于非结构化文本到事件表示的转换环节。

Method: 提出神经符号问答框架：1）将非结构化文本显式提升为事件图与区间约束，实现语义提取与符号推理的严格解耦；2）引入概率非一致性信号（PIS），融合符号置信区间与基于证据深度学习从LLM隐状态提取的 epistemic 神经不确定性，以鲁棒检测结构断裂。

Result: 在时间算术基准上，提供正确结构表示时系统达到完美1.0准确率（4000/4000），严格零假阳/假阴；在噪声注入的QA设定上保持75.1%准确率，同时实现确定性、步骤级的故障定位。

Conclusion: 通过将表示瓶颈与推理基质分离，将时间问答从算法推理挑战重新定义为结构对齐问题，为可靠神经符号AI提供了可验证路径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction. In this paper, we challenge this prevailing narrative, demonstrating that temporal reasoning is not the fundamental bottleneck; rather, the locus of failure lies in unstructured text-to-event representation. We introduce a novel neuro-symbolic question-answering framework governed by a Probabilistic Inconsistency Signal (PIS) that explicitly isolates perceptual errors from reasoning failures. By lifting unstructured text into explicit event graphs and interval constraints, our architecture strictly decouples semantic extraction from a symbolic reasoning engine. To robustly detect structural breaks, the PIS elegantly unifies symbolic credal intervals with epistemic neural uncertainty extracted via Evidential Deep Learning on LLM hidden states. Empirical evaluations reveal a striking paradigm shift: when provided with correct structural representations, our system's explicit proof traces achieve perfect 1.0 accuracy (4000/4000) and strictly zero false positives/negatives on temporal arithmetic benchmarks. On broader, noise-injected QA settings, the framework maintains a competitive 75.1\% accuracy while enabling deterministic, step-level failure localization. Ultimately, by isolating the representation bottleneck from the reasoning substrate, this work reframes temporal QA from an algorithmic reasoning challenge to a structural alignment problem, charting a verifiable path forward for reliable neuro-symbolic AI.

</details>


### [6] [Parallel Prefix Verification for Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04263)
*Yuncheng Yao,Yuxuan Xia,Shengjie Wang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSE提出了一种并行前缀验证的推测生成框架，通过语义级别的并行验证替代传统的token级别顺序验证，实现大语言模型推理加速，吞吐量提升1.25倍至4.3倍，与EAGLE-3组合可达1.6倍至4.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法受限于token级别的等价验证，导致接受长度短、加速效果有限；而语义/片段级验证虽有潜力但依赖顺序验证，引入显著开销。需要一种能够在不引入顺序验证开销的前提下实现语义级验证的方法。

Method: PARSE引入并行前缀验证机制：从草稿模型获取完整草稿后，目标模型通过自定义注意力掩码在单次前向传播中并行评估多个前缀的正确性，直接识别最大有效前缀，从而消除顺序片段验证并提高计算效率。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，PARSE相比目标模型实现1.25倍至4.3倍吞吐量提升；与EAGLE-3组合时达到1.6倍至4.5倍提升，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 并行前缀验证是一种有效、通用的LLM推理加速方法，与token级别推测解码正交并可组合使用以获得额外增益。

Abstract: We introduce PARSE (PArallel pRefix Speculative Engine), a speculative generation framework that accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by parallelizing prefix verification on a semantic level. Existing speculative decoding methods are fundamentally limited by token-level equivalence: the target model must verify each token, leading to short acceptance lengths and modest speedups. Moving to semantic or segment-level verification can substantially increase acceptance granularity, but prior approaches rely on sequential verification, introducing significant overhead and limiting practical gains. PARSE introduces parallel prefix verification, enabling semantic-level verification without sequential checks. Given a full draft from a draft model, the target model evaluates correctness across multiple prefixes in a single forward pass using a custom attention mask, directly identifying the maximal valid prefix. This eliminates sequential segment verification, and makes verification compute-efficient. PARSE is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding and can be composed with it for additional gains. Across models and benchmarks, PARSE delivers $1.25\times$ to $4.3\times$ throughput gain over the target model, and $1.6\times$ to $4.5\times$ when composed with EAGLE-3, all with negligible accuracy degradation. This demonstrates parallel prefix verification as an effective, general approach to accelerating LLM inference.

</details>


### [7] [The Scaling Properties of Implicit Deductive Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04330)
*Enrico Vompa,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度受限Transformer在Horn子句隐式演绎推理中的缩放特性，通过去相关虚假特征和算法对齐，发现足够深的双向前缀掩码模型在隐式推理上接近显式思维链（CoT）性能，但深度外推仍需CoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度受限Transformer在隐式演绎推理中的缩放规律，并分析隐式推理与显式思维链推理之间的性能差距及其边界条件。

Method: 系统性去相关证明性与虚假特征，强制算法对齐，在多种图拓扑和问题宽度上比较不同深度和掩码配置（特别是双向前缀掩码）下隐式推理与CoT的表现。

Result: 在足够深的双向前缀掩码模型中，隐式推理在多种图拓扑和问题宽度上接近显式CoT性能，但CoT在深度外推任务中仍不可或缺。

Conclusion: 深度和掩码策略对隐式推理能力至关重要，隐式推理在足够深度下可逼近CoT，但外推到更深推理链时仍需CoT的显式中间步骤。

Abstract: We investigate the scaling properties of implicit deductive reasoning over Horn clauses in depth-bounded Transformers. By systematically decorrelating provability from spurious features and enforcing algorithmic alignment, we find that in sufficiently deep models with a bidirectional prefix mask, implicit reasoning approaches explicit CoT performance across graph topologies and problem widths, though CoT remains necessary for depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [8] [When Context Hurts: The Crossover Effect of Knowledge Transfer on Multi-Agent Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04361)
*Saranyan Vigraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体软件设计中，上下文注入并非越多越好：存在交叉效应，同一类型工件在不同任务上可能提升（最高20倍）或降低（最高46%）设计探索效果；一个无上下文的基线试运行即可预测上下文注入的利弊方向（Pearson r=-0.82）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"上下文越多越好"的主流假设，探究多智能体编排中上下文注入对不同任务的差异化影响及其背后的可预测机制。

Method: 在10个任务、7种上下文注入条件和2700余次运行中系统测试；通过操纵提示设计中的收敛压力来探测机制，区分自然收敛（训练数据先验驱动）与诱导收敛（显式指令驱动）。

Result: 发现交叉效应：同一工件类型在某些任务上提升设计探索（最高20倍权衡覆盖），在其他任务上却主动降低探索（最高46%）；无关文档在某些任务上与相关工件表现相当甚至更好；方向可由无上下文时的基线探索度量预测（r=-0.82, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 上下文注入应是有条件的而非通用的；一次无上下文的试运行即可作为廉价诊断手段，预测知识工件对给定任务的帮助或损害方向。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in agent orchestration is that more context is better. We test this on multi-agent software design across 10 tasks, 7 context-injection conditions, and over 2,700 runs, and find a crossover effect: the same artifact type improves design exploration on some tasks (up to 20$\times$ tradeoff coverage) and actively degrades it on others (up to 46% reduction). On several tasks, an irrelevant document performs as well as or better than every relevant artifact. The direction is predicted by a single measurable variable--baseline exploration without context--with Pearson $r = -0.82$ ($p < 0.001$). Probing the mechanism by manipulating convergence pressure through prompt design reveals two distinct regimes: convergence driven by training data priors (natural) responds to artifact disruption, while convergence driven by explicit instructions (induced) does not. The implication is that context injection should be conditional, not universal: one no-context trial is a cheap diagnostic that predicts whether knowledge artifacts will help or hurt a given task.

</details>


### [9] [Deployment-Relevant Alignment Cannot Be Inferred from Model-Level Evaluation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04454)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了对齐评估不能仅依赖模型级评估，而应区分模型级、响应级、交互级和部署级四个证据层级。通过对11个（扩展至16个）对齐基准的结构化审计和跨模型压力测试，发现用户端验证支持在所有基准中缺失，过程可控性几乎缺失，且脚手架有效性依赖于模型。提出系统级评估议程：采用对齐画像替代单一分数、固定脚手架协议和明确推断距离的报告模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估主要停留在模型级评估，但部署相关的对齐不能仅从模型级评估推断。需要明确评估证据的层级，并揭示现有基准在交互级和部署级评估上的不足。

Method: 1) 对11个对齐基准进行结构化审计（扩展至16个），使用八维度评分标准（Cohen's kappa=0.87）进行双编码；2) 跨模型压力测试：使用180个转录本，涉及3个前沿模型和4种脚手架，测试验证脚手架的效果。

Result: 用户端验证支持在所有被检基准中完全缺失；过程可控性几乎完全缺失；少数交互基准（tau-bench、CURATe、Rifts、Common Ground）覆盖分散；脚手架有效性具有模型依赖性——同一验证脚手架可使一个模型的验证支持达到上限，但另一个模型则无明显变化。

Conclusion: 部署相关的对齐评估需要系统级评估方法：提出对齐画像替代单一分数、固定脚手架协议用于可比较的交互评估、以及明确评估证据与部署主张之间推断距离的报告模板。

Abstract: Alignment evaluation in machine learning has largely become evaluation of models. Influential benchmarks score model outputs under fixed inputs, such as truthfulness, instruction following, or pairwise preference, and these scores are often used to support claims about deployed alignment. This paper argues that deployment-relevant alignment cannot be inferred from model-level evaluation alone. Alignment claims should instead be indexed to the level at which evidence is collected: model-level, response-level, interaction-level, or deployment-level. Two studies support this position. First, a structured audit of eleven alignment benchmarks, extended to a sixteen-benchmark corpus, dual-coded against an eight-dimension rubric with Cohen's kappa = 0.87, finds that user-facing verification support is absent across every benchmark examined, while process steerability is nearly absent. The few interactional benchmarks identified, including tau-bench, CURATe, Rifts, and Common Ground, remain fragmented in coverage, and benchmark construction rather than data source determines what is measured. Second, a blinded cross-model stress test using 180 transcripts across three frontier models and four scaffolds finds that the same verification scaffold raises one model's verification support to ceiling while leaving another categorically unchanged. This shows that scaffold efficacy is model-dependent and that the gap identified by the audit cannot be closed at the model level alone. We propose a system-level evaluation agenda: alignment profiles instead of single scores, fixed-scaffolding protocols for comparable interactional evaluation, and reporting templates that make the inferential distance between evaluation evidence and deployment claims explicit.

</details>


### [10] [How Does Thinking Mode Change LLM Moral Judgments? A Controlled Instant-vs-Thinking Comparison Across Five Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04488)
*Sai Sourabh Madur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了推理模式对五个前沿LLM道德判断的影响，发现总体一致性高，但在模型有争议的场景中推理能缩小分歧，并减少部分模型的人口统计判断不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究启用推理模式是否会改变同一模型检查点的道德判断，以理解推理能力对LLM道德判断的影响。

Method: 基于100个道德判断场景，测试五个前沿推理型LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT 5.5, Gemini 3 Flash, DeepSeek V3.1, Qwen3.5 397B），比较即时模式与思考模式下的二元裁决一致性，分析模型间分歧、人口统计判断一致性和自我标签的伦理框架变化。

Result: 总体一致性高（Krippendorff's alpha 0.78 vs 0.79），但在21个有争议场景中即时模式一致性接近随机（alpha=0.08）；推理模式将平均成对一致性从5.4提升至6.7；三个模型的人口统计判断不一致性降低，未发现增加；推理模式更频繁地改变自我标签伦理框架而非二元裁决。

Conclusion: 推理模式在整体上不显著改变道德判断，但能在模型存在分歧的场景中缩小分歧，并改善部分模型的人口统计判断一致性，同时推理对模型的伦理框架标签影响大于对具体裁决的影响。

Abstract: We evaluate whether enabling provider-exposed reasoning mode changes moral judgments within the same model checkpoint. Across 100 moral-judgment scenarios and five frontier reasoning-trained LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT 5.5, Gemini 3 Flash, DeepSeek V3.1, and Qwen3.5 397B), aggregate binary-verdict agreement remains high and statistically indistinguishable between instant and thinking modes (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79). However, disagreement is concentrated in 21 model-disputed scenarios, where instant-mode agreement is near chance (alpha = 0.08). On these scenarios, reasoning directionally narrows cross-model disagreement, increasing mean pairwise agreement from 5.4 to 6.7 out of 10. Reasoning also reduces demographic-judgment inconsistency in three of five models and does not increase it for any model. Across all five model families, reasoning changes self-labeled ethical frameworks more often than binary verdicts.

</details>


### [11] [From Parameter Dynamics to Risk Scoring : Quantifying Sample-Level Safety Degradation in LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04572)
*Xiao Wang,Yifei Zhang,YongKang Liu,Xiaocui Yang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM安全对齐的脆弱性源于良性微调导致参数沿危险方向累积漂移，并提出SQSD方法通过计算参数更新的投影差异来量化每个样本对安全退化的影响，实验证明该方法能有效评估样本级微调风险并具有良好的跨模型迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过比较微调前后的参数和隐藏状态来解释安全对齐脆弱性，但忽略了微调过程中的动态演化。本文旨在揭示安全退化的关键机制并实现样本级风险量化。

Method: 提出样本级安全退化量化方法SQSD，通过计算每个训练样本在危险方向和安全方向上的参数更新投影差异，生成连续的风险分数来量化样本对安全退化的影响。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，SQSD能够有效量化样本级微调风险，并在不同模型架构、参数量和参数高效微调方法上表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: 良性微调导致参数沿危险方向累积漂移是安全退化的关键机制，SQSD方法能够有效识别高风险样本，为LLM安全微调提供预警和防护手段。

Abstract: Safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is extremely fragile, as fine-tuning on a small number of benign samples can erase safety behaviors learned from millions of preference examples. Existing studies attempt to explain this phenomenon by comparing parameters and hidden states before and after fine-tuning, but overlook their dynamic evolution during fine-tuning. In this paper, we uncover a critical mechanism underlying safety degradation by analyzing parameter dynamics, where benign fine-tuning causes parameters to cumulatively drift toward danger-aligned directions, progressively undermining the model's safety. This finding suggests that samples contributing more to this drift has greater fine-tuning risks. Based on this insight, we propose a method of Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation (SQSD), which quantifies the influence of each training sample on safety degradation. Specifically, SQSD computes continuous risk scores to samples by measuring their induced parameter updates' projection difference between danger and safety directions. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that SQSD effectively quantifies sample-level fine-tuning risks and exhibits strong transferability across model architectures, parameter scales, and parameter-efficient methods.

</details>


### [12] [SensingAgents: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Robust IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04608)
*Naiyu Zheng,Tianlong Yu,Haochen Yin,Xiaoyi Fan,Xiping Hu,Zhimeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SensingAgents，一种基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，通过分工协作（分析师、辩护者、决策者）实现鲁棒的IMU人体活动识别，在零样本设置下准确率达79.5%，远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习HAR模型严重依赖标注数据、存在位置特异性歧义、缺乏透明推理能力，亟需一种无需标注、可解释且鲁棒的识别方法。

Method: 设计多智能体系统SensingAgents：1) 分析师智能体组负责不同传感器位置（手臂、手腕、腰带、口袋）的特定分析；2) 辩护律师智能体对通过动态与静态辩证辩论解决传感器冲突；3) 决策智能体在传感器漂移或失效时确保可靠性。

Result: 在Shoaib数据集上零样本设置下准确率79.5%，比现有单/多智能体LLM模型高29%，比深度学习基线高9.4%，尤其在多传感器数据冲突或噪声场景下表现显著。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作推理能有效提升普适传感系统的鲁棒性和可解释性，为无标注HAR任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors is a cornerstone of mobile health, smart environments, and human-computer interaction. However, current deep learning-based HAR models often struggle with heavy reliance on labeled data, position-specific ambiguity, and a lack of transparent reasoning. Inspired by the advanced agents framework, which emulates a collaborative agent using Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose SensingAgents, a novel multi-agent system for robust IMU activity recognition. SensingAgents organizes LLM-powered agents into specialized roles: a group of Analyst Agents for position-specific sensor analysis (arm, wrist, belt, pocket), a pair of Advocate Agents that resolves sensor conflicts through dynamic and static dialectical debates, and a Decision Agent that ensures reliability under sensor drift or failure. Evaluation on the Shoaib dataset demonstrates that SensingAgents significantly outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and multi-agent LLM models, achieving an accuracy of 79.5% in a zero setting--29% higher than existing agent models and 9.4% higher than deep learning baselines--particularly in complex scenarios where multi-sensor data is conflicting or noisy. Our work highlights the potential of multi-agent collaborative reasoning for advancing the robustness and interpretability of ubiquitous sensing systems.

</details>


### [13] [Reward-Decomposed Reinforcement Learning for Immersive Video Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04733)
*Miao Wang,Yuling Shi,Yijiang Li,Yeheng Chen,Xiaodong Gu,Bin Li,Bo Gao,Yaduan Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EBM-RL框架，通过解耦的GRPO强化学习将角色扮演对话分解为“感知→思考→回应”三阶段，并设计四种互补奖励，在视频驱动的角色扮演任务中显著超越文本基线和大规模视觉语言模型，同时展现强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本角色扮演模型能模仿角色风格，但无法捕捉场景氛围和张力变化，这限制了在VR游戏、交互叙事等沉浸式应用中的效果。作者希望赋予模型视觉感知驱动的类人对话能力。

Method: 提出EBM-RL框架，基于解耦的GRPO，将推理过程显式分为[perception]、[think]、[answer]三个阶段，并设计四种互补奖励：CLIP场景-文本对齐奖励、感知-认知奖励（促进感知和思考过程增加参考回复的似然）、答案准确性奖励、密集格式奖励。

Result: 在沉浸式角色扮演基准上，EBM-RL显著优于纯文本基线和更大规模的视觉语言模型；在视觉氛围一致性和角色真实性上均有提升；在域外VideoQA基准上无需微调即展现强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将感知与认知过程显式解耦的强化学习框架能有效提升视频驱动角色扮演的沉浸感和场景一致性，且具有良好的跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Text-based role-playing models can imitate character styles, yet they often fail to reflect a scene's atmosphere and evolving tension, both essential for immersive applications such as Virtual Reality (VR) games and interactive narratives. We study video-grounded role-playing dialogue and introduce EBM-RL (Eye-Brain-Mouth Reinforcement Learning), a decoupled GRPO-based framework that explicitly separates observation ([perception]), reasoning ([think]), and utterance ([answer]). This structure promotes human-like sensory grounding by compelling the model to first attend to visual cues, then form internal interpretations, and finally generate context-appropriate dialogue.
  EBM-RL integrates four complementary rewards: (i) CLIP-based scene-text alignment to improve ambiance and emotion; (ii) a Perceptual-Cognitive reward that encourages [perception] and [think] processes that increase the likelihood of the reference response; (iii) answer accuracy to ensure faithfulness; and (iv) a dense format reward to enforce the desired structured output.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that EBM-RL substantially outperforms text-only role-playing baselines and larger-scale vision-language models on our immersive role-playing benchmark, delivering simultaneous gains in visual-atmosphere consistency and character authenticity. Beyond the role-playing domain, EBM-RL also exhibits strong zero-shot generalization: without any additional fine-tuning, it consistently improves performance on out-of-domain VideoQA benchmarks. We additionally release an open-source dataset for video-grounded role-playing dialogue.

</details>


### [14] [AgentTrust: Runtime Safety Evaluation and Interception for AI Agent Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04785)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentTrust 是一个运行时安全层，能在工具调用执行前进行拦截并返回结构化裁决（允许、警告、阻止、审查），结合了 shell 反混淆、SafeFix 安全替代建议、RiskChain 多步攻击链检测以及 LLM-as-Judge 处理模糊输入。在内部基准上达到 95.0% 裁决准确率，在 630 个对抗场景上达到 96.7% 裁决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI Agent 通过工具调用（文件操作、Shell 命令、HTTP 请求、数据库查询等）执行真实世界的操作，单个不安全操作（如意外删除、凭证泄露、数据窃取）可能造成不可逆损害。现有防御方案不完善：事后基准测试只能衡量执行后的行为，静态防御手段无法应对混淆和多步上下文，基础设施沙箱约束了代码运行位置但不理解操作含义。

Method: AgentTrust 是一个运行时安全层，在 Agent 工具调用执行前进行拦截，返回结构化裁决（allow/warn/block/review）。核心组件包括：Shell 反混淆归一化器、SafeFix 安全替代建议、RiskChain 多步攻击链检测、以及用于处理模糊输入的缓存感知 LLM-as-Judge。

Result: 内部基准上，生产规则集达到 95.0% 裁决准确率和 73.7% 风险等级准确率，端到端延迟为毫秒级。在 630 个对抗场景基准上，AgentTrust 达到 96.7% 裁决准确率，其中 shell 混淆负载上约 93%。

Conclusion: AgentTrust 提供了有效的运行时安全防护，能够在实际执行前检测和分类不安全操作，显著优于现有方案。系统以 AGPL-3.0 许可开源，并提供 MCP 服务端以兼容 MCP 协议的 Agent。

Abstract: Modern AI agents execute real-world side effects through tool calls such as file operations, shell commands, HTTP requests, and database queries. A single unsafe action, including accidental deletion, credential exposure, or data exfiltration, can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses are incomplete: post-hoc benchmarks measure behavior after execution, static guardrails miss obfuscation and multi-step context, and infrastructure sandboxes constrain where code runs without understanding what an action means.
  We present AgentTrust, a runtime safety layer that intercepts agent tool calls before execution and returns a structured verdict: allow, warn, block, or review. AgentTrust combines a shell deobfuscation normalizer, SafeFix suggestions for safer alternatives, RiskChain detection for multi-step attack chains, and a cache-aware LLM-as-Judge for ambiguous inputs.
  We release a 300-scenario benchmark across six risk categories and an additional 630 independently constructed real-world adversarial scenarios. On the internal benchmark, the production-only ruleset achieves 95.0% verdict accuracy and 73.7% risk-level accuracy at low-millisecond end-to-end latency. On the 630-scenario benchmark, evaluated under a patched ruleset and not claimed as zero-shot, AgentTrust achieves 96.7% verdict accuracy, including about 93% on shell-obfuscated payloads. AgentTrust is released under the AGPL-3.0 license and provides a Model Context Protocol server for MCP-compatible agents.

</details>


### [15] [DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap): A Controllable and Interactive Red-Teaming Platform for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04808)
*Zhaorun Chen,Xun Liu,Haibo Tong,Chengquan Guo,Yuzhou Nie,Jiawei Zhang,Mintong Kang,Chejian Xu,Qichang Liu,Xiaogeng Liu,Tianneng Shi,Chaowei Xiao,Sanmi Koyejo,Percy Liang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap)，首个可控且可交互的AI Agent红队测试平台，覆盖14个真实领域和50多个模拟环境；并提出了DTap-Red自主红队测试Agent，以及DTap-Bench大规模红队测试数据集。通过大规模评估，揭示了主流AI Agent的系统性安全漏洞模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent在动态、不可信环境中运行，面临严重的安全与隐私风险，但缺乏可控、可复现的大规模风险评估环境。

Method: 构建DTap平台，包含14个领域50+模拟环境；提出DTap-Red自主红队测试Agent，探索多种攻击向量（提示、工具、技能、环境及其组合）；构建DTap-Bench数据集并配备可验证的评估器。

Result: 对基于多种主流大模型的AI Agent进行大规模评估，揭示了系统性的安全漏洞模式。

Conclusion: DTap及DTap-Red为AI Agent的安全性评估提供了可控、可扩展的框架，发现的安全漏洞模式为开发更安全的下一代Agent提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed across diverse domains to automate complex workflows through long-horizon and high-stakes action executions. Due to their high capability and flexibility, such agents raise significant security and safety concerns. A growing number of real-world incidents have shown that adversaries can easily manipulate agents into performing harmful actions, such as leaking API keys, deleting user data, or initiating unauthorized transactions. Evaluating agent security is inherently challenging, as agents operate in dynamic, untrusted environments involving external tools, heterogeneous data sources, and frequent user interactions. However, realistic, controllable, and reproducible environments for large-scale risk assessment remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap), the first controllable and interactive red-teaming platform for AI agents, spanning 14 real-world domains and over 50 simulation environments that replicate widely used systems such as Google Workspace, Paypal, and Slack. To scale the risk assessment of agents in DTap, we further propose DTap-Red, the first autonomous red-teaming agent that systematically explores diverse injection vectors (e.g., prompt, tool, skill, environment, combinations) and autonomously discovers effective attack strategies tailored to varying malicious goals. Using DTap-Red, we curate DTap-Bench, a large-scale red-teaming dataset comprising high-quality instances across domains, each paired with a verifiable judge to automatically validate attack outcomes. Through DTap, we conduct large-scale evaluations of popular AI agents built on various backbone models, spanning security policies, risk categories, and attack strategies, revealing systematic vulnerability patterns and providing valuable insights for developing secure next-generation agents.

</details>


### [16] [Strat-Reasoner: Reinforcing Strategic Reasoning of LLMs in Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04906)
*Yidong He,Yutao Lai,Pengxu Yang,Jiarui Gan,Jiexin Wang,Yi Cai,Mengchen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Strat-Reasoner框架，通过递归推理、集中式CoT比较模块和分组相对RL优化，显著提升LLM在多智能体博弈中的策略推理能力，平均提升22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多智能体博弈中表现不佳，因为其他智能体的非平稳性导致推理评估和信用分配困难，现有单智能体RL及多智能体扩展方法未将其他智能体纳入推理过程。

Method: 1) 递归推理范式：将其他智能体的推理过程纳入推理；2) 集中式思维链(CoT)比较模块：评估中间推理序列质量；3) 混合优势计算与分组相对RL优化策略。

Result: 在多种多智能体博弈中，平均性能提升22.1%。

Conclusion: Strat-Reasoner有效提升了LLM在多智能体博弈中的策略推理能力，验证了递归推理和集中式CoT评估加分组RL优化的有效性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in certain reasoning tasks, they struggle in multi-agent games where the final outcome depends on the joint strategies of all agents. In multi-agent games, the non-stationarity of other agents brings significant challenges on the evaluation of the reasoning process and the credit assignment over multiple reasoning steps. Existing single-agent reinforcement learning (RL) approaches and their multi-agent extensions fail to address these challenges as they do not incorporate other agents in the reasoning process. In this work, we propose Strat-Reasoner, a novel RL-based framework that improves LLMs' strategic reasoning ability in multi-agent games. We introduce a novel recursive reasoning paradigm where an agent's reasoning also integrates other agents' reasoning processes. To provide effective reward signals for the intermediate reasoning sequences, we employ a centralized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) comparison module to evaluate the reasoning quality. Finally, we compute an accurate hybrid advantage and develop a group-relative RL approach to optimize the LLM policy. Experimental results show that Strat-Reasoner substantially improves strategic abilities of underlying LLMs, achieving 22.1\% average performance improvements across various multi-agent games.

</details>


### [17] [A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Logical Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04916)
*Yin Jun Phua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NRI是一个预训练模型，通过将文字表示为领域无关的统计属性（如类别条件概率、熵、共现等），实现零样本规则归纳，无需针对新任务重新训练。模型采用统计编码器+并行插槽解码器，利用Product T-norm放松规则执行使其可微，在规则恢复、噪声鲁棒性、虚假相关性和零样本迁移上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳逻辑编程方法是转导式的，其学习参数绑定到特定谓词，每个新任务都需要重新训练，缺乏泛化能力和零样本迁移能力。

Method: NRI包含统计编码器（将文字编码为类别条件概率、熵、共现等统计属性）和并行插槽解码器（保持逻辑析取的置换不变性）。采用Product T-norm放松使规则执行可微，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在规则恢复、标签噪声鲁棒性、虚假相关性鲁棒性以及零样本迁移到真实世界基准任务上均取得优异表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为符号推理的基础模型开辟了可能性。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) learns interpretable logical rules from data. Existing methods are transductive: their learned parameters are bound to specific predicates and require retraining for each new task. We introduce Neural Rule Inducer (NRI), a pretrained model for zero-shot rule induction. Rather than encoding literal identities, NRI represents literals using domain-agnostic statistical properties such as class-conditional rates, entropy, and co-occurrence, which generalize across variable identities and counts without retraining. The model consists of a statistical encoder and a parallel slot-based decoder. Parallel decoding preserves the permutation invariance of logical disjunction; an autoregressive decoder would instead impose an arbitrary clause order. Product T-norm relaxation makes rule execution differentiable, allowing end-to-end training on prediction accuracy alone. We evaluate NRI on rule recovery, robustness to label noise and spurious correlations, and zero-shot transfer to real-world benchmarks, and we believe this work opens up the possibility of foundation models for symbolic reasoning. Code and the reference checkpoint are available at https://github.com/phuayj/neural-rule-inducer.

</details>

*Anvay Shah,Ramsundar Anandanarayanan,Sharayu Moharir,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将树型马尔可夫决策过程（T-MDP）中的在线学习问题转化为多臂老虎机问题的方法，通过为每个策略分配一个臂，并利用策略间共享数据的置信界设计，实现了多项式级别的内存和每步计算复杂度，在样本复杂度和遗憾值上获得了与终端状态数量（而非策略数量）相关的实例依赖上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-MDP（树型马尔可夫决策过程）在完美回忆的序贯博弈中自然出现，但其在线学习面临策略数量随状态数指数增长的技术挑战，需要设计高效的算法来克服这一困难。

Method: 将每个策略视为一个臂，应用经典bandit算法（LUCB和UCB），核心创新在于设计基于策略间共享数据的置信界，使得算法能够以多项式级别的内存和每步计算量实现。

Result: 获得了实例依赖的样本复杂度上界和遗憾上界，这些上界与终端状态数量相关而非策略数量；在隐藏信息游戏上的实验表明算法持续优于现有替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的置信界设计，成功地将指数级策略空间的T-MDP在线学习问题转化为可在多项式时间内实现的bandit学习问题，理论分析与实验验证均支持方法的有效性。

Abstract: A Tree Markov Decision Problem (T-MDP) is a finite-horizon MDP with a starting state $s_{1}$, in which every state is reachable from $s_{1}$ through exactly one state-action trajectory. T-MDPs arise naturally as abstractions of decision making in sequential games with perfect recall, against stationary opponents. We consider the problem of on-line learning in T-MDPs, both in the PAC and the regret-minimisation regimes. We show that well-known bandit algorithms -- \textsc{Lucb} and \textsc{Ucb} -- can be applied on T-MDPs by treating each policy as an arm. The apparent technical challenge in this approach is that the number of policies is exponential in the number of states. Our main innovation is in the design of confidence bounds based on data shared by the policies, so that the bandit algorithms can yet be implemented with polynomial memory and per-step computation. We obtain instance-dependent upper bounds on sample complexity and regret that sum a ``gap term'' from every terminal state, rather than every policy. Empirically, our algorithms consistently outperform available alternatives on a suite of hidden-information games.

</details>

*Zhiqing Cui,Haotong Xie,Jiahao Yuan,Cheng Yang,Hanqing Wang,Yuxin Wu,Yifan Wu,Siru Zhong,Tao Yu,Yifu Guo,Siyu Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Qibing Ren,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Uno-Orchestra，一种统一的编排策略，通过强化学习联合学习任务分解与子任务分配，在13个基准测试中相比最强基线提升约16%准确率同时降低约一个数量级的查询成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统依赖刚性编排（固定路由或手工任务分解），导致分解深度、工作模型选择和推理预算无法在统一目标下联合优化。

Method: Uno-Orchestra是一种统一编排策略，选择性分解任务并将每个子任务分配给可接受的（模型，原语）对，通过从基于真实worker交互的精心策划的RL轨迹中联合学习分解决策和分配决策。

Result: 在包含数学、代码、知识、长上下文和智能体工具使用的13个基准测试、22个基线中，Uno-Orchestra达到77.0%的macro pass@1，比最强工作流基线高出约16%，同时每查询成本降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: Uno-Orchestra推动了选择性委派的准确率-效率前沿，证明了分解深度、worker选择和推理预算可以在统一框架下联合优化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems typically rely on rigid orchestration, committing either to flat per-query routing or to hand-engineered task decomposition, so decomposition depth, worker choice, and inference budget are not jointly optimized under one objective. We introduce Uno-Orchestra, a unified orchestration policy that selectively decomposes a task and dispatches each subtask to an admissible (model, primitive) pair, with both decisions learned together from curated RL trajectories grounded in real worker interactions. Against 22 baselines on a 13-benchmark suite spanning math, code, knowledge, long-context, and agentic tool-use, Uno-Orchestra reaches 77.0% macro pass@1, roughly 16% above the strongest workflow baseline, at roughly an order of magnitude lower per-query cost, advancing the accuracy-efficiency frontier of selective delegation.

</details>


### [20] [Position: Embodied AI Requires a Privacy-Utility Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05017)
*Xiaoliang Fan,Jiarui Chen,Zhuodong Liu,Ziqi Yang,Peixuan Xu,Ruimin Shen,Junhui Liu,Jianzhong Qi,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前具身智能（EAI）系统在模拟向现实部署过程中，各阶段（指令、感知、规划、交互）独立优化导致系统性隐私危机。提出SPINE框架，将隐私作为生命周期级的动态控制信号，通过多准则隐私分类矩阵实现跨阶段耦合约束，以替代碎片化的局部隐私补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EAI解决方案在指令、感知、规划、交互等孤立阶段各自优化，未考虑高频部署中隐私泄露不可逆的耦合隐私问题。独立优化各组件在敏感环境中部署时产生了系统性隐私危机。

Method: 提出SPINE（Secure Privacy Integration in Next-generation Embodied AI）统一隐私感知框架：将EAI流水线分解为多个阶段，建立多准则隐私分类矩阵，在各阶段边界间编排上下文敏感性，将隐私作为动态控制信号治理整个EAI生命周期中的跨阶段耦合。

Result: 通过初步仿真和真实场景案例研究，概念性验证了隐私约束如何向下游传播以重塑系统行为，证明了碎片化隐私补丁的不足。

Conclusion: EAI中的隐私应是生命周期级的架构约束，而非局部阶段特征。SPINE框架为未来构建安全且功能完整的具身智能系统指明了研究方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) systems are rapidly transitioning from simulations into real-world domestic and other sensitive environments. However, recent EAI solutions have largely demonstrated advancements within isolated stages such as instruction, perception, planning and interaction, without considering their coupled privacy implications in high-frequency deployments where privacy leakage is often irreversible. This position paper argues that optimizing these components independently creates a systemic privacy crisis when deployed in sensitive settings, thereby advancing the position that privacy in EAI is a life cycle-level architectural constraint rather than a stage-local feature. To address these challenges, we propose Secure Privacy Integration in Next-generation Embodied AI (SPINE), a unified privacy-aware framework that treats privacy as a dynamic control signal governing cross-stage coupling throughout the entire EAI life cycle. SPINE decomposes the EAI pipeline into various stages and establishes a multi-criterion privacy classification matrix to orchestrate contextual sensitivity across stage boundaries. We conduct preliminary simulation and real-world case studies to conceptually validate how privacy constraints propagate downstream to reshape system behavior, illustrating the insufficiency of fragmented privacy patches and motivating future research directions into secure yet functional embodied AI systems. We detail the SPINE framework and case studies at https://github.com/rminshen03/EAI_Privacy_Position.

</details>

### [21] [Are Multimodal LLMs Ready for Clinical Dermatology? A Real-World Evaluation in Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04098)
*Roy Jiang,Hyunjae Kim,Zhenyue Qin,Morten Lee,Margaret MacGibeny,Ailish Hanly,Angela Sadlowski,Shanin Chowdhury,Xuguang Ai,Jeffrey Gehlhausen,Qingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在皮肤病学领域的表现，发现其在公开基准测试中表现尚可，但在包含5,811例病例和46,405张临床图像的真实医院会诊队列中诊断准确率大幅下降（最佳开源模型Top-3准确率从26.55%降至13.35%，GPT-4.1从42.25%降至24.65%）。结合临床背景可提升性能，但模型对不完整或错误背景高度敏感。基于严重程度的分诊虽有一定敏感性（>60%），但尚不足以支持临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在公开皮肤病学基准上表现良好，但基准性能可能无法推广到真实临床决策场景。为量化这种"从基准到临床"的差距，作者开展了大规模真实世界评估。

Method: 评估了四种开源MLLM（InternVL-Chat v1.5、LLaVA-Med v1.5、SkinGPT4、MedGemma-4B-Instruct）和一个商业MLLM（GPT-4.1），在三个公开数据集和一个包含5,811例病例和46,405张临床图像的多中心回顾性医院皮肤病学会诊队列上进行测试。设计了两个临床相关任务：鉴别诊断生成和基于严重程度的分诊。

Result: 公开基准上最佳开源模型Top-3准确率为26.55%，GPT-4.1为42.25%。真实世界仅用图像时，开源模型降至1.50%-13.35%，GPT-4.1降至24.65%。加入临床背景后，开源模型提升至28.75%，GPT-4.1提升至38.93%。模型对不完整或错误背景高度敏感。分诊任务敏感性>60%但可靠性不足。

Conclusion: 当前皮肤病学MLLMs的基准测试性能严重高估了其真实临床能力，模型尚不具备可靠的临床部署条件。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promise on publicly available dermatology benchmarks. However, benchmark performance may not generalize to real-world dermatologic decision-making. To quantify this benchmark-to-bedside gap, we evaluated four open-weight MLLMs (InternVL-Chat v1.5, LLaVA-Med v1.5, SkinGPT4 and MedGemma-4B-Instruct) and one commercial MLLM (GPT-4.1) across three publicly available dermatology datasets and a retrospective multi-site hospital-based dermatology consultation cohort comprising 5,811 cases and 46,405 clinical images. Models were evaluated on two clinically relevant tasks: differential diagnosis generation and severity-based triage. Diagnostic performance was modest on public datasets and declined substantially in the real-world cohort. On public benchmarks, top-3 diagnostic accuracy reached 26.55% for the best open-weight model and 42.25% for GPT-4.1. On real-world consultation cases using images alone, top-3 diagnostic accuracy fell to 1.50%-13.35% among open-weight models and 24.65% for GPT-4.1. Incorporating clinical context improved performance across all models, increasing top-3 diagnostic accuracy up to 28.75% among open-weight models and 38.93% for GPT-4.1. However, model outputs were highly sensitive to incomplete or erroneous consultation context. For severity-based triage, models achieved moderate sensitivity (above 60%), suggesting potential utility for screening but insufficient reliability for clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that benchmark performance substantially overestimates the real-world clinical capability of current dermatology MLLMs.

</details>


### [22] [MuCALD-SplitFed: Causal-Latent Diffusion for Privacy-Preserving Multi-Task Split-Federated Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04108)
*Chamani Shiranthika,Hadi Hadizadeh,Parvaneh Saeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合因果表示学习和潜在扩散的多任务SplitFed框架MuCALD-SplitFed，在医学图像分割任务中优于基线方法，同时降低了信息泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习和分割联邦学习假设客户端共享单一任务，但实际临床场景中不同医疗机构往往处理不同任务；多任务学习虽能扩展但存在不稳定性和隐私漏洞。

Method: 在SplitFed框架中引入因果表示学习（Causal Representation Learning）和潜在扩散（Latent Diffusion），构建多任务学习机制。

Result: MuCALD-SplitFed在分割任务上持续提升性能，而基线SplitFed无法收敛；有效减少了分割点的信息泄漏，抵御重构攻击和成员推断攻击；优于当前最先进的个性化联邦学习和多任务联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: MuCALD-SplitFed通过因果表示学习和潜在扩散有效解决了多任务场景下SplitFed的不稳定性和隐私问题，在医学图像分割中表现出色。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables decentralized training by aggregating model updates across clients without sharing raw data, while Split Federated Learning further partitions the model between clients and a server to reduce computation and communication at the client side. However, decentralized medical institutions rarely operate on a single shared task, making standard Federated and SplitFed collaborations poorly aligned with real clinical workflows. Multi-task FL extends these frameworks by allowing clients to handle different tasks, but often introduces instability and privacy vulnerabilities. This study proposes \textbf{MuCALD-SplitFed}, a multi-task SplitFed framework that integrates causal representation learning and latent diffusion. Experiments show MuCALD-SplitFed consistently improves segmentation, while baseline SplitFed fails to converge. The proposed approach further reduces information leakage at split points, mitigating reconstruction-based and membership inference attacks. Additionally, MuCALD SplitFed outperforms state-of-the-art personalized FL and multi-task FL approaches. The code repository is: https://github.com/ChamaniS/MuCALD_SplitFed.

</details>


### [23] [Anatomy of a failure: When, how, and why deep vision fails in scientific domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04231)
*Ji-Hun Oh,Dou Hoon Kwark,Kianoush Falahkheirkhah,Kevin Yeh,John Cheville,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Rohit Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度学习在科学成像（如红外成像）中直接应用时，由于数据先验与DL的简化偏差不匹配，会导致模型崩溃为一维预测，即便使用现有鲁棒性策略也无法解决，警示了AI安全性问题并倡导开发模态专属算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在科学成像领域应用日益广泛，但科学图像（如红外成像）具有多通道、定量化的物理化学特性，与日常RGB图像存在本质差异。作者质疑直接套用RGB领域成熟的DL框架是否有效，并试图揭示潜在失败模式。

Method: 以病理学为案例，对比染色组织的RGB图像与红外（IR）成像的生化特征；训练DL模型比较两者性能；分析IR数据先验与DL简化偏差的交互作用；测试现有SOTA鲁棒化策略是否有效。

Result: IR数据虽信息量更大，但DL模型在IR数据上表现反而更差；模型陷入一维预测崩溃，表征能力未被充分利用；现有RGB导向的鲁棒化策略无法解决该问题。

Conclusion: 科学成像中直接应用通用DL框架存在严重安全隐患，需要研究模态特定的失败模式以开发专用、安全的AI算法。

Abstract: Mirroring its ubiquity in popular media and all human activities, the use of deep learning (DL) is rapidly growing in scientific imaging modalities. However, unlike everyday RGB pictures, pixels encode precise physicochemical properties in scientific imaging across potentially thousands of channels. While DL is well validated on human-centric RGB perceptual tasks, its effectiveness for scientific imaging remains uncertain. Here, we show that the naive application of DL frameworks to scientific images can lead to critical failures. We evaluate the use of DL for pathology, comparing RGB images of stained tissue with the quantitative and information-rich biochemical signatures of infrared (IR) imaging. Despite this informational advantage, DL models trained on IR data paradoxically underperform. We investigate this discrepancy to find that IR data priors interact poorly with the simplicity bias of DL, causing models to collapse to one-dimensional predictions. This constitutes a catastrophic DL failure because the model's representational capacity remains largely unused, while furthermore raising AI safety concerns and undermining the advantages of such scientific modalities. Notably, this problem persists even with state-of-the-art DL robustification strategies, which are primarily designed and validated for RGB imagery and thus inherit the same prior-bias mismatch. This work establishes a framework for understanding the limitations of generic DL in science and advocates for the study of modality-specific failure modes to guide the development of specialized, safe AI algorithms.

</details>


### [24] [Densification and forecasting of Sentinel-2 time series from multimodal SAR and Optical satellite data using deep generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04239)
*Véronique Defonte,Dawa Derksen,Alexandre Constantin,Bastien Nespoulous*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率深度学习框架，利用Sentinel-2光学和Sentinel-1 SAR多模态卫星数据，实现光学卫星图像时间序列的任意时间点（包括过去和未来）的加密与预测，并重点关注生成图像的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星图像时间序列重建方法仅能填补观测时段内的缺失或云污染数据，无法预测未来观测，限制了连续监测能力。

Method: 提出概率深度学习框架，联合利用Sentinel-2光学图像和Sentinel-1 SAR观测的多模态数据，生成任意过去或未来日期的光学图像，并关注生成图像的不确定性。

Result: 在稀疏且时间不对齐的时间序列上，实现了有效的图像加密与预测。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅实现了时间序列的加密，还扩展到了未来预测能力，且量化了生成图像的不确定性，优于仅能填补观测时段内缺失的现有方法。

Abstract: Optical satellite image time series are extensively used in many Earth observation applications, including agriculture, climate monitoring, and land surface analysis. However, clouds and swath edges result in irregular sampling along the temporal dimension, limiting continuous monitoring. To address this issue, a growing body of work has focused on temporal densification and reconstruction of satellite image time series, with the objective of filling missing or cloud-contaminated observations within the temporal extent of the available data. While these approaches improve temporal continuity, they are inherently restricted to the reconstruction of the gaps within the observed time periods, and do not address the prediction of future observations. This work proposes a probabilistic deep learning framework for the densification and forecasting of Sentinel-2 time series by generating optical images at arbitrary past or future dates. The approach leverages multimodal satellite data by jointly exploiting Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 SAR observations. Unlike most existing works, we propose to focus on the uncertainty of the generated images. Experimental results demonstrate effective densification and forecasting, on sparse and temporally misaligned time series.

</details>


### [25] [Physics-Guided Regime Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04247)
*Paula Pacheco,Pablo Granitto,Juan B. Cabral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的混合模型切换方法PGRU，通过像素级参数ξ自适应激活非线性混合，在三个数据集上优于基线方法，物理一致性ρ>0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMM在多次散射场景下失效，现有非线性模型对整幅场景统一施加固定混合模式，缺乏对局部物理特性的自适应能力。

Method: 从可观测物理特征估计像素级标量ξ∈[0,1]；通过学习注意力机制融合GBM、PPNM、Hapke三个模型的残差，生成可解释的混合模式图。

Result: 在Samson、Jasper Ridge和Urban数据集上持续优于基线方法，物理一致性指标ρ>0.90。

Conclusion: PGRU通过像素级物理引导的混合模式切换，在保持物理可解释性的同时提升了光谱解混性能。

Abstract: The Linear Mixing Model (LMM) dominates spectral unmixing for its simplicity, but fails under multiple scattering; existing nonlinear models compensate by applying a fixed regime uniformly across entire scenes. We propose Physics-Guided Regime Unmixing (PGRU), which estimates a pixel-wise scalar $ξ_i \in [0,1]$ from observable physical features to activate nonlinear mixing only where justified. Residuals from the Generalized Bilinear Model (GBM), the Post-Nonlinear Mixing Model (PPNM), and Hapke are combined via learned attention, yielding interpretable regime maps. Experiments on Samson, Jasper Ridge, and Urban show consistent improvements over baselines, with physical coherence $ρ> 0.90$.

</details>


### [26] [Imagery Dataset for Remaining Useful Life Estimation of Synthetic Fibre Ropes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04262)
*Anju Rani,Daniel Ortiz-Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含约34,700张高分辨率图像的新型公开数据集，记录了11根Dyneema SK75/78 HMPE合成纤维绳在60kN至280kN七种轴向载荷下从疲劳到失效的完整退化过程，旨在支持基于视觉的剩余使用寿命预测、损伤演化建模、异常检测和载荷条件预测等机器学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成纤维绳在海上起重机、风力涡轮机安装等关键应用中的失效可能导致灾难性安全事故和高昂停机成本，但目前缺乏公开可用的、覆盖完整退化生命周期的图像数据集，制约了数据驱动状态监测和剩余使用寿命预测研究的发展。

Method: 在滑轮弯曲试验台上对11根Dyneema SK75/78 HMPE绳索施加60kN至280kN七种不同轴向载荷进行循环疲劳测试直至机械失效；每隔固定次数（检测批次）在绳索不同横截面位置拍摄10张高分辨率图像，记录表面退化过程，并标注对应的循环次数以计算剩余使用寿命。

Result: 生成了约34,700张高分辨率图像，涵盖695至8,340个循环的疲劳寿命范围，每根绳索在不同载荷水平下的完整退化过程均被可视化记录，图像与循环次数一一对应，可直接用于计算任意时刻的剩余使用寿命。

Conclusion: 该数据集为基于视觉的合成纤维绳状态监测和预后算法提供了标准化基准资源，可支持剩余使用寿命回归、损伤演化建模、异常检测和载荷条件预测等多种机器学习任务，有助于推动该领域的算法开发与对比研究。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) estimation of synthetic fibre ropes (SFRs) is critical for safe operation in offshore-crane, wind turbine installation, and heavy-load handling applications, where rope failure can result in catastrophic safety incidents and costly downtime. Despite growing research interest in data-driven condition monitoring, there is no publicly available image dataset that captures the complete degradation lifecycle of SFRs under controlled cyclic fatigue loading. To address this gap, we present a novel image dataset comprising approximately 34,700 high-resolution images of eleven Dyneema SK75/78 high-modulus polyethylene (HMPE) rope samples subjected to cyclic fatigue on a sheave-bend test stand at seven distinct axial load levels ranging from 60 kN to 280 kN. Ropes were loaded until mechanical failure, with fatigue lifetimes ranging from 695 cycles to 8,340 cycles. After every fixed number of sheave cycles (an inspection burst), ten images were captured at different cross-sectional positions along the rope, providing spatially representative sampling of surface degradation throughout the rope's entire service life. The images obtained from each load are annotated with the corresponding elapsed cycle count, enabling a direct computation of RUL for any rope in the sequence. This dataset aims to support a broad range of machine learning (ML) tasks including RUL regression, damage progression modelling, anomaly detection, and load-conditioned prognostics. The dataset is intended to serve as a benchmark resource for the development and comparison of vision-based condition monitoring (CM) and prognostics algorithms for SFRs.

</details>

*Maryam Sadat Hosseini Azad,Shahriar Baradaran Shokouhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应置信度阈值选择方法，用于多任务可解释自动驾驶感知系统，并引入了一个包含958张图像、带有驾驶决策及推理人工标注的跨文化数据集IUST-XAI-AD，实验表明该方法能显著提升多任务F1分数，并揭示了跨文化驾驶行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在自动驾驶中缺乏透明性和安全性，现有的固定阈值方法在多任务场景下表现不佳，且缺少面向不同驾驶文化背景的专用评估数据集。

Method: 1) 提出全面的置信度阈值敏感性分析，评估不同阈值以确定各任务的最优决策边界；2) 引入自适应阈值选择方法替代传统固定阈值；3) 构建IUST-XAI-AD数据集，包含958张图像及人工标注的驾驶决策与推理。

Result: 自适应阈值选择方法在多任务场景下提升了F1分数；IUST-XAI-AD数据集揭示了跨文化驾驶行为模式，提供了更具挑战性的测试环境。

Conclusion: 本文的方法论和数据集共同为开发更可靠、可解释且文化适应性强的自动驾驶系统提供了实用工具，有助于推动其全球部署。

Abstract: Scene understanding is a vital part of autonomous driving systems, which requires the use of deep learning models. Deep learning methods are intrinsically black box models, which lack transparency and safety in autonomous driving. To make these systems transparent, multi-task visual understanding has become crucial for explainable autonomous driving perception systems, where simultaneous prediction of multiple driving behaviors and their underlying explanations is essential for safe navigation and human trust in autonomous vehicles. In order to design an accurate and cross-cultural explainable autonomous driving system, we introduce a comprehensive confidence threshold sensitivity analysis that evaluates various threshold values to identify optimal decision boundaries for different tasks. Our analysis demonstrates that traditional fixed threshold approaches are suboptimal for multi-task scenarios. Through extensive evaluation, we demonstrate that our adaptive threshold selection methodology improves F1-scores across different tasks. In addition, we introduce IUST-XAI-AD, a novel dataset consisting of 958 images with human annotations for driving decisions and corresponding reasoning. This dataset addresses the critical gap in domain-specific evaluation benchmarks for distinct driving contexts and provides a more challenging test environment compared to existing datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that confidence threshold sensitivity analysis can significantly improve model performance, while the introduction of the IUST-XAI-AD dataset reveals important insights about cross-cultural driving behavior patterns. The combined contributions of this work provide both methodological advances and practical evaluation tools that can accelerate the development of more reliable, explainable, and culturally-adaptive autonomous driving systems for global deployment.

</details>


### [28] [Hierarchical Visual Agent: Managing Contexts in Joint Image-Text Space for Advanced Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04304)
*Qihua Dong,Ruozhen He,Junwen Chen,Yizhou Wang,Xu Ma,Songyao Jiang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HierVA提出了一种分层视觉智能体框架，通过高层管理器生成计划并维护精简上下文、专用工作者执行推理和证据收集，结合图像-文本联合空间和缩放工具限制视觉范围，在CharXiv推理子集上取得了优于多模态基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在理解单一图表方面表现良好，但在跨多个子图的多步推理上存在困难，需要同时精确感知微小视觉元素并执行复杂推理。

Method: 提出HierVA分层视觉智能体框架：高层管理器生成计划并维护仅含关键信息的紧凑上下文；专用工作者执行推理、收集证据并返回结果；智能体维护分离的视觉和文本上下文，通过缩放工具限制视觉范围。

Result: 在CharXiv推理子集上取得了一致优于强多模态基线的结果；消融实验验证了分层架构、限定视觉范围和精简上下文各自贡献了互补的提升。

Conclusion: HierVA通过层次化设计、视觉范围控制和上下文精简，有效解决了跨子图多步推理的挑战。

Abstract: Advanced chart question answering requires both precise perception of small visual elements and multi-step reasoning across several subplots. While existing MLLMs are strong at understanding single plots, they often struggle with multi-step reasoning across multiple subplots. We propose HierVA, a hierarchical visual agent framework for chart reasoning that iteratively constructs and updates a working context in a joint image--text space. A high-level manager generates plans and maintains a compact context containing only key information, while specialized workers perform reasoning, gather evidence, and return results. In particular, the agent maintains separate visual and textual contexts, using a zoom-in tool to restrict the visual context. Experiments on the CharXiv reasoning subset demonstrate consistent improvements over strong multimodal baselines, and ablation studies verify that hierarchical architecture, scoped visual context, and distilled context contribute complementary gains.

</details>


### [29] [Intermediate Representations are Strong AI-Generated Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04358)
*Zhenhan Huang,Pin-Yu Chen,Tejaswini Pedapati,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于中间层数据嵌入敏感性的搜索方法，通过比较原始图像嵌入与扰动图像嵌入的相似性来检测AI生成图像，在GenImage和Forensics Small基准上优于现有训练-free和训练-based方法，在Forensics Small上AUROC分别提升39.61%（对比最佳训练-free方法）和5.14%（对比最佳训练-based方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测方法存在局限：训练-based方法计算成本高且难以泛化到未见数据域，而训练-free方法检测性能不足。因此需要一种兼顾泛化能力和检测性能的方法。

Method: 提出基于搜索的方法，利用中间层数据嵌入敏感性。给定一组真实和AI生成图像，计算原始图像嵌入与扰动图像嵌入之间的相似性，基于该相似性差异来检测AI生成图像。

Result: 在GenImage和Forensics Small两个基准上，该方法相比训练-free和训练-based现有最优方法均取得更优性能。在Forensics Small基准上，AUROC得分平均比最优训练-free方法高39.61%，比最优训练-based方法高5.14%。

Conclusion: 提出的搜索-based方法有效桥接了训练-free方法与训练-based方法之间的性能差距，在计算效率和检测性能之间取得更好平衡，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The rapid advancement in generative AI models has enabled the creation of photorealistic images. At the same time, there are growing concerns about the potential misuse and dangers of generated content, as well as a pressing need for effective AI-generated image detectors. However, current training-based detection techniques are typically computationally costly and can hardly be generalized to unseen data domains, while training-free methods fall short in detection performance. To bridge this gap, we propose a search-based method employing data embedding sensitivity in intermediate layers to detect AI-generated images. Given a set of real and AI-generated images, our method examines the similarity between original image embeddings and perturbed image embeddings, and detects AI-generated images based on the similarity. We examine the proposed method on two comprehensive benchmarks: GenImage and Forensics Small. Our method exhibits improved performance across different datasets compared to both training-free and training-based state-of-the-art methods. On average, our method achieves the largest performance gain on the Forensics Small benchmark by 39.61% compared to the best training-free method and 5.14% compared to the best training-based method in AUROC score.

</details>


### [30] [Optimize-at-Capture: Highly-adaptive Exposure Controlling for In-Vehicle Non-contact Heart-rate Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04397)
*Jieying Wang,Xinqi Cai,Caifeng Shan,Wenjin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向驾驶场景的远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）自适应曝光控制框架，通过历史皮肤反射的预测建模主动调整曝光参数，使面部感兴趣区域保持在最优动态范围内。同时发布了ExpDrive公开数据集（48名真实驾驶场景受试者），实验表明该方法相比固定曝光和相机自动曝光将MAE从14.1降至7.79 bpm，成功率从24.9%提升至57.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG系统在驾驶场景中受动态光照变化影响严重，而关键问题在于视频采集时缺乏曝光控制——固定曝光或相机内置自动曝光均无法在快速变化的照明条件下维持面部亮度稳定，导致rPPG信号提取质量下降。

Method: 提出一种高适应性的曝光控制框架，通过对历史皮肤反射信号的预测建模来主动调整曝光参数；该方法专为rPPG测量优化，确保皮肤ROI始终处于最适合rPPG信号提取的动态范围；同时构建了ExpDrive真实驾驶场景数据集（48人，同步面部视频与ECG参考）。

Result: 在挑战性驾驶场景中，相比固定曝光和标准自动曝光，MAE降低6.31 bpm（从14.1降至7.79 bpm），成功率提升32.3个百分点（从24.9%升至57.2%，p<0.001）；在弱光（雨天）和高眩光（晴天）条件下均显著改善了非接触心率监测性能。

Conclusion: 曝光感知的采集设计对提升驾驶场景下rPPG心率监测的鲁棒性至关重要，所提方法有效应对了动态光照变化挑战，验证了主动曝光控制相对于传统曝光策略的显著优势。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) holds great promise for continuous heart-rate monitoring of drivers in intelligent vehicles. However, its performance is severely degraded by the highly dynamic illumination changes. A critical yet overlooked factor is the lack of exposure controlling during video acquisition -- most existing systems rely on either fixed exposure settings or camera build-in auto-exposure, both of which fail to maintain stable facial brightness under rapidly changing lighting conditions during driving. To address this gap, we propose a highly-adaptive exposure controlling framework that proactively adjusts exposure parameters based on predictive modeling of historical skin reflections. Unlike standard auto-exposure, our method is specifically optimized for rPPG measurement, ensuring the skin region of interest (ROI) remains within the optimal dynamic range for rPPG signal extraction. As an important contribution of this study, we introduce ExpDrive, a public in-vehicle physiological monitoring dataset comprising synchronized facial video and reference ECG from 48 subjects captured under real driving conditions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms fixed exposure and standard auto-exposure strategies. Specifically, it reduces the Mean Absolute Error (MAE) by 6.31 bpm (from 14.1 to 7.79 bpm) and significantly increases the success rate by 32.3 percentage points (p < 0.001) (from 24.9% to 57.2%) across challenging driving scenarios. Notably, it clearly improved the performance of non-contact heart-rate monitoring in both low-light (rainy) and high-glare (sunny) conditions, validating the efficacy of exposure-aware acquisition design.

</details>


### [31] [Detecting Deepfakes via Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04405)
*Harry Cheng,Ming-Hui Liu,Tianyi Wang,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理启发的深度伪造检测方法HAAD，将图像隐空间建模为势能面，利用哈密顿动力学进行稳定性分析，通过轨迹统计量区分真实与伪造图像，在跨数据集迁移任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器需不断重新校准以适应生成式AI模型的新型伪造痕迹，存在静态模式识别的局限性。本文旨在打破这一循环，从动态稳定性分析视角出发，引入物理先验假设：真实图像趋向于低能稳定状态，而伪造图像处于高能不稳定状态。

Method: 1) 将图像隐空间建模为势能面，真实图像产生盆状低能响应，伪造图像产生高势高梯度响应；2) 采用哈密顿动力学作为稳定性探针，从静止释放隐状态，稳定区域样本保持有界，高梯度样本产生更大轨迹响应；3) 通过哈密顿作用量和能量耗散两个轨迹统计量量化动态行为。

Result: 在具有挑战性的跨数据集迁移基准上，HAAD优于所有评估的现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 实验支持了物理启发的稳定性先验在数字取证中的有效性，为深度伪造检测提供了从静态模式识别向动态稳定性分析转变的新视角。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid development of generative AI models, deepfake detectors are compelled to undergo periodic recalibration to capture newly developed synthetic artifacts. To break this cycle, we propose a new perspective on deepfake detection: moving from static pattern recognition to dynamical stability analysis. Specifically, our approach is motivated by physics-inspired priors: we hypothesize that natural images, as products of dissipative physical processes, tend to settle near stable, low-energy equilibria. In contrast, generative models optimize for statistical similarity to real images but do not explicitly enforce structural constraints such as geometric smoothness, leaving deepfakes more likely to occupy unstable, high-energy states. To operationalize this, we introduce Hamiltonian Action Anomaly Detection (HAAD), comprising three contributions: \textbf{i)} We model the image latent manifold as a potential energy surface. Under this hypothesis, real images are expected to produce basin-like low-energy responses, whereas fake images are more likely to induce high-potential, high-gradient responses. \textbf{ii)} We employ Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics as a stability probe. By releasing latent states from rest, samples near stable regions remain bounded, while high-gradient samples produce larger trajectory responses. \textbf{iii)} We quantify these dynamic behaviors through two trajectory statistics, \ie, Hamiltonian action and energy dissipation. Extensive experiments show that HAAD outperforms evaluated state-of-the-art baselines on challenging cross-dataset transfer benchmarks, supporting a physics-inspired stability prior for digital forensics.

</details>


### [32] [UAV as Urban Construction Change Monitor: A New Benchmark and Change Captioning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04409)
*Yupeng Gao,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PTNet，一种原型引导的任务自适应框架，用于遥感图像变化字幕生成与检测，并构建了UCCD数据集，在高分辨率城市建筑场景上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSICC方法依赖隐式特征差分，缺乏对结构化变化语义的显式建模，难以调和变化检测与字幕生成之间的表征冲突；同时现有基准对高分辨率城市建筑场景覆盖有限。

Method: PTNet包含三个核心模块：1) 可学习原型库引导跨时相交互，显式建模结构化变化语义；2) 多头门控机制解耦任务特定表征；3) 将检测所得空间先验注入字幕生成过程。此外构建了包含9,000对高分辨率图像和45,000句标注的UCCD基准数据集。

Result: 在UCCD和WHU-CDC两个数据集上的大量实验表明，PTNet持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PTNet通过原型引导的任务自适应框架有效解决了RSICC中变化检测与字幕生成的任务冲突，UCCD数据集填补了城市建筑场景的空白。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to generate spatially grounded natural language descriptions of scene evolution from bi-temporal imagery, moving beyond binary change masks toward semantic-level understanding. However, existing methods rely on implicit feature differencing without explicitly modeling structured change semantics, and struggle to reconcile the conflicting representation demands of change detection and caption generation. In addition, current benchmarks provide limited coverage of high-resolution urban construction scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose PTNet, a prototype-guided task-adaptive framework for joint change captioning and detection. PTNet explicitly models structured change semantics through a learnable prototype bank that guides cross-temporal interaction, disentangles task-specific representations via multi-head gating, and injects detection-derived spatial priors into caption generation, enabling coherent semantic correspondence while preserving fine-grained spatial sensitivity. Furthermore, we construct UCCD, a large-scale UAV-based benchmark comprising 9,000 high-resolution image pairs and 45,000 annotated sentences for urban construction monitoring. Extensive experiments on UCCD and WHU-CDC demonstrate that PTNet consistently outperforms existing methods. The dataset and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/G124556/ptnet.

</details>


### [33] [Evaluation Cards for XAI Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04410)
*Rokas Gipiškis,Olga Kurasova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI评估卡模板，用于标准化可解释人工智能评估指标的文档记录，以解决评估报告不透明、指标定义不一致等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释AI评估方法缺乏标准化，指标定义不一致、报告不完整、缺乏基线验证，评估报告透明度是核心但未充分解决的问题。

Method: 提出XAI评估卡（XAI Evaluation Card），一种类似模型卡的文档模板，要求明确声明目标属性、验证层级、指标假设、验证证据、博弈风险及已知失败案例。

Result: 该模板有望减少评估碎片化、支持元分析、提升XAI研究的问责性。

Conclusion: 将XAI评估卡作为社区规范采用，可系统性地改善可解释AI评估的标准化问题。

Abstract: The evaluation of explainable AI (XAI) methods is affected by a lack of standardization. Metrics are inconsistently defined, incompletely reported, and rarely validated against common baselines. In this paper, we identify transparency of evaluation reporting as a central, under-addressed problem. We propose the XAI Evaluation Card, a documentation template analogous to model cards, designed to accompany any study that introduces an XAI evaluation metric. The card covers explicit declaration of target properties, grounding levels, metric assumptions, validation evidence, gaming risks, and known failure cases. We argue that adopting this template as a community norm would reduce evaluation fragmentation, support meta-analysis, and improve accountability in XAI research.

</details>


### [34] [Modeling Subjective Urban Perception with Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00764)
*Lin Che,Xi Wang,Marc Pollefeys,Konrad Schindler,Martin Raubal,Peter Kiefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Place Pulse-Gaze数据集（街景图像+眼动追踪+感知标签）和Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework，通过三种设置（仅注视、注视+语义场景表示、注视+隐式视觉表示）研究注视行为如何助力主观城市感知建模，实验表明注视信号能有效预测主观感知，融合场景表示可进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市感知计算方法直接从街景图像建模，忽视了人类感知过程中注视行为的作用。

Method: 构建Place Pulse-Gaze数据集（街景图像+眼动记录+感知标签），提出Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework，包含三种设置：仅注视建模、注视融合显式语义场景表示、注视融合隐式丰富视觉表示。

Result: 单独注视信号即可有效预测主观城市感知；注视与场景表示融合可进一步提升预测性能，在语义和隐式视觉表示上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 融入人类感知过程（注视行为）对城市场景理解至关重要，为注视引导的多模态城市计算开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Urban perception describes how people subjectively evaluate urban environments, shaping how cities are experienced and understood. Existing computational approaches primarily model urban perception directly from street view images, but largely ignore the human perceptual process through which such judgments are formed. In this paper, we introduce Place Pulse-Gaze, an urban perception dataset that augments street view images with synchronized eye-tracking recordings and individual perception labels. Based on this dataset, we propose a Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework to study how gaze behavior contributes to the modeling of subjective urban perception. The framework systematically investigates three complementary settings: gaze-only modeling, gaze fusion with explicit semantic scene representations, and gaze fusion with implicit richer visual representations. Experiments show that gaze alone already carries useful predictive signals for subjective urban perception, and that integrating gaze with scene representations further improves prediction under both semantic and richer visual representations. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of incorporating human perceptual processes into urban scene understanding and open a direction for gaze-guided multimodal urban computing.

</details>


### [35] [Joint Semantic Token Selection and Prompt Optimization for Interpretable Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04425)
*Yating Wang,Yaqi Zhao,Yongshun Gong,Yilong Yin,Haoliang Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IPL框架，通过交替优化离散语义标记选择和连续提示学习，在保持下游任务适应性的同时提升可解释性和准确性，可作为现有点提示学习方法的即插即用扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续提示学习存在过拟合和可解释性差的问题，而离散提示优化依赖大型外部模型导致计算成本高、可扩展性有限，亟需一种兼顾可解释性与效率的混合框架。

Method: 将语义标记选择建模为近似子模优化问题，选择人类可理解且语义多样的标记；采用交替优化策略，在离散标记选择与连续提示调优之间迭代优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，IPL在五种代表性提示学习方法中一致提升了可解释性和准确性。

Conclusion: IPL是一种有效且可扩展的混合提示学习框架，能以即插即用方式增强现有方法，同时提升可解释性与性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP achieve strong visual-textual alignment, but often suffer from overfitting and limited interpretability when adapted through continuous prompt learning. While discrete prompt optimization improves interpretability, it usually depends on large external models, leading to high computational costs and limited scalability. In this paper, we propose Interpretable Prompt Learning (IPL), a hybrid framework that alternates between discrete semantic token selection and continuous prompt optimization. Specifically, IPL formulates semantic token selection as an approximate submodular optimization problem, encouraging tokens that are both human-understandable and semantically diverse. It further adopts an alternating optimization strategy to integrate discrete token selection with continuous prompt tuning, improving interpretability while preserving adaptability to downstream tasks. Our framework is plug-and-play, allowing seamless integration with existing prompt learning methods. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that IPL consistently improves both interpretability and accuracy across five representative prompt learning methods, providing an effective and scalable extension to existing frameworks.

</details>


### [36] [Ground4D: Spatially-Grounded Feedforward 4D Reconstruction for Unstructured Off-Road Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04435)
*Shuo Wang,Jilin Mei,Fuyang Liu,Wenfei Guan,Fanjie Kong,Zhihua Zhao,Shuai Wang,Chen Min,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ground4D提出了一种基于空间体素约束的4D前馈框架，通过体素化高斯注意力聚合和表面法线几何引导，解决了非结构化越野场景中由于高频几何、运动抖动和非刚性动态导致的时序观测冲突问题，在ORAD-3D和RELLIS-3D上实现了超越现有前馈方法的重建质量，并具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈高斯泼溅方法在非结构化越野场景中性能下降，原因是高频几何、自运动抖动和非刚性动态导致不同时间戳的高斯观测存在冲突，产生过度平滑渲染或结构伪影。

Method: 1) 体素化时序高斯聚合：将规范高斯空间划分为空间体素，在每个体素内执行查询条件化的时序注意力，通过体素内Softmax归一化使时序选择性与空间占用相互增强；2) 表面法线辅助几何引导：引入表面法线线索作为辅助几何约束，正则化高斯原语的几何结构。

Result: 在ORAD-3D和RELLIS-3D数据集上，Ground4D在重建质量上一致优于现有前馈方法，并零样本泛化到未见过的越野场景域。

Conclusion: 通过空间约束的时序注意力机制有效解决越野场景中的时序观测冲突，为无位姿的4D前馈越野场景重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Feedforward Gaussian Splatting has recently emerged as an efficient paradigm for 4D reconstruction in autonomous driving. However, in unstructured off-road scenes, its performance degrades due to high-frequency geometry, ego-motion jitter, and increased non-rigid dynamics. These factors introduce conflicting Gaussian observations across timestamps, leading to either over-smoothed renderings or structural artifacts. To address this issue, we propose Ground4D, a spatially-grounded 4D feedforward framework for pose-free off-road reconstruction. The key idea is to resolve temporal conflicts through spatially localized conditioning. Specifically, we introduce voxel-grounded temporal Gaussian aggregation, which partitions the canonical Gaussian space into spatial voxels and performs query-conditioned temporal attention within each voxel. Intra-voxel softmax normalization ensures that temporal selectivity and spatial occupancy become mutually reinforcing rather than conflicting. We furthermore introduce surface normal cues as auxiliary geometric guidance to regularize the geometry of Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments on ORAD-3D and RELLIS-3D demonstrate that Ground4D consistently outperforms existing feedforward methods in reconstruction quality and generalizes zero-shot to unseen off-road domains. Project page and code:https://github.com/wsnbws/Ground4D.

</details>


### [37] [Deep Reprogramming Distillation for Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04447)
*Siyuan Du,Yuhang Zhou,Haolin Li,Jiangchao Yao,Haishuai Wang,Hui Lin,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度重编程蒸馏（DRD）框架，通过可重编程模块解决医学基础模型适配下游任务时的领域差异、任务差异及计算约束问题，并采用中心核对齐（CKA）蒸馏实现鲁棒知识迁移，在18项医学下游任务上全面超越以往的PEFT和KD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型在适配特定下游场景时面临预训练与下游任务之间的领域/任务差异、实际计算和速度约束等挑战，而现有知识蒸馏（KD）要求师生模型共享任务、训练策略和结构族，参数高效微调（PEFT）难以实现个性化和轻量化部署，二者结合也无法解决模型结构和训练策略不一致导致的低效知识迁移问题。

Method: 提出深度重编程蒸馏（DRD）框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 可重编程模块（Reprogramming Module），一方面克服预训练与下游场景的领域和任务差异，另一方面构建从基础模型到轻量级下游模型的student-friendly高效蒸馏；(2) 中心核对齐（CKA）蒸馏方法，缓解不同训练条件下的变异性，促进鲁棒知识迁移。

Result: 在18项医学下游任务（涵盖2D/3D分类和2D/3D分割）上，DRD在不同基础模型下均全面超越了以往的PEFT和KD方法。

Conclusion: DRD框架有效解决了医学基础模型适配下游场景的通用挑战，通过重编程模块弥合领域与任务差异，并通过CKA蒸馏实现鲁棒高效的知识迁移，为医学基础模型的轻量化、个性化部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Medical foundation models pre-trained on large-scale datasets have shown powerful versatile performance. However, when adapting medical foundation models for specific medical scenarios, it remains the inevitable challenge due to the gap induced by the discrepancy between pre-training and downstream tasks, the real-world computation, and speed constraints. Relevant techniques that probably handle this challenge more or less suffer from some intrinsic limitations. For example, knowledge distillation (KD) assumes that teacher and student models share the same task, training strategy, and model structure family, while prevalent parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) fails to achieve personalized and lightweight deployment. Even the combination of PEFT and KD still struggles to resolve model structures and training strategies inconsistencies between teacher and student models, leading to inefficient knowledge transfer. In this study, we propose a novel framework called Deep Reprogramming Distillation (DRD) to combat the general adaptation challenge. Specifically, DRD introduces the novel reprogramming module that on the one side overcomes the domain and task discrepancy between pretraining and downstream scenarios, and on the other side builds the student-friendly efficient distillation from foundation models to lightweight downstream models. Furthermore, to mitigate variability under different training conditions, we design a centered kernel alignment (CKA) distillation method to promote robust knowledge transfer. Empirical results show that DRD surpasses previous PEFT and KD methods across 18 medical downstream tasks under different foundation models, covering various scenarios including 2D/3D classification and 2D/3D segmentation.

</details>


### [38] [RemoteZero: Geospatial Reasoning with Zero Human Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04451)
*Liang Yao,Fan Liu,Shengxiang Xu,Chuanyi Zhang,Rui Min,Shimin Di,Yuhui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RemoteZero，一种无需边界框监督的地理空间推理框架，利用MLLM更强的判别能力（验证能力优于生成能力）替代几何监督，通过内在语义验证和GRPO训练实现自进化，在无标注遥感数据上取得与强监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间推理模型虽然实现了推理路径的自主生成，但最终定位仍依赖人工标注的坐标监督，无法在大量无标注遥感数据上实现真正的自我进化。为了打破这一瓶颈，需要一个无需几何监督（边界框/坐标标注）的推理框架。

Method: RemoteZero利用一个关键发现：MLLM验证某个区域是否满足查询的能力通常强于直接生成精确坐标的能力。基于这种不对称性，用内在的语义验证信号替代几何监督，并结合GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）训练方法，实现无需边界框标注的训练。同时支持迭代式自进化，模型可通过自身验证信号从未标注遥感图像中持续改进。

Result: 在实验评估中，RemoteZero取得了与强监督方法相竞争的性能，验证了自验证训练策略在地理空间推理定位中的潜力。

Conclusion: RemoteZero证明了通过自验证（而非外部标注）进行训练是可行的，为地理空间推理在无标注遥感数据上的自我进化提供了新路径，有望突破当前对人工标注坐标的依赖瓶颈。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning requires models to resolve complex spatial semantics and user intent into precise target locations for Earth observation.
