Motivation: 自动化青光眼检测对防止不可逆视力丧失和减轻医疗负担至关重要，但现有模型在不同患者群体间存在公平性问题，缺乏能同时处理多个敏感属性的去偏方法。

Method: 1) 文本编码器：利用大语言模型生成带不同敏感属性的合成临床描述（保持疾病语义不变），采用对比对齐目标鼓励人口统计不变表征。2) 视觉编码器：双层公平策略，包括互信息正则化（降低特征与人口统计组的统计依赖）和多判别器对抗去偏。

Result: 在哈佛Fair-VLMed数据集上，零样本和线性探测评估下均有效降低DPD和DEOdds人口统计差异；在私有FairFundus数据集上，跨域跨模态设置下保持公平性优势，诊断性能处于竞争范围。

Conclusion: FairEnc能够在分布偏移下泛化公平性，支持在真实临床环境中实现更公平的部署。

Abstract: Automated glaucoma detection is critical for preventing irreversible vision loss and reducing the burden on healthcare systems. However, ensuring fairness across diverse patient populations remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we propose FairEnc, a fair pretraining method for vision-language models (VLMs) that enables simultaneous debiasing across multiple sensitive attributes. FairEnc jointly mitigates biases in both textual and visual modalities with respect to multiple sensitive attributes, including race, gender, ethnicity, and language. Specifically, for the textual encoder, we leverage a large language model to generate synthetic clinical descriptions with varied sensitive attributes while preserving disease semantics, and employ a contrastive alignment objective to encourage demographic-invariant representations. For the visual encoder, we propose a dual-level fairness strategy that combines mutual information regularization to reduce statistical dependence between learned features and demographic groups, with multi-discriminator adversarial debiasing. Comprehensive experiments on the publicly available Harvard-FairVLMed dataset demonstrate that FairEnc effectively reduces demographic disparity as measured by DPD and DEOdds while achieving strong diagnostic performance under both zero-shot and linear probing evaluations. Additional experiments on the private FairFundus dataset show that FairEnc consistently preserves fairness advantages under cross-domain and cross-modality settings and maintains diagnostic performance within a competitive range. These results highlight FairEnc's ability to generalize fairness under distribution shifts, supporting its potential for more equitable deployment in real-world clinical settings. Our codebase and synthetic clinical notes are available at https://github.com/Mohamed-Elhabebe/FairEnc

</details>


### [76] [DART: A Vision-Language Foundation Model for Comprehensive Rope Condition Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04943)
*Anju Rani,Daniel Ortiz-Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART是一个视觉-语言基础模型，通过统一的多任务架构实现合成纤维绳索的全面状态监测，包括损伤分类（93.22%准确率）、严重程度回归（Spearman rho=0.94）、少样本识别（20-shot下89.2% macro-F1）等，且无需任务特定的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成纤维绳索的状态监测不仅需要分类器，还需要连续的严重程度估计、维护建议、异常标记、退化时间线和自动化报告，而现有方法无法从单张检测图像中同时提供这些信息。

Method: DART将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）扩展到跨模态领域，通过Severity-Conditioned Cross-Modal Fusion (SC-CMF)模块耦合Vision Transformer (ViT-H/14)与Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct。三大创新：(1) HD-MASK，基于显著性引导的掩码策略；(2) 每类可学习的严重程度门控；(3) Contrastive Damage Disentanglement (CDD)损失函数。

Result: 在4,270张图像、14个细粒度损伤类别的数据集上，DART达到：损伤分类93.22%准确率（+38.5 pp优于纯视觉基线）、连续严重程度回归Spearman rho=0.94、20-shot少样本识别89.2% macro-F1。

Conclusion: DART作为一个通用状态监测基础模型，从单一共享表示中提供可操作的检测智能，远超传统分类能力，无需任务特定微调即可支持多种下游任务。

Abstract: The condition monitoring (CM) of synthetic fibre ropes (SFRs) used in offshore, maritime, and industrial settings demands more than a classifier: inspectors need continuous severity estimates, maintenance recommendations, anomaly flags, deterioration timelines, and automated reports, all from a single inspection image. We present DART (Damage Assessment via Rope Transformer), a vision-language foundation model that addresses the full rope inspection workflow through a unified multi-task architecture. DART extends the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) to the cross-modal domain by coupling a Vision Transformer (ViT-H/14) with Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct via a Severity-Conditioned Cross-Modal Fusion (SC-CMF) module. Three architectural innovations drive the model's versatility: (1) HD-MASK, a saliency-guided masking strategy that focuses self-supervised reconstruction on damage-dense patches; (2) per-class learnable severity gates that adaptively weight language grounding by damage category; and (3) a Contrastive Damage Disentanglement (CDD) loss that shapes the embedding space to simultaneously encode damage type, severity ordering, and cross-modal semantics. Trained once on 4,270 images spanning 14 fine-grained rope damage classes, the frozen DART backbone supports downstream tasks without any task-specific fine-tuning: damage classification (93.22 % accuracy, 91.04 % macro-F1, +38.5 pp over a vision-only baseline), continuous severity regression (Spearman rho = 0.94, within-1-ordinal accuracy 99.6 %), few-shot recognition (89.2 % macro-F1 at 20 shots). These results demonstrate that DART functions as a general-purpose CM backbone that goes well beyond classification, providing actionable inspection intelligence from a single shared representation.

</details>


### [77] [ICPR 2026 Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera (TVRID)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04977)
*Raphaël Delécluse,Hazem Wannous,Laurent Guimas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛，该竞赛聚焦于隐私感知的俯视行人重识别。竞赛使用由四台俯视Intel RealSense D455相机采集的86个身份的RGB-Depth数据集，包含三种赛道：RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID和跨模态检索。结果表明难度排序为RGB > Depth > Cross-Modal，强调了模态约束检索的挑战性和模态不变学习的可行性。数据集和评估脚本已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人重识别数据集多基于水平视角，缺乏从俯视角度且考虑隐私保护的RGB-Depth数据集。TVRID旨在填补这一空白，提供一个可重复的基准测试平台，推动隐私感知的俯视行人重识别研究。

Method: 使用四台同步的俯视Intel RealSense D455相机采集RGB和Depth配对数据，涵盖平坦、上升、下降和倾斜四种几何视角变化。设置了三个评估赛道：RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID和RGB↔Depth跨模态检索，使用mAP和CMC-1作为统一评估指标，由服务器端进行统一评分。

Result: 竞赛结果显示任务难度排序为RGB > Depth > Cross-Modal，即RGB模态的检索最具挑战性，而跨模态检索相对容易。这表明模态约束下的检索更具难度，而模态不变学习可以实现较强的性能。

Conclusion: TVRID通过公开数据集、评估脚本和文档，为俯视、基于深度和跨模态的行人重识别建立了一个可重复的基准。该基准突出了隐私感知场景下各模态任务的难度差异，并为未来研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: This companion paper reports the ICPR 2026 TVRID competition on privacy-aware top-view person re-identification. We present the competition setting, the released RGB-Depth dataset, and a summary of final results with descriptions of the top entries. TVRID contains 86 identities captured by four synchronized overhead Intel RealSense D455 cameras, with paired RGB/Depth streams and structured geometric variation across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. The evaluation protocol includes three tracks: RGB Re-ID, Depth Re-ID, and RGB$\leftrightarrow$Depth cross-modal retrieval. Submissions are ranked using mAP and CMC-1 under a unified server-side evaluation. The final results show a clear difficulty ordering (RGB $>$ Depth $>$ Cross-Modal), highlighting both the challenge of modality-constrained retrieval and the feasibility of strong performance with modality-invariant learning. By releasing the dataset at https://zenodo.org/records/17909410, the evaluation scripts at https://github.com/RaphaelDel/ICPR-TVRID, and the accompanying documentation, TVRID establishes a reproducible benchmark for top-view, depth-based, and cross-modal person re-id.

</details>


### [78] [Attention-Based Chaotic Self-Supervision for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04985)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Amanda Pontes de Oliveira Ornelas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE）作为医学图像分类的SSL预训练策略，通过混沌变换替代随机掩码来保留细粒度诊断特征，并设计注意力融合机制结合通用与领域特定特征，在ISIC 2018和APTOS 2019数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL方法（如MAE）使用随机掩码可能破坏医学图像中的细粒度诊断特征，需要一种能保留关键诊断信息的预训练策略。

Method: 提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE），对输入图像施加混沌变换替代随机掩码，训练自编码器重建原图；同时设计注意力融合机制，将CDAE训练得到的编码器特征与标准编码器特征进行融合。

Result: ISIC 2018：准确率0.9221，F1-macro 0.8530；APTOS 2019：准确率0.8644，F1-macro 0.7433。

Conclusion: CDAE通过"逆混沌"重建迫使编码器学习鲁棒的领域特定特征，结合注意力融合机制能有效提升医学图像分类性能。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification usually achieve promising results but typically rely on large, annotated datasets or standard transfer learning from ImageNet. Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, yet common methods like masked autoencoders (MAEs) may inadvertently destroy fine-grained diagnostic features by using random masking. In this paper, we propose a novel SSL pre-training strategy, the Chaotic Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE). Instead of masking, we apply a chaotic transformation to the input image, tasking an autoencoder to reconstruct the original. We hypothesize this forces the encoder to learn robust, domain-specific features by "inverting the chaos". Furthermore, we propose an attentive fusion mechanism that combines features from our CDAE-trained encoder with a standard encoder, leveraging the strengths of both general and domain-specific representations. Our method is evaluated on two public medical datasets: ISIC 2018 (skin lesions) and APTOS 2019 (diabetic retinopathy). The proposed model achieves high performance, with an accuracy of 0.9221 and an F1-macro of 0.8530 on ISIC 2018, and an accuracy of 0.8644 and F1-macro of 0.7433 on APTOS 2019, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [79] [Low-Rank Adaptation of Geospatial Foundation Models for Wildfire Mapping Using Sentinel-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04989)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种地理空间基础模型（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）在野火烧痕地映射中的表现，发现LoRA微调方法在更新不到1%参数的情况下，取得了最佳的跨域泛化性能，其中Prithvi-v2+LoRA组合总体精度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火烧痕地映射对灾害评估、排放建模及理解火-气候交互至关重要，但现有地理空间基础模型在跨地理和时间的域迁移下如何高效适配尚不明确。

Method: 利用2017-2023年美国与加拿大3,820个野火事件的Sentinel-2数据，对Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2三种模型进行全参数微调、仅解码器微调和LoRA三种适配策略的系统对比，并在不同生物群落中开展空间和时间泛化测试。

Result: LoRA在所有实验中均取得最佳的跨域泛化能力，仅更新不足1%的参数；Prithvi-v2结合LoRA取得最高总体精度，且相比全参数微调提升最大。

Conclusion: 采用LoRA等轻量参数高效方法适配的地理空间基础模型，为大规模野火烧痕地映射提供了鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Wildfire burned-area mapping is essential for damage assessment, emissions modeling, and understanding fire-climate interactions across diverse ecological regions. Recent geospatial foundation models provide strong general-purpose representations for satellite imagery, yet there is still no clear understanding of how to efficiently adapt these models for downstream Earth observation tasks, particularly under geographic and temporal domain shift. This study evaluates three state-of-the-art Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) - Terramind, DINOv3, and Prithvi-v2 - for burned-area mapping across the United States and Canada using Sentinel-2 data. Leveraging 3,820 wildfire events from 2017-2023, we conduct spatial and temporal generalization tests across diverse biomes. We systematically compare full fine-tuning, decoder-only fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for adapting each model. Across all experiments, LoRA provides the strongest cross-domain generalization while updating less than 1% of parameters, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Prithvi-v2 with LoRA achieves the highest overall accuracy and the largest improvement compared to full fine-tuning. These findings indicate that geospatial foundation models, when adapted using lightweight parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA, offer a robust and scalable solution for large-scale burned-area mapping. Code is available at https://github.com/alishibli97/wildfire-lora-gfm.

</details>


### [80] [CARD: A Multi-Modal Automotive Dataset for Dense 3D Reconstruction in Challenging Road Topography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05014)
*Gasser Elazab,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Malte Splietker,Aditya Date,Michael Unterreiner,Maximilian Jansen,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARD是一个多模态驾驶数据集，提供准稠密3D真值，覆盖减速带、坑洼、非平整路面和越野路段，深度点数约为KITTI的6.5倍，包含立体相机、前后LiDAR、6-DoF位姿等传感器数据，并提供了面向路面不平整区域的2D边界框标注及标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶数据集大多在平整路面采集，且仅提供稀疏LiDAR真值，难以评估细粒度几何深度信息；同时缺乏针对路面不平整区域（如减速带、坑洼）的标注和评估基准。

Method: 采用全局快门立体相机、前后LiDAR、LiDAR-惯性里程计等传感器融合，通过多LiDAR融合生成每帧约50万有效深度像素，并提供六自由度位姿、每轮运动轨迹及完整标定参数。

Result: 数据集覆盖德国和意大利约110公里、4.7小时驾驶数据；深度点数约为KITTI Depth Completion的6.5倍，平均为其他公开数据集的10倍以上。

Conclusion: CARD填补了路面不平整场景下稠密深度真值的空白，为几何深度估计和路面不规则感知任务提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Autonomous driving must operate across diverse surfaces to enable safe mobility. However, most driving datasets are captured on well-paved flat roads. Moreover, recent driving datasets primarily provide sparse LiDAR ground truth for images, which is insufficient for assessing fine-grained geometry in depth estimation and completion. To address these gaps, we introduce CARD, a multi-modal driving dataset that delivers quasi-dense 3D ground truth across continuous sequences rich in speed bumps, potholes, irregular surfaces and off-road segments. Our sensor suite includes synchronized global-shutter stereo cameras, front and rear LiDARs, 6-DoF poses from LiDAR-inertial odometry, per-wheel motion traces, and full calibration. Notably, our multi-LiDAR fusion yields ~500K valid depth pixels per frame, about 6.5x more than KITTI Depth Completion and 10x more on average than other public driving datasets. The dataset spans ~110 km and 4.7 hours across Germany and Italy. In addition, CARD provides 2D bounding boxes targeting road-topography irregularities, enabling accurate benchmarking for both geometry and perception tasks. Furthermore, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol for road surface irregularities on CARD and benchmark state-of-the-art depth estimation models to provide strong baselines. The CARD dataset is hosted on https://huggingface.co/CARD-Data.

</details>


### [81] [Local Intrinsic Dimension Unveils Hallucinations in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05026)
*Bartlomiej Sobieski,Matthew Tivnan,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Pengfei Jin,Przemyslaw Biecek,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流形不稳定性的视角来解释扩散模型中的结构幻觉，发现局部内在维度（LID）是导致幻觉的主要因素，并提出了Intrinsic Quenching（IQ）机制来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的结构性幻觉（如多指手）难以解释，现有研究仅提供部分解释（如模式插值），缺乏统一的视角和有效的缓解方法。

Method: 将幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性；基于该不稳定性的过滤性能优于时间过滤；识别局部内在维度（LID）为不稳定性的驱动力；提出Intrinsic Quenching（IQ）机制通过降低LID来缓解幻觉。

Result: 基于不稳定性的幻觉过滤器达到或超越时间过滤器的性能；IQ在多个基准上一致优于标准的幻觉减少基线；在医学成像下游任务中有效保证解剖结构一致性。

Conclusion: 流形不稳定性是扩散模型结构幻觉的关键来源，LID是其主要驱动力，IQ作为一种直接校正机制为提升生成模型的可靠性提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are prone to generating structural hallucinations - samples that match the statistical properties of the training data yet defy underlying structural rules, resulting in anomalies like hands with more than five fingers. Recent research studied this failure mode from several viewpoints, offering partial explanations to their occurrence, such as mode interpolation. In this work, we propose a complementary perspective that treats hallucinations as instabilities on the model-induced manifold. We begin by showing that a hallucination filter based on such instabilities matches or exceeds the performance of the recently proposed temporal one. By tracing the source of these instabilities, we identify local intrinsic dimension (LID) as their primary driver and propose Intrinsic Quenching (IQ), a direct corrective mechanism that deflates it to alleviate hallucinations. IQ consistently outperforms standard hallucination reduction baselines across a wide array of benchmarks and offers a highly promising solution for enforcing anatomical consistency in downstream medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [82] [Few-Shot Learning Pipeline for Monkeypox Skin Disease Classification Using CNN Feature Extractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05034)
*Md. Safirur Rashid,Sabbir Ahmed,Muhammad Usama Islam,Sumona Hoque Mumu,Md. Hasanul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SimpleShot的少样本学习框架，用于猴痘及痘样皮肤病变识别。该框架使用冻结的预训练CNN骨干提取特征，通过最近质心分类器进行分类。在三个公开数据集上对六种CNN骨干进行了系统评测，结果显示MobileNetV2_100在各类任务中准确率最高。跨数据集评估表明二分类迁移相对稳定，而多分类在域偏移下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在疾病分类中表现优异，但依赖大规模标注数据集，这对于猴痘等新兴或罕见疾病不切实际。因此需要一种能够在有限标注样本下有效识别的方法。

Method: 提出FSL框架，使用冻结的预训练CNN骨干提取皮肤病变图像的特征嵌入，然后通过SimpleShot（基于归一化嵌入空间的最近质心分类）进行分类。在三种数据集上对六种CNN骨干进行系统基准测试，涵盖2-way、4-way、6-way任务及1-shot、5-shot、10-shot配置。

Result: MobileNetV2_100在所有模型中一致取得最高准确率。跨数据集评估显示二分类Mpox-vs-Others迁移相对稳定，而多分类在域偏移下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 归纳式FSL方法与轻量级CNN骨干结合具有实用价值，域鲁棒性对于可靠的临床部署至关重要。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in disease classification, their effectiveness often depends on access to large annotated datasets, which is an impractical requirement for emerging or rare conditions such as Monkeypox. To overcome this limitation, we propose a few-shot learning (FSL) framework that employs SimpleShot, a lightweight, non-parametric, inductive classifier, for Monkeypox and pox-like skin disease recognition from limited labeled examples. The proposed pipeline passes the skin lesion images through a frozen, pretrained CNN backbone to obtain feature embeddings, which are then classified via SimpleShot using nearest-centroid comparisons in a normalized embedding space. We systematically benchmark six widely used CNN backbones as feature extractors under consistent experimental settings, enabling fair comparison. Experiments on three publicly available datasets (MSLD v1.0, MSID, and MSLD v2.0) are conducted across 2-way, 4-way, and 6-way tasks with 1-shot, 5-shot, and 10-shot configurations. Among all models, MobileNetV2_100 consistently achieves the highest accuracy. In addition, we present a cross-dataset evaluation for Monkeypox classification, revealing that binary Mpox-vs-Others transfer remains comparatively stable while multi-class performance degrades significantly under domain shift. Together, these results demonstrate the practical utility of combining inductive FSL methods with lightweight CNN backbones and highlight the importance of domain robustness for reliable real-world clinical deployment.

</details>


### [83] [When Relations Break: Analyzing Relation Hallucination in Vision-Language Model Under Rotation and Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05045)
*Philip Wootaek Shin,Ajay Narayanan Sridhar,Sivani Devarapalli,Rui Zhang,Jack Sampson,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉扰动（旋转和噪声）对视觉语言模型（VLM）关系推理能力的影响，发现即使轻微的扰动也会显著降低关系理解性能。虽然提示增强和预处理策略（方向校正、去噪）能部分改善，但无法完全消除关系幻觉，揭示了模型在感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多模态任务中表现出色，但在涉及对象间交互的关系推理中容易出现关系幻觉（relation hallucination）。现有研究尚未充分探讨视觉扰动（如旋转和噪声）如何影响模型的关系理解能力，以及现有补救策略的有效性。

Method: 在多个VLM和数据集上，系统地施加视觉扰动（旋转和噪声），评估关系推理性能的变化；同时测试提示增强（prompt-based augmentation）和预处理策略（方向校正、去噪）对缓解关系幻觉的效果。

Result: 轻微扰动即显著降低关系推理性能；提示增强和预处理策略仅能部分恢复性能，无法完全消除关系幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在感知鲁棒性和关系理解之间存在明显差距，亟需开发更鲁棒、更具几何感知能力的模型。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong multimodal performance but remain prone to relation hallucination, which requires accurate reasoning over inter-object interactions. We study the impact of visual perturbations, specifically rotation and noise, and show that even mild distortions significantly degrade relational reasoning across models and datasets. We further evaluate prompt-based augmentation and preprocessing strategies (orientation correction and denoising), finding that while they offer partial improvements, they do not fully resolve hallucinations. Our results reveal a gap between perceptual robustness and relational understanding, highlighting the need for more robust, geometry-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [84] [Direct Product Flow Matching: Decoupling Radial and Angular Dynamics for Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05054)
*Hongxu Chen,Yanghao Wang,Bowei Zhu,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Ziqi Jiang,Lin Li,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Warped Product流匹配（WP-FM）的统一黎曼框架，通过解耦径向和角度子流形，解决现有流匹配方法在视觉-语言模型少样本适配中的几何先验不兼容问题，其中直接积分流匹配（DP-FM）在11个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在预训练跨模态特征上存在几何先验不兼容的问题，导致少样本适配性能不佳。

Method: 从极分解角度分析现有方法的三个局限性（角度动力学失真、径向动力学忽视、上下文无关无条件流），提出Warped Product流匹配（WP-FM）统一黎曼框架，并推导出直接积分流匹配（DP-FM），引入常数扭曲度量实现解耦的圆柱流形，同时通过条件化预训练VLM隐藏状态注入数据集特定信息。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上，DP-FM在多步少样本适配任务中达到新的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角分析并改进流匹配方法，解耦径向和角度动力学可有效提升少样本适配性能。

Abstract: Recent flow matching (FM) methods improve the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models, by modeling cross-modal alignment as a continuous multi-step flow. In this paper, we argue that existing FM methods are inherently constrained by incompatible geometric priors on pre-trained cross-modal features, resulting in suboptimal adaptation performance. We first analyze these methods from a polar decomposition perspective (i.e., radial and angular sub-manifolds). Under this new geometric view, we identify three overlooked limitations in them: 1) Angular dynamics distortion: The radial-angular coupling induces non-uniform speed on the angular sub-manifold, leading to regression training difficulty and extra truncation errors. 2) Radial dynamics neglect: Feature normalization discards modality confidence, failing to distinguish out-of-distribution and in-distribution data, and abandoning crucial radial dynamics. 3) Context-agnostic unconditional flow: Dataset-specific information loss during pre-trained cross-modal feature extraction remains unrecovered. To resolve these issues, we propose warped product flow matching (WP-FM), a unified Riemannian framework that reformulates alignment on a warped product manifold. Within this framework, we derive direct product flow matching (DP-FM) by introducing a constant-warping metric, which yields a decoupled cylindrical manifold (i.e., direct product manifold). DP-FM enables independent radial evolution and constant-speed angular geodesic transport, effectively eliminating angular dynamics distortion while preserving radial consistency. Meanwhile, we incorporate classifier-free guidance by conditioning the flow on the pre-trained VLMs' hidden states to inject missing dataset-specific information. Extensive results across 11 benchmarks have demonstrated that DP-FM achieves a new state-of-the-art for multi-step few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [85] [ScriptHOI: Learning Scripted State Transitions for Open-Vocabulary Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05057)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,SuiYang Guang,Tuan Kiet Pham,Linh Chi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScriptHOI，一种基于脚本化状态转移的结构化框架，将交互短语分解为多个语义槽（身体角色、接触、几何、 affordance、运动、对象状态），通过视觉状态分词器解析状态令牌，利用槽匹配器计算脚本覆盖率和冲突度来校准HOI逻辑值，并引入区间部分标签学习和反事实脚本对比损失，以减少物体 affordance 主导的误判，提升开放词汇HOI检测中罕见和未见交互的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言HOI检测器在预测时往往被物体 affordance 和短语级共现所主导（如看到刀和蛋糕就预测"切蛋糕"），而未能验证手、工具、目标、接触模式和对象状态是否共同支持该动作，导致预测缺乏结构化证据验证，产生大量 affordance 冲突的假阳性。

Method: 1) 将交互短语分解为body-role、contact、geometry、affordance、motion、object-state六个语义槽，构建软脚本化状态转移；2) 视觉状态分词器将检测到的人-物对解析为对应的状态令牌；3) 槽匹配器计算脚本覆盖率和脚本冲突度，用于校准HOI逻辑值；4) 区间部分标签学习为未标注候选分配基于脚本推导的概率上下界，避免直接视为负类；5) 反事实脚本对比损失通过交换单个脚本槽来抑制仅基于物体的捷径学习。

Result: 在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI数据集上，ScriptHOI在罕见和未见交互识别上取得改进，同时显著减少了 affordance 冲突的假阳性。

Conclusion: 将交互短语分解为结构化脚本状态转移，并引入基于脚本的校准与训练约束，能有效提升开放词汇HOI检测的泛化能力，尤其是在缺乏标注的交互类别上，同时降低物体共现导致的错误预测。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires recognizing interaction phrases that may not appear as annotated categories during training. Recent vision-language HOI detectors improve semantic transfer by matching human-object features with text embeddings, but their predictions are often dominated by object affordance and phrase-level co-occurrence. As a result, a model may predict \textit{cut cake} from the presence of a knife and a cake without verifying whether the hand, tool, target, contact pattern, and object state jointly support the action. We propose \textbf{ScriptHOI}, a structured framework that represents each interaction phrase as a soft scripted state transition. Rather than treating a phrase as a single class token, ScriptHOI decomposes it into body-role, contact, geometry, affordance, motion, and object-state slots. A visual state tokenizer parses each detected human-object pair into corresponding state tokens, and a slot-wise matcher estimates both script coverage and script conflict. These two quantities calibrate HOI logits, expose missing visual evidence, and provide training constraints for incomplete annotations. To avoid suppressing valid but unannotated interactions, we further introduce interval partial-label learning, which constrains unannotated candidates with script-derived lower and upper probability bounds instead of assigning closed-world negatives. A counterfactual script contrast loss swaps individual script slots to discourage object-only shortcuts. Experiments on HICO-DET, V-COCO, and open-vocabulary HOI splits show that ScriptHOI improves rare and unseen interaction recognition while substantially reducing affordance-conflict false positives.

</details>


### [86] [Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization for Camera-LiDAR Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05072)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Jiawei Lian,Zhengxue Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPR 提出了一种高度引导的投影重参数化方法，通过 LiDAR 高度先验自适应调整柱状采样范围，并结合渐进式高度条件化训练策略，在保持实时推理的同时实现了优于现有方法的 3D 占用预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于固定投影空间的 2D-to-3D 视图变换方法中，3D 参考点沿柱体均匀采样，难以捕捉真实场景的稀疏性和高度变化，导致特征对应模糊和聚合不可靠。

Method: 1) 利用 LiDAR 编码为 BEV 高度图，捕获点云最大高度；2) 基于高度先验自适应调整每个柱体的采样范围，实现投影空间的重参数化；3) 掩码无效高度区域以避免误导特征聚合；4) 引入渐进式高度条件化训练策略，从真值高度逐步过渡到 LiDAR 高度以缓解训练不稳定。

Result: 在多个数据集上一致超越现有最先进方法，同时保持实时推理速度。

Conclusion: HiPR 通过高度引导的投影重参数化有效解决了固定投影空间在 3D 占用预测中的局限性，验证了自适应的几何感知采样对提升场景理解性能的重要性。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction aims to infer dense, voxel-wise scene semantics from sensor observations, where the 2D-to-3D view transformation serves as a crucial step in bridging image features and volumetric representations. Most previous methods rely on a fixed projection space, where 3D reference points are uniformly sampled along pillars. However, such sampling struggles to capture the sparsity and height variations of real-world scenes, leading to ambiguous correspondences and unreliable feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose HiPR, a camera-LiDAR occupancy framework with Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization. HiPR first encodes LiDAR into a BEV height map to capture the maximum height of the point cloud. HiPR then adjusts the sampling range of each pillar using the height prior, enabling adaptive reparameterization of the projection space. As a result, the projected points are redistributed into geometrically meaningful regions rather than fixed ranges. Meanwhile, we mask out the invalid parts of the height map to avoid misleading the feature aggregation. In addition, to alleviate the training instability caused by noisy LiDAR-derived heights, we introduce a training-time Progressive Height Conditioning strategy, which gradually transitions the conditioning signal from ground-truth heights to LiDAR heights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiPR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining real-time inference. The code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/Rayn-Wu/HiPR.

</details>


### [87] [FlowDIS: Language-Guided Dichotomous Image Segmentation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05077)
*Andranik Sargsyan,Shant Navasardyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowDIS是一种基于流匹配框架的二值图像分割方法，通过学习时间相关向量场将图像分布转换为掩码分布，并引入位置感知实例配对训练策略实现文本条件控制，在DIS-TE测试集上相比最优方法提升5.5%的Fβω指标并降低43%的MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值图像分割方法难以保留细粒度细节，且无法充分捕捉前景的语义结构，亟需一种能同时兼顾精细细节与语义结构的高精度分割方法。

Method: 基于流匹配框架，学习时间相关向量场将图像分布传输到对应掩码分布；提出位置感知实例配对训练策略，实现文本提示引导的精确像素级分割。

Result: 在DIS-TE测试集上，FlowDIS相比最优方法Fβω指标提升5.5%，MAE降低43%。

Conclusion: FlowDIS通过流匹配框架和文本条件控制，在二值图像分割任务上显著超越现有方法，证明了该框架的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Accurate image segmentation is essential for modern computer vision applications such as image editing, autonomous driving, and medical image analysis. In recent years, Dichotomous Image Segmentation (DIS) has become a standard task for training and evaluating highly accurate segmentation models. Existing DIS approaches often fail to preserve fine-grained details or fully capture the semantic structure of the foreground. To address these challenges, we present FlowDIS, a novel dichotomous image segmentation method built on the flow matching framework, which learns a time-dependent vector field to transport the image distribution to the corresponding mask distribution, optionally conditioned on a text prompt. Moreover, with our Position-Aware Instance Pairing (PAIP) training strategy, FlowDIS offers strong controllability through text prompts, enabling precise, pixel-level object segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches both with and without language guidance. Compared with the best prior DIS method, FlowDIS achieves a 5.5% higher $F_β^ω$ measure and 43% lower MAE ($\mathcal{M}$) on the DIS-TE test set. The code is available at: https://github.com/Picsart-AI-Research/FlowDIS

</details>


### [88] [A unified Benchmark for Multi-Frame Image Restoration under Severe Refractive Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05079)
*Maxim V. Shugaev,Md Reshad Ul Hoque,Bridget Kennedy,Joseph T. Riley,Fiona Hwang,Justin Hagen,Harvir Ghuman,Ethan Garcia-O'Donnell,Syed Noor Qadri,Freddie Santiago,Mun Wai Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对折射动态介质（如湍流空气或水面）引起的视频几何畸变去除的全面基准测试，涵盖从轻度湍流到强不连续折射变形，包含实验室真实数据和基于物理光折射建模的合成序列，评估了多种方法（从经典配准到基于学习的DATUM和扩散模型V-cache），提供了首个大规模几何畸变去除分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对轻度大气湍流，缺乏系统性评估强且高度非均匀折射条件下视频几何畸变恢复的基准。

Method: 构建了涵盖4种畸变等级和多种表面波类型的实验室真实数据与物理仿真合成数据，评估从简单基线、经典配准到DATUM及新提出的扩散模型V-cache等多种方法，采用像素级（PSNR, SSIM）和感知级（LPIPS, DINO, CLIP）指标。

Result: 提供了首个大规模几何畸变去除分析，确立了从高度畸变光学环境中重建视频的新基准。

Conclusion: 该基准为开发和评估从高度畸变光学环境中恢复视频的算法奠定了基础，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Video sequence capturing through refractive dynamic media, such as a turbulent air or water surface, often suffer from severe geometric distortions and temporal instability. While recent advances address mild atmospheric turbulence, no existing benchmarks systematically evaluate restoration methods under strong and highly nonuniform refractive conditions. We present a comprehensive benchmark for geometric distortion removal in video, covering a range from turbulence-like mild warping to strong discontinuous refractive deformations. The benchmark includes both laboratory-captured real data and synthetic sequences generated for static scenes via physics-based light refraction modeling across four distortion levels and multiple surface wave types. We evaluate a spectrum of methods from simple baselines and classical registration algorithms to advanced learning-based approaches including DATUM and our proposed diffusion based V-cache for high and extreme distortions regimes. Evaluation uses both pixel-level (PSNR, SSIM), and perceptual (LPIPS, DINO, CLIP) metrics providing the first large scale analysis of geometric distortion removal. Our benchmark establishes a new foundation for developing and evaluating algorithms capable of reconstructing video from highly distorted optical environments. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/iafoss/refractive-mfir-benchmark.

</details>


### [89] [PhysForge: Generating Physics-Grounded 3D Assets for Interactive Virtual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05163)
*Yunhan Yang,Chunshi Wang,Junliang Ye,Yang Li,Zanxin Chen,Zehuan Huang,Yao Mu,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForge是一个解耦的两阶段框架，结合大规模物理标注数据集PhysDB，通过VLM规划层次化物理蓝图，再由物理扩散模型生成功能合理、可直接用于仿真的3D交互资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D资产生成方法主要关注静态几何，忽略了交互所必需的功能属性，导致生成的资产无法用于物理仿真和具身AI。

Method: 首先利用VLM作为"物理架构师"规划层次化物理蓝图（包含材料、功能、运动学约束），然后通过物理扩散模型（含KineVoxel Injection机制）生成高保真几何与精确运动学参数。框架基于含15万资产、四层物理标注的PhysDB数据集。

Result: PhysForge能生成功能合理、可直接用于仿真的3D资产，可作为交互式3D内容和具身智能体的数据引擎。

Conclusion: 将物理功能逻辑和层次化先验引入3D资产生成是可行的，解耦的两阶段框架（物理规划+物理扩散）能有效弥合静态几何生成与交互功能需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Synthesizing physics-grounded 3D assets is a critical bottleneck for interactive virtual worlds and embodied AI. Existing methods predominantly focus on static geometry, overlooking the functional properties essential for interaction. We propose that interactive asset generation must be rooted in functional logic and hierarchical physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysForge, a decoupled two-stage framework supported by PhysDB, a large-scale dataset of 150,000 assets with four-tier physical annotations. First, a VLM acts as a "physical architect" to plan a "Hierarchical Physical Blueprint" defining material, functional, and kinematic constraints. Second, a physics-grounded diffusion model realizes this blueprint by synthesizing high-fidelity geometry alongside precise kinematic parameters via a novel KineVoxel Injection (KVI) mechanism. Experiments demonstrate that PhysForge produces functionally plausible, simulation-ready assets, providing a robust data engine for interactive 3D content and embodied agents.

</details>


### [90] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Holistic Quality Assessment for 4D World Model (PhyScore)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05187)
*Wei Luo,Yiting Lu,Xin Li,Haoran Li,Fengbin Guan,Chen Gao,Xin Jin,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen,Sijing Wu,Kang Fu,Yunhao Li,Ziang Xiao,Huiyu Duan,Jing Liu,Qiang Hu,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai,Manxi Sun,Zixuan Guo,Yun Li,Ziyang Chen,Manabu Tsukada,Zhengyang Li,Zhenglin Du,Yi Wen,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Lingling Li,Yiwen Ren,Zhilong Song,Dubing Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Tianyi Yan,Huan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛，旨在评估世界模型生成视频的整体质量，包含质量、物理真实性、条件对齐和时间一致性四个维度，共1554个视频，涉及7个生成模型、3个赛道和26个类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估实践中仅依靠感知质量不足以判断生成动态的物理合理性、时间一致性和条件一致性，需要一个更全面的评估指标。

Method: 参赛者需构建联合预测视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频对齐和时间一致性四个维度的指标，并定位物理异常时间戳。数据集包含1554个由7个世界模型生成的视频，涵盖文本-2D、图像-4D和视频-4D三个赛道、26个类别，包含动力学、光学和热力学等物理场景。通过训练有素的人工标注和自动质量控制生成标签。

Result: 采用分数预测和异常定位组合评估协议，结合TimeStamp_IOU和SRCC/PLCC指标。

Conclusion: 本文总结了挑战赛设计，并提供了参赛方案的方法级洞见。

Abstract: This paper reports on the LoViF 2026 PhyScore challenge, a competition on holistic quality assessment of world-model-generated videos across both 2D and 4D generation settings. The challenge is motivated by a central gap in current evaluation practice: perceptual quality alone is insufficient to judge whether generated dynamics are physically plausible, temporally coherent, and consistent with input conditions. Participants are required to build a metric that jointly predicts four dimensions, i.e., Video Quality, Physical Realism, Condition-Video Alignment, and Temporal Consistency. Depart from that, participants also need to localize physical anomaly timestamps for fine-grained diagnosis.
  The benchmark dataset contains 1,554 videos generated by seven representative world generative models, organized into three tracks (text-2D, image-to-4D, and video-to-4D) and spanning 26 categories. These categories explicitly cover physics-relevant scenarios, including dynamics, optics, and thermodynamics, together with diverse real-world and creative content. To ensure label reliability, scores and anomaly timestamps are produced through trained human annotation with an additional automated quality-control pass.
  Evaluation is based on both score prediction and anomaly localization, with a composite protocol that combines TimeStamp_IOU and SRCC/PLCC. This report summarizes the challenge design and provides method-level insights from submitted solutions.

</details>


### [91] [D-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continuously Tuning Step-Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05204)
*Dengyang Jiang,Xin Jin,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Ruoyi Du,Xiangpeng Yang,Qilong Wu,Zhen Li,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对少步扩散模型在微调过程中会丧失其固有少步推理能力的问题，提出D-OPSD训练范式，通过利用LLM/VLM编码器的上下文能力，实现模型在自身轨迹上的在线策略自蒸馏学习，在不牺牲少步生成能力的前提下学习新概念和风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高效少步图像生成模型（如Z-Image-Turbo、FLUX.2-klein）在直接进行连续监督微调时会严重损害其固有的少步推理能力，亟需一种能保持少步生成能力的新型微调方法。

Method: 提出D-OPSD（On-Policy Self-Distillation）范式：利用现代扩散模型中LLM/VLM编码器的上下文能力，将训练构建为在线策略自蒸馏过程。训练时模型同时充当教师和学生，学生仅以文本特征为条件，教师以文本提示和目标图像的多模态特征为条件，最小化两者在学生自身轨迹上的预测分布差异。

Result: D-OPSD使模型能够在自身轨迹上进行优化并在自身监督下学习，成功习得新概念、新风格等，同时不牺牲原有的少步生成能力。

Conclusion: D-OPSD为少步扩散模型提供了一种有效的在线策略自蒸馏微调范式，解决了少步模型在微调时能力退化的问题，且不依赖外部教师模型，具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: The landscape of high-performance image generation models is currently shifting from the inefficient multi-step ones to the efficient few-step counterparts (e.g, Z-Image-Turbo and FLUX.2-klein). However, these models present significant challenges for directly continuous supervised fine-tuning. For example, applying the commonly used fine-tuning technique would compromises their inherent few-step inference capability. To address this, we propose D-OPSD, a novel training paradigm for step-distilled diffusion models that enables on-policy learning during supervised fine-tuning. We first find that the modern diffusion model where the LLM/VLM serves as the encoder can inherit its encoder's in-context capabilities. This enables us to make the training as an on-policy self-distillation process. Specifically, during training, we make the model acts as both the teacher and the student with different contexts, where the student is conditioned only on the text feature, while the teacher is conditioned on the multimodal feature of both the text prompt and the target image. Training minimizes the two predicted distributions over the student's own roll-outs. By optimized on the model's own trajectory and under it's own supervision, D-OPSD enables the model to learn new concept, style, etc. without sacrificing the original few-step capacity.

</details>


### [92] [Taming Outlier Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05206)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Tsu-Jui Fu,Liang-Chieh Chen,Zhe Gan,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中的离群token现象，发现预训练ViT编码器和DiT去噪器均会产生高范数离群token，且简单掩码无效。为此提出双阶段寄存器（DSR）干预方法，在ImageNet和文本到图像生成中有效减少离群伪影并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明Vision Transformer（ViT）中存在少量高范数离群token，它们吸引过多注意力但携带有限局部信息。然而，这一现象在生成模型（尤其是DiT）中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Dual-Stage Registers（DSR）方法，包含两阶段干预：1）编码器端：使用训练好的寄存器（可用时）或递归测试时寄存器；2）去噪器端：引入扩散寄存器。通过同时在RAE-DiT管线的编码器和去噪器中注册额外的寄存器token，来吸收和抑制离群值。

Result: 在ImageNet和大规模文本到图像生成任务上，DSR方法一致地减少了离群伪影，并提升了生成质量。消融实验表明，离群token问题不仅源于少数极端值，更与局部patch语义被破坏相关。

Conclusion: 离群token控制是构建更强DiT模型的关键组成部分，DSR方法为生成式Transformer中的token异常问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study outlier tokens in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation. Prior work has shown that Vision Transformers (ViTs) can produce a small number of high-norm tokens that attract disproportionate attention while carrying limited local information, but their role in generative models remains underexplored. We show that this phenomenon appears in both the encoder and denoiser of modern Representation Autoencoder (RAE)-DiT pipelines: pretrained ViT encoders can produce outlier representations, and DiTs themselves can develop internal outlier tokens, especially in intermediate layers. Moreover, simply masking high-norm tokens does not improve performance, indicating that the problem is not only caused by a few extreme values, but is more closely related to corrupted local patch semantics. To address this issue, we introduce Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based intervention for both components: trained registers when available, recursive test-time registers otherwise, and diffusion registers for the denoiser. Across ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image generation, these interventions consistently reduce outlier artifacts and improve generation quality. Our results highlight outlier-token control as an important ingredient in building stronger DiTs.

</details>


### [93] [Syn4D: A Multiview Synthetic 4D Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05207)
*Zeren Jiang,Yushi Lan,Yihang Luo,Yufan Deng,Zihang Lai,Edgar Sucar,Christian Rupprecht,Iro Laina,Diane Larlus,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Syn4D是一个多视角合成的动态场景数据集，提供密集的几何标注（深度、跟踪、相机运动、人体姿态），可支持任意像素在任意时间/相机间的3D反投影，实验证明其在多个动态场景理解任务中具有显著效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目视频进行动态场景的密集3D重建与跟踪因缺乏高质量、密集且几何标注准确的数据集而受到限制。

Method: 构建了一个包含真实相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪和参数化人体姿态标注的多视角合成动态场景数据集Syn4D，支持将任意像素反投影到任意时间点和任意相机的3D空间。

Result: 在4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向和人体姿态估计等多个下游任务上的广泛评估验证了Syn4D数据集的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: Syn4D数据集能够推动动态场景理解与时空建模领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction and tracking of dynamic scenes from monocular video remains an important open challenge in computer vision. Progress in this area has been constrained by the scarcity of high-quality datasets with dense, complete, and accurate geometric annotations. To address this limitation, we introduce Syn4D, a multiview synthetic dataset of dynamic scenes that includes ground-truth camera motion, depth maps, dense tracking, and parametric human pose annotations. A key feature of Syn4D is the ability to unproject any pixel into 3D to any time and to any camera. We conduct extensive evaluations across multiple downstream tasks to demonstrate the utility and effectiveness of the proposed dataset, including 4D scene reconstruction, 3D point tracking, geometry-aware camera retargeting, and human pose estimation. The experimental results highlight Syn4D's potential to facilitate research in dynamic scene understanding and spatiotemporal modeling.

</details>


### [94] [What Matters in Practical Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05148)
*Kedar Tatwawadi,Parisa Rahimzadeh,Zhanghao Sun,Zhiqi Chen,Ziyun Yang,Sanjay Nair,Divija Hasteer,Oren Rippel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种兼顾感知质量与运行效率的实用型学习图像编解码器，通过消融研究、神经架构搜索等技术优化，在主观测试中比传统编码器节省2.3-3x码率，比最佳学习编码器节省20-40%码率，且在iPhone 17 Pro Max上编码12MP图像仅需230ms、解码150ms，速度超越运行在V100 GPU上的多数ML编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型编解码器具有直接针对人眼视觉系统进行优化的潜力，但目前尚未有一种既具备感知质量又实用的学习图像编解码器被提出，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 对影响实用学习图像编解码器设计的关键建模选择（感知质量与运行效率联合优化）进行全面研究，消融实验中包含若干新技术；2) 对数百万个主干配置进行性能感知的神经架构搜索，以识别在目标设备上实现目标运行时间的同时最大化感知指标下压缩性能的模型；3) 将各种优化组合构建新编解码器。

Result: 主观用户研究表明：相比AV1、AV2、VVC、ECM和JPEG-AI节省2.3-3x码率；相比最佳学习编解码器节省20-40%码率。在iPhone 17 Pro Max上，编码12MP图像仅需230ms，解码150ms，速度快于运行在V100 GPU上的多数ML编解码器。

Conclusion: 通过系统性优化和神经架构搜索，成功构建了在感知质量与运行速度之间取得显著更优权衡的实用学习图像编解码器，推动了学习编解码器在实际部署中的可行性。

Abstract: One of the major differentiators unlocked by learned codecs relative to their hard-coded traditional counterparts is their ability to be optimized directly to appeal to the human visual system. Despite this potential, a perceptual yet practical image codec is yet to be proposed.
  In this work, we aim to close this gap. We conduct a comprehensive study of the key modeling choices that govern the design of a practical learned image codec, jointly optimized for perceptual quality and runtime -- including within the ablations several novel techniques. We then perform performance-aware neural architecture search over millions of backbone configurations to identify models that achieve the target on-device runtime while maximizing compression performance as captured by perceptual metrics.
  We combine the various optimizations to construct a new codec that achieves a significantly improved tradeoff between speed and perceptual quality. Based on rigorous subjective user studies, it provides 2.3-3x bitrate savings against AV1, AV2, VVC, ECM and JPEG-AI, and 20-40% bitrate savings against the best learned codec alternatives. At the same time, on an iPhone 17 Pro Max, it encodes 12MP images as fast as 230ms, and decodes them in 150ms -- faster than most top ML-based codecs run on a V100 GPU.

</details>


### [95] [Aes3D: Aesthetic Assessment in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05155)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Haoxian Zhou,Xuanhua Yin,Zihan Deng,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Aes3D是首个针对3D神经渲染场景的美学评估框架，包含首个3D场景美学数据集Aesthetic3D和轻量级模型Aes3DGSNet，可直接从3D高斯原语预测场景级美学分数，无需渲染多视图图像，在保持轻量设计的同时实现了强性能，为3D场景美学评估建立了新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景评估方法主要关注重建保真度和感知真实性，忽略了构图、和谐度、视觉吸引力等更高层次的美学属性。此外，缺乏带美学标注的3DGS数据集，且3DGS作为低级原语表示难以捕捉高级美学特征。

Method: 提出Aes3D框架，包括：(1) Aesthetic3D数据集——首个专用于3D场景美学评估的数据集，配有专门的美学标注策略；(2) Aes3DGSNet——轻量级模型，直接对3D高斯原语进行操作，通过多视图3DGS场景表示上的美学监督学习，捕捉高级美学线索并回归美学分数，无需渲染多视图图像。

Result: 该方法在保持轻量设计的同时实现了强性能，为3D场景美学评估建立了新基准。

Conclusion: Aes3D是首个系统性解决3D神经渲染场景美学评估问题的框架，填补了该领域的空白，通过专用数据集和轻量级模型有效捕捉高级美学特征。

Abstract: As 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) gains attention in immersive media and digital content creation, assessing the aesthetics of 3D scenes becomes important in helping creators build more visually compelling 3D content. However, existing evaluation methods for 3D scenes primarily emphasize reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism, largely overlooking higher-level aesthetic attributes such as composition, harmony, and visual appeal. This limitation comes from two key challenges: (1) the absence of general 3DGS datasets with aesthetic annotations, and (2) the intrinsic nature of 3DGS as a low-level primitive representation, which makes it difficult to capture high-level aesthetic features. To address these challenges, we propose Aes3D, the first systematic framework for assessing the aesthetics of 3D neural rendering scenes. Aes3D includes Aesthetic3D, the first dataset dedicated to 3D scene aesthetic assessment, built on our proposed annotation strategy for 3D scene aesthetics. In addition, we present Aes3DGSNet, a lightweight model that directly predicts scene-level aesthetic scores from 3DGS representations. Notably, our model operates solely on 3D Gaussian primitives, eliminating the need for rendering multi-view images and thus reducing computational cost and hardware requirements. Through aesthetics-supervised learning on multi-view 3DGS scene representations, Aes3DGSNet effectively captures high-level aesthetic cues and accurately regresses aesthetic scores. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance while maintaining a lightweight design, establishing a new benchmark for 3D scene aesthetic assessment. Code and datasets will be made available in a future version.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Membership Inference Attacks for Retrieval Based In-Context Learning for Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04116)
*Tejas Kulkarni,Antti Koskela,Laith Zumot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了即便服务提供商与用户为独立第三方，基于检索增强的上下文学习型远程托管应用仍面临成员推断攻击风险。作者提出两种黑盒攻击方法：第一种利用参考模型估算损失；第二种通过加权平均方案消除参考模型依赖。实验表明，攻击在查询文本经改写后仍具鲁棒性，且在小样本前缀场景下优于三类基线方法。此外，现有的集成提示防御策略可有效缓解第二种攻击带来的隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究远程托管的、采用上下文学习并搭配检索函数选取上下文示例的应用是否易受成员推断攻击，尤其是在服务提供商与用户为独立实体的情况下。

Method: 提出两种黑盒成员推断攻击：(1) 基于参考模型估计损失度量进行成员推断；(2) 通过新颖的加权平均方案计算成员统计量，消除对参考模型的依赖。实验设置中考虑攻击者仅持有查询文本的改写版本这一更严格场景。

Result: 两种攻击在查询经改写后仍表现出强鲁棒性，在少量前缀设置下多项指标优于三种现有攻击方法。同时，集成提示防御可显著减轻第二种攻击的隐私泄露。

Conclusion: 检索增强的上下文学习应用存在成员推断攻击风险，但可通过集成提示等防御手段缓解；加权平均方案为无参考模型的黑盒攻击提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We show that remotely hosted applications employing in-context learning when augmented with a retrieval function to select in-context examples can be vulnerable to membership-inference attacks even when the service provider and users are separate parties. We propose two black-box membership inference attacks that exploit query text prefixes to distinguish member from non-member inputs. The first attack uses a reference model to estimate an otherwise unavailable loss metric. The second attack improves upon it by eliminating the reference model and instead computing a membership statistic through a simple but novel weighted-averaging scheme. Our comprehensive empirical evaluations consider a stricter case in which the adversary has a paraphrased version of the text in the queries and show that our attacks can exhibit stronger resilience to paraphrasing and outperform three prior attacks in many cases with small number of prefixes. We also adapt an existing ensemble prompting defense to our setting, demonstrating that it substantially mitigates the privacy leakage caused by our second attack.

</details>


### [97] [Towards a Zero-Trust Supply-Chain Assurance Rubric for ORAN RIC Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04249)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对O-RAN RIC应用提出了一套零信任供应链保障框架，包括应用生命周期威胁模型、威胁-控制-证据映射关系，以及基于安全实践、SBOM透明度和SLSA溯源的分级准入配置文件，支持在应用接入时做出接受、升级或阻断决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放RAN允许第三方xApps/rApps以运营节奏快速接入和更新，形成了涵盖开发者、CI系统、注册表、接入管道和运行时执行点的软件供应链，亟需针对性的安全保障框架。

Method: 首先构建了RIC应用全生命周期（构建、签名、发布、接入、运行时、更新/回滚）的威胁模型；其次建立了与WG11对齐的威胁-控制-证据映射；最后设计了结合安全开发实践、SBOM透明度和SLSA溯源的分级接入准入配置文件。

Result: 通过分析案例研究和最小化证据检查工作流，展示了该框架如何支持RIC应用接入时的显式接受、升级或阻断决策。评估侧重于适用性而非部署性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为O-RAN RIC应用的供应链安全提供了系统化的零信任保障方案，但运营开销、决策一致性和检测覆盖率等实证测量留待未来工作。

Abstract: Open RAN enables third-party xApps and rApps to be onboarded and updated at operational cadence, creating a software supply chain that spans developers, CI systems, registries, onboarding pipelines, and runtime enforcement points. This preprint proposes a zero-trust supply-chain assurance rubric for O-RAN RIC applications. It makes three contributions: first, an app-centric lifecycle threat model for RIC applications across build, signing, publication, onboarding, runtime, and update or rollback stages; second, a WG11-aligned threat-control-evidence mapping that relates lifecycle threats to O-RAN security baselines and complementary supply-chain evidence; and third, an operator-facing assurance profile that combines secure software development practices, SBOM transparency, and SLSA-style provenance into incremental onboarding levels. Analytical case-study walkthroughs and a minimal evidence-checking workflow illustrate how the rubric can support explicit Accept, Escalate, or Block decisions during RIC app onboarding. The evaluation is intended to assess applicability rather than deployment-scale performance; empirical measurements of operational overhead, decision consistency, and detection coverage are left for future work.

</details>


### [98] [Binary Image-Based Intrusion Detection for Operational Technology Networks: Extending the SPHBI Methodology from IoT to Modbus TCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04250)
*Aamir Omar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将SPHBI入侵检测方法从IoT扩展到Modbus TCP协议，在CIC Modbus 2023数据集上评估了五种协议深度方案。仅使用TCP/IP头部准确率仅51.8%，增加8字节应用层信息后二分类准确率提升至98.1%（仅63个参数），最佳方法多分类准确率达94.4%，参数数量仅为ResNet50的1/430。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SPHBI方法从IoT领域扩展到Modbus TCP协议，验证其在资源受限的OT边缘设备上进行逐包分类的可行性，解决SCADA环境中TCP/IP头部缺乏异构性的问题。

Method: 在CIC Modbus 2023数据集上评估五种不同协议深度的SPHBI方法，从仅TCP/IP头部到包含应用层信息，使用轻量级模型进行二分类和多分类任务，并通过10次随机种子重复实验评估稳定性。

Result: TCP/IP头部仅达51.8%二分类准确率；增加8字节应用层信息后二分类准确率98.1%（63参数）；最佳多分类准确率94.4%±2.2pp，56,873参数（约为ResNet50的1/430）；7/8攻击类型召回率>94%，重放攻击单包方法不可检测。

Conclusion: SPHBI方法在Modbus TCP场景中有效，轻量级模型（63参数）即可实现高准确率，适合资源受限边缘设备；但重放攻击因其结构特性无法通过单包检测识别。

Abstract: This paper extends the Single Packet Header Binary Image (SPHBI) intrusion detection methodology from IoT to Modbus TCP, evaluating five approaches spanning a gradient of protocol depth on the CIC Modbus 2023 dataset (11.4 million packets, eight detectable attack types). TCP/IP headers alone achieve only 51.8% binary accuracy, confirming that header-level heterogeneity exploited in IoT traffic is absent in uniform SCADA environments. Adding eight bytes of application-layer information improves binary accuracy to 98.1% with just 63 parameters, directly relevant to per-packet classification on resource-constrained OT edge devices. The best-performing approach achieves 94.4% +/- 2.2pp multiclass accuracy across nine classes (95% CI [92.9%, 95.9%], 10 seeds) with 56,873 parameters, roughly 430 times fewer than comparable ResNet50-based approaches. Per-class recall analysis shows seven of eight detectable attack types identified with recall above 94%, while replay attacks remain structurally undetectable by any single-packet method.

</details>


### [99] [Root-Cause-Driven Automated Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04251)
*Hulin Wang,Zion Leonahenahe Basque,Jie Hu,Ati Priya Bajaj,Yibo Liu,Samuel Zhu,Giorgi Kobakhia,Nikhil Chapre,Will Rosenberg,Siddharth Mishra,Aditya Maheshbhai Gabani,Moritz Schloegel,Adam Doupé,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Ruoyu Wang,Tiffany Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Kumushi——一种基于根因驱动的漏洞修复智能体，结合多样化动态故障定位与证据加权排序，引导LLM关注缺陷核心代码；同时引入两层级补丁质量评估指标（自动化验证+专家评估）。在178个C/C++漏洞上的实验表明，Kumushi显著优于先前专用修复智能体，性能接近前沿商业编码智能体，且专家评估确认其产生更多根因修复、更少表面修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动漏洞修复面临两个关键挑战：1) 缺乏明确的缺陷位置信号，导致修复智能体倾向于产生治标不治本的浅层编辑；2) 根因定位本身困难，即使是熟悉代码库的开发者也会经常修复症状而非根因，LLM智能体因上下文噪声和程序理解不足更易如此。

Method: 1) 多样化动态故障定位（diversified dynamic fault localization）：多角度定位缺陷位置；2) 证据加权排序（evidence-weighted ranking）：将LLM的注意力聚焦于与缺陷最相关的代码；3) 两层级补丁质量评估指标（two-tier patch quality metric）：自动化Oracle验证 + 结构化专家评估。

Result: 在178个C/C++漏洞上，Kumushi在自动化评估下大幅超越先前专用修复智能体，效果接近前沿商业编码智能体。专家评估进一步揭示了Oracle无法区分的差异：Kumushi产生的根因修复更多、表面修补更少，在大多数决定性成对比较中被专家优先选择。

Conclusion: 自动化漏洞修复的进步不仅需要更强的修复系统，还需要更丰富的评估方法来区分真正的修复与仅通过Oracle验证的修复。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based systems have made automated vulnerability repair increasingly practical, but two challenges remain. First, without strong signals about where a bug originates, repair agents drift toward shallow edits that silence the observed failure while leaving the underlying defect unresolved. Second, finding the root cause for bugs is hard: even developers familiar with the codebase frequently produce fixes that address symptoms rather than the root cause, and LLM-based agents, operating with noisier context and less program understanding, are no exception. We present Kumushi, a root-cause-driven patching agent that addresses both challenges by combining diversified dynamic fault localization with evidence-weighted ranking to focus the LLM on the code most relevant to the defect. To rigorously measure whether Kumushi produces genuinely better patches, we also introduce a two-tier patch quality metric that pairs automated oracle validation with structured expert assessment of patches. Evaluated on 178 C/C++ vulnerabilities, Kumushi substantially outperforms prior specialized repair agents under automated evaluation while matching a frontier commercial coding agent. Expert assessment then reveals differences that oracles cannot: Kumushi produces more root-cause fixes and fewer superficial patches, and is preferred in the majority of decisive pairwise comparisons. Together, these results demonstrate that progress in automated vulnerability repair requires not only stronger patching systems, but also richer evaluation methods capable of distinguishing genuine fixes from oracle-passing ones.

</details>


### [100] [Lightweight Vulnerability Detection from Code Metrics and Token Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04260)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种轻量级C/C++函数级漏洞检测流水线，仅使用稀疏token n-gram和简单代码度量（如NLOC、圈复杂度等），结合TF-IDF和加权逻辑回归，避免深度学习与程序图。在Devign数据集上，随机划分PR-AUC达0.642，Recall@10%为0.161，但跨项目泛化较差（PR-AUC约0.436），表明该方法作为透明基线有效，但对表面词汇线索敏感且跨项目迁移能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有C/C++漏洞检测依赖代码图与深度模型，复杂度高且可复现性差，而实际工作流仍需要快速、可复现的排序基线以支持人工分类。本文旨在探索一种无需深度学习、程序图的轻量级函数级漏洞分类流水线，作为透明且高效的基线方案。

Method: 使用原始函数文本中的稀疏token n-gram特征（TF-IDF）结合5种简单代码度量（NLOC、近似圈复杂度、token数、最大花括号深度、参数数量），采用类别加权逻辑回归分类器。在Devign数据集上进行随机划分和跨项目（FFmpeg→QEMU）评估，使用PR-AUC和Recall@10%作为面向排序的评估指标。

Result: 随机划分下，最佳组合变体达到PR-AUC 0.642、Recall@10% 0.161；跨项目泛化难度显著增加，PR-AUC约为0.436。消融实验、标识符重命名鲁棒性测试和端到端效率分析均被报告。

Conclusion: 简单token和度量特征可作为有用的透明基线，但模型对表层词汇线索敏感，且跨项目迁移能力有限。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection for C/C++ code increasingly relies on heavy representations such as code graphs and deep models, while many practical workflows still benefit from fast and reproducible ranking baselines for human triage. This preprint studies a lightweight function-level vulnerability triage pipeline that combines sparse token n-grams from raw function text with a small set of inexpensive code metrics, including NLOC, approximate cyclomatic complexity, token count, maximum brace depth, and parameter count. We use TF-IDF token features and a class-weighted logistic regression classifier, avoiding deep learning, transformers, and program graphs.
  Using the Devign function-level labels, we evaluate random and cross-project settings, including a FFmpeg-to-QEMU transfer experiment. We emphasize precision-recall AUC and Recall@10% as ranking-oriented metrics for skewed or triage-oriented workloads. On the random split, the best combined variant reaches PR-AUC 0.642 and Recall@10% 0.161, while cross-project generalization is substantially harder, with PR-AUC around 0.436. We further report ablations, test-only identifier-renaming robustness, and end-to-end efficiency. The results suggest that simple token and metric features provide a useful transparent baseline, but also expose sensitivity to superficial lexical cues and limited cross-project transfer.

</details>


### [101] [Laundering AI Authority with Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04261)
*Jie Zhang,Pura Peetathawatchai,Florian Tramèr,Avital Shafran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉语言模型（VLM）存在一种新型安全漏洞——AI权威洗钱（AI authority laundering）：攻击者通过微小的对抗性扰动，使VLM对错误输入产生自信且权威的回应。该攻击不破坏模型对齐，而是完全在感知层面操作。标准对抗攻击（如对CLIP的攻击）可迁移至GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6等生产级VLM。在身份操纵、NSFW规避等场景下，攻击成功率高达22-100%。所用技术是十多年前已知的基本方法，但足以构成实际威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被广泛部署为可信权威（事实核查、内容审核、产品推荐等），用户默认VLM能像人类一样感知视觉内容。然而，对抗性攻击可打破这一假设。现有研究多关注越狱（jailbreak）或提示注入（prompt injection），但这些攻击破坏模型对齐（alignment）。作者希望展示一种在感知层面操纵VLM的新型攻击，既不破坏对齐，又能产生严重后果。

Method: 使用标准对抗攻击方法（如PGD等已知超过十年的技术），对公开可用的CLIP模型生成对抗性扰动，然后将扰动后的图像输入生产级VLM（GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3, Grok 4.2）。攻击覆盖四个攻击面：放大虚假信息、贬低个人、规避内容审核、操纵产品推荐。攻击类型包括身份操纵（将A识别为B）和NSFW内容规避等。

Result: 在六种模型上，身份操纵和NSFW规避攻击的成功率为22-100%。标准攻击从CLIP迁移到生产级VLM的效果可靠。无需新颖的攻击算法——十年前已知的基本方法即可实现高成功率。

Conclusion: 视觉对抗鲁棒性是一个实际且尚未解决的安全问题。攻击者能力下限（基本方法）已足以构成威胁，防御者应引起重视。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as trusted authorities -- fact-checking images on social media, comparing products, and moderating content. Users implicitly trust that these systems perceive the same visual content as they do. We show that adversarial examples break this assumption, enabling \emph{AI authority laundering}: an attacker subtly perturbs an image so that the VLM produces confident and authoritative responses about the \emph{wrong} input. Unlike jailbreaks or prompt injections, our attacks do not compromise model alignment; the attack operates entirely at the perceptual level. We demonstrate that standard attacks against publicly available CLIP models transfer reliably to production VLMs -- including GPT-5.4, Claude Opus~4.6, Gemini~3, and Grok~4.2. Across four attack surfaces, we show that authority laundering can amplify misinformation, disparage individuals, evade content moderation, and manipulate product recommendations. Our attacks have high success rates: In hundreds of attacks targeting identity manipulation and NSFW evasion, we measure success rates of $22 - 100\%$ across six models. No novel attack algorithm is required: basic techniques known for over a decade suffice, establishing a lower bound on attacker capability that should concern defenders. Our results demonstrate that visual adversarial robustness is now a practical -- and still largely unsolved -- safety problem.

</details>


### [102] [Revocation-Ready CP-ABE Key Management for Blockchain-Based IoT Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04280)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CP-ABE的撤销就绪密钥管理层，通过密文密钥发布替代在线密钥释放，结合epoch时间绑定和轻量级CK轮换协议，实现前向撤销和策略演化，无需重加密大文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链IoT数据共享系统在密钥访问控制上存在瓶颈：依赖在线RBAC或智能合约门返回密钥，引入可信在线策略执行点，削弱可审计性。

Method: 将在线密钥释放替换为密文密钥发布：分类账本记录(CID, CK, PolicyID, epoch)元数据，CK为CP-ABE密文封装AES-GCM密钥；引入epoch时间绑定属性和轻量级CK轮换协议支持撤销和策略演化。

Result: CP-ABE加密主导存储延迟（k=6混合布尔策略约186ms），账本和存储操作约1-2ms；epoch撤销摊销密钥更新成本；网关辅助模式将客户端解密中位数时间降低4倍以上；账本增长与共享资产数而非读者数成比例。

Conclusion: 该密钥管理层在不重加密大文件的前提下实现了前向撤销与策略演化，有效解耦了密钥管理开销，适用于混合架构区块链IoT数据共享系统。

Abstract: Blockchain-based IoT data sharing systems increasingly adopt a hybrid architecture in which a permissioned ledger stores tamper-evident metadata while encrypted payloads are placed in content-addressed storage. In such systems, a central security bottleneck is key access control: enforcing dynamic, multi-user authorization for releasing or using bulk-data decryption keys. Existing designs often rely on always-online RBAC or smart-contract gates that return keys to authorized users, reintroducing a trusted online policy enforcement point and weakening auditability. This paper presents a revocation-ready key management layer that replaces online key release with ciphertext key publication: the ledger records metadata of the form (CID, CK, PolicyID, epoch), where CK is a CP-ABE ciphertext encapsulating an AES-GCM key. Users retrieve CK from the ledger and decrypt locally if their attributes satisfy the policy.
  To support forward revocation and policy evolution without re-encrypting large files, the design introduces an epoch/time-bound attribute and a lightweight CK-rotation protocol that updates only small ciphertext keys and ledger entries. We implement a minimal end-to-end prototype using a local content-addressed store, a hash-chained ledger, and a CP-ABE backend, with the goal of isolating key-management costs rather than benchmarking production blockchain throughput. Experiments on a commodity MacBook show that CP-ABE encryption dominates store latency, with approximately 186 ms for a k=6 mixed-Boolean policy, while ledger and storage operations remain around 1-2 ms. Epoch-based revocation amortizes key update cost under churn, gateway-assisted mode reduces median client-side decryption time by more than 4x under a simulated 4x client slow-down, and ledger growth scales with the number of shared assets rather than the number of readers.

</details>


### [103] [Misrouter: Exploiting Routing Mechanisms for Input-Only Attacks on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04446)
*Zekun Fei,Zihao Wang,Weijie Liu,Ruiqi He,Jianing Geng,Zheli Liu,XiaoFeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Misrouter是一种针对MoE架构大语言模型的输入级攻击框架，通过在开源替代模型上白盒优化对抗性输入，然后迁移到同系列公开API服务，从而操纵路由机制将输入导向弱对齐专家，绕过安全对齐产生有害输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构通过路由机制引入新的攻击面，现有攻击需要修改模型（仅适用于本地部署模型），而真实LLM服务是远程托管的，只能通过输入查询访问。因此需要探索能否仅通过输入攻击利用MoE路由机制诱导更强的有害行为。

Method: 提出Misrouter框架：1) 通过分析有害查询配对不安全续写下的专家激活，识别弱对齐专家；2) 优化对抗输入将路由导向弱对齐专家并远离强对齐专家；3) 将路由偏向从良性问答任务中识别的高能力通用专家；4) 采用两阶段优化策略，先稳定路由再优化有害输出。

Result: 成功将对抗性输入从开源替代模型迁移到同系列公开API服务，证明仅通过输入扰动即可间接影响路由并绕过安全对齐产生有害内容。

Conclusion: MoE模型的路由机制在仅输入攻击下存在安全漏洞，即使模型远程托管无法修改，攻击者仍可通过对抗性输入操纵路由绕过安全对齐。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a leading paradigm for scaling large language models through sparse, routing-based computation. However, this design introduces a new attack surface: the routing mechanism that determines which experts process each input. Prior work shows that manipulating routing can bypass safety alignment, but existing attacks require model modification and thus apply only to locally deployed models. By contrast, real-world LLM services are remotely hosted and accessible only through input queries. This raises a fundamental question: can MoE routing be exploited through input-only attacks to induce stronger unsafe behaviors in real-world services? Our key insight is to optimize attacks in a white-box setting on open-source surrogate MoE models and transfer the resulting adversarial inputs to public API services within the same model family. This setting presents three main challenges: routing can be influenced only indirectly through input perturbations, routing control and output generation are tightly coupled, and even a successful safety bypass may still produce low-quality responses. To address these challenges, we propose Misrouter, an input-only attack framework that jointly targets routing behavior and expert functionality. Misrouter identifies weakly aligned experts that are willing to produce target harmful content by analyzing expert activations under harmful queries paired with unsafe continuations. It then optimizes adversarial inputs to steer routing toward these experts and away from strongly aligned ones. It further biases routing toward highly capable general-purpose experts identified from benign question-answering tasks. Finally, because routing and output objectives can conflict, Misrouter uses a two-phase optimization strategy that first steers routing and then optimizes harmful outputs while preserving routing stability.

</details>


### [104] [PINSIGHT: A Comprehensive Threat Exploration of Domain-Adaptive Wi-Fi based PIN Code Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04570)
*Johannes Kortz,Paul Staat,Christof Paar,Christian Zenger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PINSIGHT方法，利用机器人打字平台系统性地评估Wi-Fi PIN码推断攻击的泛化能力，发现现有攻击在环境变化下表现可靠，但在信道编码（打字本身）发生变化时性能显著下降，表明当前SOTA攻击的实际威胁被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wi-Fi PIN码推断攻击（如WiKI-Eve）声称在不同环境、设备和用户下均具有高精度，但其实际泛化性能和真实威胁尚未被严格评估，存在理解空白。

Method: 提出PINSIGHT方法论，将环境变化与PIN码打字效应分离；使用机器人打字平台产生高可重复性击键事件，并在系统化环境变化下采集数据集，作为首个环境泛化基准测试集。

Result: 攻击在周围环境变化下泛化可靠，但在信道对打字本身的编码发生变化时性能大幅下降——而这正是真实攻击场景的关键条件。

Conclusion: 当前SOTA Wi-Fi PIN码推断攻击的报告性能不能代表实际真实威胁，其泛化能力存在严重局限。

Abstract: Wi-Fi signals can be exploited by adversaries as a sensing side channel to eavesdrop on physical information. By monitoring propagation effects of radio waves within the victim's environment, attackers can remotely infer sensitive information. One particularly concerning example is PIN code inference, where the attacker faces the challenge of mapping Wi-Fi physical-layer channel estimations back into typed digits. While effective in their training environment, such attacks typically fail as soon as they are deployed in unseen environments. The current state-of-the-art attack, WiKI-Eve, attempts to overcome this problem using a deep-learning approach, reporting high PIN code inference accuracy independent of environments, devices, and users. While this suggests a significant real-world threat, it is not well understood how far the attack actually reaches, nor what its underlying generalization performance is based on. In this work, we close this gap by presenting PINSIGHT, a novel methodology that separates the effects of environmental variation and PIN code typing. This enables the first rigorous threat assessment of such attacks, evaluating their generalization capabilities and limitations. Our approach leverages a robotic typing platform that produces highly repeatable keystroke events across systematically varied environment changes [...]. This dataset constitutes the first benchmark for environment generalization in Wi-Fi PIN code inference attacks. Evaluating several state-of-the-art methods, we find that attacks generalize reliably across changes in the surrounding environment but degrade substantially when the channel's encoding of typing itself shifts - precisely the condition that defines a realistic attack scenario. We conclude that the reported performance of current state-of-the-art Wi-Fi PIN inference attacks is not representative of the actual real-world threat.

</details>


### [105] [Securing the Web with HSTS-Enforced](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04642)
*Aaron van Diepen,Adrian Zapletal,Fernando Kuipers*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HSTS-Enforced机制，将TLS剥离防护从当前的选择加入（opt-in）模型转变为选择退出（opt-out）模型，即所有连接默认使用HTTPS，网站运营者可显式选择退出以支持HTTP，从而消除TLS剥离攻击面，同时保持向后兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TLS剥离防护机制（如HSTS头部）存在配置困难、兼容性问题、易被滥用导致用户追踪等局限性，且采用选择加入的安全模型，覆盖面不足。

Method: 提出HSTS-Enforced机制，将安全模型从选择加入翻转为选择退出：默认所有连接强制HTTPS，运营者可通过两种HTTP-Required指示器（新型DNS记录和HTTP-Required预加载列表）显式声明网站需要使用HTTP。

Result: 在多种部署场景下的评估表明，HSTS-Enforced能阻断所有实际的TLS剥离攻击，同时保持对需要HTTP的站点的兼容性，且不引入典型情况下的额外开销。

Conclusion: HSTS-Enforced消除了TLS剥离的剩余攻击面，并提出了加速全球采用的实用过渡路径。

Abstract: TLS stripping attacks expose sensitive web traffic by forcing secure HTTPS connections to fall back to unencrypted HTTP. At present, protection against these attacks relies on website operators explicitly opting into security by deploying mechanisms such as HTTP Strict Transport Security (HSTS) headers. These mechanisms have significant limitations: some are weak or difficult to configure, which raises the risk of misconfiguration and reduces practical adoption; others violate HTTP backward compatibility; at least one can even be abused to enable unintended user tracking.
  We introduce HSTS-Enforced, a mechanism that eliminates the remaining attack surface for TLS stripping while still allowing operators to securely specify that their websites need to be accessed over HTTP when necessary, thereby maintaining accessibility. To achieve this, we flip the current opt-in security model to an opt-out model: all connections default to HTTPS, and operators can explicitly opt out if their websites require HTTP using so-called HTTP-Required indicators. We propose two such HTTP-Required indicators: a new DNS record and an HTTP-Required Preload list. We evaluate HSTS-Enforced under multiple deployment scenarios, demonstrating that it blocks all practical TLS stripping attempts while maintaining compatibility for sites that require HTTP - without introducing overhead in the typical case. Finally, we outline a practical transition path to accelerate global adoption.

</details>


### [106] [Gray-Box Poisoning of Continuous Malware Ingestion Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04698)
*Jan Dolejš,Martin Jureček,Róbert Lórencz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向持续学习型恶意软件检测管道的灰盒投毒攻击，利用功能保持的IAT和节区注入操作生成对抗性二进制样本，并评估其对LightGBM模型的影响。实验表明，基于IAT的微小扰动可以有效降低检测召回率。同时，一种同质集成防御机制能够识别并过滤高达95.6%的投毒尝试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代恶意软件检测管道依赖持续数据摄入和机器学习来应对大量新型威胁，但现有的对抗性攻击研究多关注逃避攻击而非投毒攻击，且缺乏对实际生产环境中灰盒威胁模型的探讨。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究现实场景下针对持续学习管道的投毒攻击及其防御。

Method: 使用secml_malware框架，通过功能保持的IAT（导入地址表）注入和节区注入操作生成问题空间的对抗性二进制样本，模拟灰盒投毒攻击场景。将这些有毒样本注入到基于LightGBM的恶意软件检测模型的训练集中，评估其对模型召回率的影响，并进一步采用同质集成方法进行防御验证。

Result: 基于IAT的微小扰动能够生成紧凑的投毒样本，显著降低检测模型的召回率；同质集成防御机制可成功识别并过滤最多95.6%的投毒尝试，同时保持对合法数据的高保留率。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，在持续学习系统中开发低可见性且高效能的对抗性扰动是一项固有挑战，强调了在生产管道中实施鲁棒的摄入前验证机制的必要性。

Abstract: Modern malware detection pipelines rely on continuous data ingestion and machine learning to counter the high volume of novel threats. This work investigates a realistic gray-box poisoning threat model targeting these pipelines. Using the secml_malware framework, we generate problem-space adversarial binaries through functionality-preserving manipulations, specifically Import Address Table (IAT) and section injections. We evaluate the impact of these poisoned samples when ingested into a defender's training set for a LightGBM malware detection model. Our empirical results demonstrate that subtle IAT-based perturbations enable compact poisoning samples that significantly degrade detection recall. These findings illustrate the inherent challenge of developing low-visibility adversarial perturbations that maintain high poisoning efficacy within continuous learning systems. We further evaluate a defense mechanism based on a homogeneous ensemble, which successfully identifies and filters up to 95.6% of poisoning attempts while maintaining a high retention rate for legitimate data. These findings emphasize the necessity of robust pre-ingestion validation in production pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [Sparse Tokens Suffice: Jailbreaking Audio Language Models via Token-Aware Gradient Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04700)
*Zheng Fang,Xiaosen Wang,Shenyi Zhang,Shaokang Wang,Zhijin Ge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现音频语言模型的越狱攻击中，梯度能量在音频token上高度不均匀分布，仅少数token主导优化信号；据此提出Token-Aware Gradient Optimization (TAGO)方法，通过保留高梯度能量token对应的波形梯度、屏蔽其余梯度实现稀疏优化，在三种ALM上达到与全token更新相当的攻击成功率（例如Qwen3-Omni上token保留率0.25时ASR_l仍达86%），证明密集波形更新存在大量冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究音频语言模型越狱攻击中密集波形优化的必要性，分析token级梯度结构，发现梯度能量分布不均匀，从而提出更高效的稀疏优化方法。

Method: 提出Token-Aware Gradient Optimization (TAGO)，在每次迭代中仅保留高梯度能量音频token对应的波形梯度，屏蔽其余梯度，实现稀疏越狱优化。

Result: 在三种ALM上，TAGO优于基线方法；即使token保留率降至0.25，仍能保持高攻击成功率（如Qwen3-Omni上ASR_l为86%，接近全保留时的87%）。

Conclusion: 密集波形更新存在大量冗余，未来的音频越狱与安全对齐研究应进一步利用这种异质的token级梯度结构。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on audio language models (ALMs) optimize audio perturbations to elicit unsafe generations, and they typically update the entire waveform densely throughout optimization. In this work, we investigate the necessity of such dense optimization by analyzing the structure of token-aligned gradients in ALMs. We find that gradient energy is highly non-uniform across audio tokens, indicating that only a small subset of token-aligned audio regions dominates the optimization signal. Motivated by this observation, we propose Token-Aware Gradient Optimization (TAGO), which enables sparse jailbreak optimization by retaining only waveform gradients aligned with audio tokens that have high gradient energy, while masking the remaining gradients at each iteration. Across three ALMs, TAGO outperforms baselines, and substantial sparsification preserves strong attack success rates (e.g. on Qwen3-Omni, $\mathrm{ASR}_{l}$ remains at 86% with a token retention ratio of 0.25, compared to 87% with full token retention). These results demonstrate that dense waveform updates are largely redundant, and we advocate that future audio jailbreak and safety alignment research should further leverage this heterogeneous token-level gradient structure.

</details>


### [108] [Vol-Mark: A Watermark for 3D Medical Volume Data Via Cubic Difference Expansion and Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04705)
*Jiangnan Zhu,Yuntao Wang,Shengli Pan,Yujie Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vol-Mark，一种面向医学体数据的可逆零水印方法，通过对比学习提取鲁棒体特征和立方体差值扩展(c-DE)嵌入水印，实现低失真、无损移除的完整性与所有权双重验证，在多种攻击场景下准确率均超过0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程医疗中医学体数据共享面临数据篡改和未授权复制的安全风险，现有水印方法在鲁棒性和数据完整性保护方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 基于对比学习的体数据特征提取器，提取判别性强且稳定的体特征；2) 立方体差值扩展(c-DE)技术，利用3D整数小波变换在低频系数邻域体素间嵌入水印位，通过差值扩展创建嵌入空间，提取时采用多数投票机制增强可靠性。

Result: 在常规、几何和混合等多种攻击场景下，Vol-Mark的ACC一致超过0.90，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Vol-Mark实现了低失真、无损移除的完整性验证和所有权验证，有效保障远程医疗中医学体数据的安全与诊断精度。

Abstract: Today, advances in medical technology extensively utilize 3D volume data for accurate and efficient diagnostics. However, sharing these data across networks in telemedicine poses significant security risks of data tampering and unauthorized copying. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel reversible-zero watermarking approach, termed Vol-Mark, for medical volume data to protect their ownership and authenticity in telemedicine. The proposed Vol-Mark method offers two key benefits: 1) it designs a volume data feature extractor that leverages contrastive learning to efficiently extract discriminative and stable volumetric features, ensuring robustness against 3D attacks; 2) it introduces the cubic difference expansion (c-DE) technique, which leverages the 3D integer wavelet transform to embed watermark bits into neighboring voxels within cubes at low-frequency coefficients. The voxel differences within each cube are expanded to create embedding space, and a majority voting mechanism is employed during extraction to enhance reliability. The embedding process incurs low distortion and supports lossless removal, thereby preserving the integrity and diagnostic accuracy of medical volume data. Through these two benefits, Vol-Mark enables both integrity verification and ownership verification. Integrity verification is first performed, and ownership verification through hypothesis testing is further conducted to enhance reliability, particularly under data tampering or watermark removal attacks. Comprehensive experimental results show the effectiveness of the proposed method and its superior robustness against conventional, geometric, and hybrid attacks on medical volume data. In particular, through multiple tasks evaluations, Vol-Mark consistently achieves an ACC above 0.90 in most attack scenarios, outperforming existing methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [109] [AFL-ICP: Enhancing Industrial Control Protocol Reliability via Specification-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04760)
*Jiaying Meng,Xuewei Feng,Qi Li,Min Liu,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AFL-ICP，一种基于规范驱动的自动化模糊测试框架，通过上下文感知的规范形式化流水线、LLM 驱动的协议适配与种子生成、以及 LLM 支持的差分检查器，在四种工业控制协议中发现了 24 个未知漏洞（含 16 个语义/逻辑漏洞），并获得了厂商确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制协议（ICP）的安全性因规范盲区瓶颈而严重受损，现有模糊测试器受限于观测驱动的推理，难以深入协议状态或检测细微语义偏差。

Method: 提出规范驱动范式 AFL-ICP，包含三个核心组件：(1) 上下文感知的规范形式化流水线，将复杂规范转化为机器可执行的语法规则；(2) LLM 驱动的协议自适应与种子生成，实现快速协议扩展；(3) LLM 驱动的差分检查器，交叉比对实现输出与规范要求以检测语义和逻辑缺陷。

Result: 在四种广泛使用的 ICP（含开源和闭源变体）上评估，AFL-ICP 在覆盖率上显著优于现有最先进模糊测试器，发现了 24 个未知漏洞并获厂商致谢（如 FreyrSCADA），其中包括 16 个可静默破坏工业操作并降低服务可用性的语义和逻辑缺陷。

Conclusion: 规范驱动的自动化模糊测试框架 AFL-ICP 有效克服了传统模糊测试的规范性盲区问题，显著提升了工业控制协议的漏洞检测能力，尤其擅长发现传统方法无法检测的语义和逻辑缺陷。

Abstract: Industrial Control Protocols (ICPs) are critical to the reliability and stability of industrial infrastructure, yet their security is fundamentally compromised by a specification-blindness bottleneck. Modern fuzzers, constrained by observation-driven inference, struggle to penetrate deep protocol states or detect subtle semantic deviations. In this paper, we present AFL-ICP, an autonomous fuzzing framework that pioneers a specification-driven paradigm. AFL-ICP features a context-aware specification formalization pipeline to transform complex specifications into rigorous machine-executable grammars. Building on this formalized specification, AFL-ICP leverages LLMs to enable automated protocol adaptation and seed generation, allowing for rapid extension to new protocols with minimal manual effort. Additionally, it includes an LLM-powered differential checker that cross-references implementation outputs with specification requirements to detect subtle semantic and logic bugs that existing fuzzers cannot detect. We implement AFL-ICP and evaluate it on four widely used ICPs, including both open-source and closed-source variants. Results show that AFL-ICP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art fuzzers in coverage and uncovers 24 previously unknown vulnerabilities, for which we have received acknowledgments from affected vendors (e.g., FreyrSCADA). Specifically, the identified vulnerabilities include 16 semantic and logic bugs that can silently disrupt industrial operations and degrade service availability.

</details>


### [110] [Long-Term Risks of IoT Devices: The Case of the Smart Fridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04787)
*Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化方法识别智能冰箱的长期运行风险，发现即使最基本的制冷功能也可能因IT生态依赖而面临风险，但未发现"威胁性"危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统设备替换为智能设备虽带来便利，但其IT组件（软硬件、库、协议、生态）寿命远短于传统家电（如冰箱、电视），导致智能设备长期运行存在风险，这一问题亟待系统性研究。

Method: 采用系统化方法：识别三种典型智能冰箱的8种用例（如制冷、保质期管理）→建模所需IT生态系统→逐一检查生态中各资产的长期风险。

Result: 即使最基础的制冷用例也存在长期风险，因为制冷参数设置可能依赖用户无法控制的IT生态组件；但未发现任何属于"威胁性"类别的风险。

Conclusion: 智能冰箱的长期风险可推广至其他智能设备，提醒智能设备设计需考虑IT组件与传统设备的寿命差异及其带来的依赖风险。

Abstract: Replacing conventional devices with smart ones has many advantages, e.g., a seamless integration of physical objects into the users digital environment or improved modes of use. However, if a conventional device is replaced by a smart device, its IT components can cause risks, that shorten the life of the device.
