Such risks stem from different life cycles of embedded soft- and hardware, libraries and protocols used, and the IT ecosystem required. This is problematic, because many conventional household appliances, say, a fridge or TV, have a much longer life span than typical IT equipment. In this paper, we use a systematic approach to identify long-term risks for the operational life span of a smart fridge. In particular, we identify 8 different use cases of three typical smart fridges, e.g., cooling or managing "best before" dates. We model the IT ecosystem needed to run these use cases, and we inspect each asset in this ecosystem for potential long-term risks. We found that even cooling, the most basic use case, is at risk in the long run. This is because the setting cooling parameters may depend on parts of the IT ecosystem that are not under the users control. On the other hand, we did not find any risk that may lead to harm of the category "threatening". Our findings on the smart fridge can be generalized to other smart devices easily.

</details>


### [111] [On the (In-)Security of the Shuffling Defense in the Transformer Secure Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04901)
*Zhengyi Li,Yakai Wang,Kang Yang,Yu Yu,Jiaping Gui,Yu Feng,Ning Liu,Minyi Guo,Jingwen Leng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对Transformer模型安全推理中shuffling防御机制的攻击方法，能够以极低的成本（约1美元）恢复模型权重，证明现有防御并不鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型的安全推理中，非线性层的计算是效率瓶颈。现有方案通过暴露中间激活值给客户端来提升效率，但为防范权重提取攻击采用了shuffling防御。本文旨在证明该防御并不如先前声称的那样安全。

Method: 提出一种对齐攻击，将不同随机排列的激活值对齐到同一排列，进而利用对齐后的激活值提取模型权重。

Result: 在Pythia-70m和GPT-2上，对齐后的均方误差在$10^{-9}$到$10^{-6}$之间；以约1美元的查询成本，恢复的权重与真实权重的L1范数差异在$10^{-4}$到$10^{-2}$之间。

Conclusion: Shuffling防御机制不够鲁棒，攻击者可以低成本高效地提取模型权重，亟需更安全的防御方案。

Abstract: For Transformer models, cryptographically secure inference ensures that the client learns only the final output, while the server learns nothing about the client's input. However, securely computing nonlinear layers remains a major efficiency bottleneck due to the substantial communication rounds and data transmission required. To address this issue, prior works reveal intermediate activations to the client, allowing nonlinear operations to be computed in plaintext. Although this approach significantly improves efficiency, exposing activations enables adversaries to extract model weights. To mitigate this risk, existing works employ a shuffling defense that reveals only randomly permuted activations to the client. In this work, we show that the shuffling defense is not as robust as previously claimed. We propose an attack that aligns differently shuffled activations to a common permutation and subsequently exploits them to extract model weights. Experiments on Pythia-70m and GPT-2 demonstrate that the proposed attack can align shuffled activations with mean squared errors ranging from $10^{-9}$ to $10^{-6}$. With a query cost of approximately \$1, the adversary can recover model weights with L1-norm differences ranging from $10^{-4}$ to $10^{-2}$ compared to the oracle weights.

</details>


### [112] [You Snooze, You Lose: Automatic Safety Alignment Restoration through Neural Weight Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04992)
*Marco Arazzi,Vignesh Kumar Kembu,Antonino Nocera,Stjepan Picek,Saraga Sakthidharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NeWTral框架，通过神经权重翻译技术将不安全的领域特化LoRA适配器映射到安全对齐流形上，在保持90%领域知识保真度的同时将攻击成功率从70%降至13%，无需原始训练数据或重训练即可恢复安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态中第三方LoRA适配器在提供专业领域知识的同时，会破坏基础模型的安全对齐；而用安全数据微调恢复对齐又会严重退化适配器的专业知识。现有方法无法在不牺牲任一目标的前提下解决这一零资源挑战。

Method: 提出NeWTral框架，核心是一个预训练在大量不安全到安全适配器对上的非线性翻译模块。该模块在参数空间中执行映射，采用自适应混合专家（MoE）路由策略，融合高保真局部翻译专家和激进对齐专家。

Result: 在Llama、Mistral、Qwen、Gemma四大架构家族（最高72B参数）的8个科学和专业领域上，MoE变体将平均攻击成功率从70%降至13%，同时保持90%的平均知识保真度。

Conclusion: NeWTral以独立可下载模块形式存在，无需原始训练数据或硬件密集型重训练即可即时恢复安全对齐，为开源LLM生态的安全-专业性兼顾提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The open-source ecosystem has accelerated the democratization of Large Language Models (LLMs) through the public distribution of specialized Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules. However, integrating these third-party adapters often induces catastrophic forgetting of the base model's foundational safety alignment. Restoring these guardrails via fine-tuning on safety data introduces an opposing failure mode: the severe degradation of the specialized domain knowledge the adapter was originally designed to provide. To overcome this zero-resource challenge, we propose Neural Weight Translation (NeWTral), a framework that directly maps unsafe, domain-specific adapters onto a safe alignment manifold while rigorously preserving their core expertise. NeWTral operates as a non-linear translation module pre-trained on a diverse corpus of unsafe-to-safe adapter pairs. By executing this mapping entirely within the parameter space, NeWTral utilizes an adaptive Mixture of Experts (MoE) routing strategy to autonomously blend high-fidelity surgical translators and aggressive alignment experts. We evaluate our framework across four architectural families (Llama, Mistral, Qwen, and Gemma) at scales up to 72B parameters across eight diverse scientific and professional domains. Our results demonstrate that the MoE variant achieves a radical reduction in the average Attack Success Rate (ASR), dropping from 70% in unsafe experts to just 13%, while maintaining an exceptional 90\% average knowledge fidelity. Much like the crowdsourced adapters it remedies, the NeWTral module is designed as a standalone, downloadable asset that allows practitioners to restore safety alignment instantly without requiring access to original training data or hardware-intensive retraining.

</details>


### [113] [SoK: Robustness in Large Language Models against Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05058)
*Feiyue Xu,Hongsheng Hu,Chaoxiang He,Sheng Hang,Hanqing Hu,Xiuming Liu,Yubo Zhao,Zhengyan Zhou,Bin Benjamin Zhu,Shi-Feng Sun,Dawu Gu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Security Cube，一个用于全面评估大语言模型越狱攻击与防御的统一多维框架，并基于该框架对13种攻击和5种防御进行了基准研究，提炼了关键发现与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM越狱攻击与防御的评估方法不充分，常仅依赖攻击成功率等单一指标，无法全面反映LLM安全的多维特性，亟需系统化、多维度的评估框架。

Method: 提出了Security Cube统一多维评估框架，构建了越狱攻击与防御的系统性分类体系，并基于该框架对13种代表性攻击和5种防御方法进行了基准实验。

Result: 提供了详细的攻击与防御对比表，揭示了当前越狱攻击、防御、自动评判器以及LLM漏洞的整体现状，并提炼出关键发现与开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为增强LLM越狱鲁棒性指明了研究方向，有助于构建更鲁棒、可解释和可信赖的LLM系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success but remain highly susceptible to jailbreak attacks, in which adversarial prompts coerce models into generating harmful, unethical, or policy-violating outputs. Such attacks pose real-world risks, eroding safety, trust, and regulatory compliance in high-stakes applications. Although a variety of attack and defense methods have been proposed, existing evaluation practices are inadequate, often relying on narrow metrics like attack success rate that fail to capture the multidimensional nature of LLM security. In this paper, we present a systematic taxonomy of jailbreak attacks and defenses and introduce Security Cube, a unified, multi-dimensional framework for comprehensive evaluation of these techniques. We provide detailed comparison tables of existing attacks and defenses, highlighting key insights and open challenges across the literature. Leveraging Security Cube, we conduct benchmark studies on 13 representative attacks and 5 defenses, establishing a clear view of the current landscape encompassing jailbreak attacks, defenses, automated judges, and LLM vulnerabilities. Based on these evaluations, we distill critical findings, identify unresolved problems, and outline promising research directions for enhancing LLM robustness against jailbreak attacks. Our analysis aims to pave the way towards more robust, interpretable, and trustworthy LLM systems. Our code is available at Code.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Perturbation is All You Need for Extrapolating Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04344)
*Zetai Cen,Jin Zhu,Xinwei Shen,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扰动的语言模型训练框架，通过将前缀转换为语义邻居再进行下一词预测，建立层次化预加噪声结构，并严格证明了该方法的可外推性理论，实验表明该方法在保持域内性能的同时显著提升域外预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归语言模型基于精确前缀进行下一词预测，导致模型对训练语料分布外的序列缺乏泛化能力（即外推性不足）。作者旨在通过引入前缀扰动机制，使模型学会在语义近似的上下文中进行预测，从而提升对分布外序列的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出"扰动-预测"框架：先将输入前缀通过语义变换（如加噪）转化为其语义邻居，再以该扰动后的变体为条件进行下一词预测。该过程形成层次化模型，具有预加性噪声结构（pre-post-additive noise）。理论部分证明了模型类的外推能力边界，实验部分在合成数据和真实语言数据上验证了有效性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实语言数据上，所提方法持续优于标准自回归模型在域外预测的性能，同时域内预测性能保持竞争力。实验证明了扰动策略作为语言模型实用路线的可行性。

Conclusion: 扰动训练能够在不牺牲域内性能的前提下显著提升语言模型的域外泛化能力，为构建更具鲁棒性和外推性的语言模型提供了理论依据和实用框架。

Abstract: We introduce a simple yet powerful framework for training large language models. In contrast to the standard autoregressive next-token prediction based on an exact prefix, we propose a perturbation-based procedure that first transforms the prefix into a semantic neighbor and then conditions on this perturbed variant for next-token prediction. This yields a hierarchical model with a pre-post-additive noise structure. Within this framework, we develop a rigorous theory of extrapolability, namely, the capacity of a model class to make reliable predictions for token sequences that lie outside the empirical support of the training corpus. We evaluate the finite-sample performance of the proposed procedure using both synthetic and real-world language data. Results show that the proposed method consistently improves out-of-support prediction while maintaining competitive in-support performance, demonstrating that perturbation offers a practical route to language modeling.

</details>


### [115] [A Consistency-Centric Approach to Set-Based Optimization with Multiple Models of Unranked Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04051)
*Danielle F. Morey,Giulia Pedrielli,Cherry Y. Wakayama,Zelda B. Zabinsky*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为S-BOMM的基于集合的优化方法，它能在不假设存在最精确高保真模型的情况下，利用多个模型之间的一致性来识别优质解，并提供了概率分析保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实优化问题中常存在多种不同保真度的模型，但传统方法通常预设一个最精确的高保真模型，而实际应用中模型精度往往未知，这种假设可能导致误导性结果。

Method: 提出S-BOMM（基于集合的多模型优化）方法，利用多个模型之间的一致性而非依赖单一高保真模型来识别优质解，并对一致性方法进行了概率分析，界定了方法产生正确或错误结果的可能性。

Result: 在测试问题上展示了S-BOMM的有效性，验证了不依赖单一最精确模型、转而利用模型间一致性的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: S-BOMM为需要同时考虑多个模型且无法假设单一最精确高保真模型的优化问题提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Abstract: In complex real-world settings, optimization is challenged by the presence of diverse models of differing fidelity. In many optimization problems, a single model is treated as the most accurate representation of the underlying system, while other models are evaluated primarily by their agreement with this presumed most accurate model. Yet in real-world applications, model accuracy is rarely known a priori and assuming a single most accurate model can be misleading. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a flexible set-based optimization methodology called Set-Based Optimization with Multiple Models (S-BOMM) that works with multiple models without the assumption of a most accurate high-fidelity model. Unlike traditional optimization approaches that focus on finding an optimal solution according to the high-fidelity model, our methodology utilizes consistency between models to identify good solutions across multiple models. A probabilistic analysis of the consistency method is provided that bounds the likelihood of the methodology producing correct or incorrect results. Empirical results demonstrate the effectiveness of S-BOMM on test problems. By focusing on the consistency across models rather than relying on a single best solution, this set-based approach offers a practical alternative to optimization problems where multiple models must be considered without assuming a single most accurate high-fidelity model.

</details>


### [116] [Heterogeneous Ordinal Structure Learning with Bayesian Nonparametric Complexity Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04191)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种异质有序结构学习框架，结合单调高斯分数嵌入、贝叶斯非参数复杂度发现和确认性固定K估计，用于发现公众对AI态度的异质性DAG结构。在2024年Pew调查中，K*=5的确认性模型相比单图基线降低了25.8%的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么假设所有受访者共享单一DAG，要么只做子组发现而不做确认性估计，或者完全丢弃依赖结构。需要一种既能发现异质性又能进行确认性估计的框架。

Method: 提出发现-确认工作流：1) 单调高斯分数嵌入处理有序数据；2) 截断stick-breaking先验的BNP非参数阶段发现原型复杂度；3) 固定K确认性阶段通过内部验证进行稀疏DAG学习。

Result: 在Pew W152调查(N=4,788, 8个有序题项)中，K*=5的确认模型在保留集上MSE较单图基线降低25.8%，较纯混合聚类降低4.6%。半合成基准验证了恢复能力并揭示了失败模式。

Conclusion: 异质有序DAG学习框架能有效发现公众AI态度的潜在结构，确认性阶段显著提升模型性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Public attitudes toward artificial intelligence are heterogeneous, ordinally measured, and poorly captured by any single dependency graph. Existing ordinal structure learners assume a shared directed acyclic graph (DAG) across all respondents; recent heterogeneous ordinal graphical-model approaches focus on subgroup discovery rather than confirmatory cluster-specific DAG estimation; and latent profile analyses discard dependency structure entirely. We introduce a heterogeneous ordinal structure-learning framework combining monotone Gaussian score embedding, Bayesian nonparametric (BNP) complexity discovery via a truncated stick-breaking prior, and confirmatory fixed-K estimation with cluster-specific sparse DAG learning. The key methodological insight is a discovery-to-confirmation workflow: the nonparametric stage calibrates plausible archetype complexity, while inner-validated confirmatory refitting yields stable, interpretable structural estimates. On the 2024 Pew American Trends Panel AI attitudes survey, Wave 152 (W152) survey, (N = 4,788, 8 ordinal items), the confirmatory K*=5 model reduces holdout transformed-score mean squared error (MSE) by 25.8% over a single-graph baseline and by 4.6% over mixture-only clustering. A controlled tiered semi-synthetic benchmark calibrated to W152 structure validates recovery across difficulty regimes and transparently reveals failure modes under stress conditions.

</details>


### [117] [Adapt or Forget: Provable Tradeoffs Between Adam and SGD in Nonstationary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04269)
*Sharan Sahu,Abir Sarkar,Cameron J. Hogan,Martin T. Wells*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对Adam优化器在非平稳随机目标下的性能进行了理论分析，区分了两个分析框架：自适应强单调条件下的欧几里得跟踪和一般L-光滑条件下的高概率投影平稳性保证。推导了有限时间的期望和高概率界，揭示了初始化、目标漂移、一阶矩跟踪误差和预处理扰动四个分量，并刻画了到达不可约跟踪底限的预热时间。同时证明了分布偏移下平均投影平稳性差距的高概率界，揭示了噪声-漂移权衡：噪声主导时自适应步长有利，漂移主导时SGD更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam优化器虽然在深度学习中被广泛使用并表现出色，但其在非平稳目标（如分布偏移、概念漂移）下的理论性质尚不清楚，特别是其对比SGD的优势与劣势缺乏严格的理论刻画，且Adam在实际中表现出的不稳定性缺乏理论解释。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，将Adam的分析分为两个框架：(1) Adam预处理后均值梯度算子的自适应强单调性下的欧几里得跟踪分析；(2) 一般L-光滑条件下高概率投影平稳性保证。推导了显式的(β₁, β₂, ε)依赖的有限时间界。

Result: 推导了Adam在非平稳目标下的四个分量误差界，刻画了常数步长和逐步衰减策略下的预热时间。证明了噪声-漂移权衡：噪声主导时自适应预处理可提升高概率误差，漂移主导时陈旧的一阶矩和预处理扰动会加剧非平稳性代价，使SGD的跟踪底限更小。

Conclusion: 本文提供了Adam在非平稳环境下何时有益何时有害的显式理论条件，为Adam在分布偏移下的不稳定性和稳定化现象提供了理论机制解释。

Abstract: We provide a theoretical analysis of Adam under non-stationary stochastic objectives, separating two regimes: Euclidean tracking under adaptive strong monotonicity of the Adam-preconditioned mean-gradient operator, and high-probability projected stationarity guarantees under general $L$-smooth objectives. In the tracking regime, we derive finite-time expected and high-probability bounds that decompose sharply into four components: initialization, objective drift, a first-moment tracking error governed by $β_1$, and a preconditioner perturbation governed by $β_2$. We characterize the burn-in time to reach Adam's irreducible tracking floor under constant and step-decay schedules. We also prove a high-probability bound on the average projected stationarity gap for Adam under distribution shift. Across both analyses, our bounds reveal a noise--drift tradeoff: in noise-dominated regimes, first-moment averaging and adaptive preconditioning can improve the high-probability error, whereas in drift-dominated regimes, stale first-moment information and preconditioner perturbations can compound the cost of nonstationarity, allowing vanilla SGD to achieve a smaller tracking floor. Our explicit $(β_1,β_2,ε)$-dependent bounds delineate when adaptive step-sizing is beneficial versus harmful, and provide a theoretical mechanism for Adam's empirical instability and stabilization under distribution shift.

</details>


### [118] [Scalable inference of spatial regions and temporal signatures from time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05008)
*Jiayu Weng,Alec Kirkley*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最小描述长度原则的非参数化空间时间序列分区框架，能联合推断空间分区和代表性时间序列原型，时间复杂度为时间序列数量的对数线性，在合成数据和真实空气质量/植被指数数据中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间分区方法多依赖静态空间快照而忽略时间演化；时间序列聚类方法又常忽视空间结构或通过临时正则化强制空间连续性，且需要先验指定区域数量。

Method: 基于信息论的最小描述长度原则，提出一种高效且完全非参数化的空间时间序列分区框架，联合推断空间分区和一组代表性时间序列原型（"驱动者"），以最优压缩时空数据集，运行时间与时间序列数量呈对数线性关系。

Result: 在合成时间序列中准确恢复人工分区结构和驱动者；在真实空气质量指数和植被指数的大规模实证数据中提取到有意义的结构规律。

Conclusion: 该方法为空间连续分区提供了原则性且可扩展的框架，使可解释的时间模式和同质区域能够直接从数据中自发涌现。

Abstract: Regionalization aims to partition a spatial domain into contiguous regions that share similar characteristics, enabling more effective spatial analysis, policy making, and resource management. Existing approaches for spatial regionalization typically rely on static spatial snapshots rather than evolving time series. Meanwhile, most time series clustering methods ignore spatial structure or enforce spatial continuity through ad hoc regularization, constraining the number of inferred regions a priori either explicitly or implicitly. Utilizing the minimum description length principle from information theory, here we propose an efficient and fully nonparametric framework for the regionalization of spatial time series. Our method jointly infers a spatial partition along with a set of representative time series archetypes ("drivers") that best compress a spatiotemporal dataset, with a runtime log-linear in the number of time series. We demonstrate that this method can accurately recover planted regional structure and drivers in synthetic time series, and can extract meaningful structural regularities in large-scale empirical air quality and vegetation index records. Our method provides a principled and scalable framework for spatially contiguous partitioning, allowing interpretable temporal patterns and homogeneous regions to emerge directly from the data itself.

</details>


### [119] [Hypergraph Generation via Structured Stochastic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05024)
*Christopher Nemeth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HEDGE，一种基于结构化随机扩散的超图生成模型，直接在松弛关联矩阵上操作，通过双侧重热算子与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck组件的前向过程实现结构感知加噪，利用线性高斯性质推导闭合形式的条件均值、协方差、分数和反向漂移目标，学习置换等变的状态反向漂移场生成样本，理论上证明了理想状态下的精确性和有限时域稳定性，实验表明生成质量优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图生成方法无法忠实捕获关联性、超边大小异质性和重叠结构，因为这些属性在成对约简中会丢失。

Method: 提出HEDGE生成模型，在松弛关联矩阵上定义结构化随机扩散，前向过程结合超图特定双侧重热算子与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck分量，利用线性高斯性质获得闭合形式的条件目标，学习置换等变的状态反向漂移场，通过模拟反向SDE生成样本。

Result: 理论上证明了理想状态下逆向过程精确恢复原始分布，并给出有限时域稳定性保证；实验上超图生成质量显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: HEDGE通过在关联空间直接建模并利用结构化扩散的线性高斯可解性，有效解决了超图生成中关联结构保持的挑战，兼具理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: Hypergraphs model higher-order interactions, but realistic hypergraph generation remains difficult because incidence, hyperedge-size heterogeneity, and overlap structure are not faithfully captured by pairwise reductions. We propose \HEDGE, a generative model defined directly on relaxed incidence matrices via a structured stochastic diffusion. The forward process combines a hypergraph-specific two-sided heat operator with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck component, preserving structure-aware noising near the data while yielding an explicit Gaussian terminal law. Conditional on an observed hypergraph, this forward process is linear-Gaussian, so conditional means, covariances, scores, and reverse-drift targets are available in closed form. We therefore learn a permutation-equivariant state-only reverse-drift field in incidence space by regressing onto exact conditional targets, and generate samples by simulating a learned reverse-time SDE from the Gaussian base law. We establish exactness in the ideal state-only setting together with finite-horizon stability guarantees, and empirically show improved hypergraph generation quality relative to strong baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Proximal Projection for Doubly Sparse Regularized Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05093)
*Jia Wei He,R. Ayesha Ali,Gerarda Darlington*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高维回归场景下利用预测变量图结构进行正则化的方法。通过将系数向量分解为对应每个节点贡献的潜变量之和，并对潜变量而非系数直接施加弹性网惩罚，结合新型近端投影算子实现分组交集优化，在保持稳定性能的同时显著降低计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维回归中正则化常被用于生成稀疏模型以节省计算资源并识别关键预测变量。当预测变量可由高斯图模型表示时，现有方法未能充分利用预测变量图的结构信息来提升正则化效果。

Method: 将估计的系数向量分解为对应每个节点贡献的潜变量之和；对潜变量施以弹性网惩罚（允许用户在L1和L2惩罚间自定义权衡）；提出新型近端投影算子，在优化过程中计算选定组的交集投影；相比预测变量复制方法，该投影算子在高维数据下更节省计算资源。

Result: 通过在不同图结构和节点数量下的仿真实验以及在真实数据上的验证，该方法相较于其他单稀疏或双稀疏图回归模型表现出稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过利用预测变量图结构进行分解式正则化，结合高效的交集投影算子，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低计算开销，尤其适用于高维数据场景。

Abstract: Regularization is often used in high-dimensional regression settings to generate a sparse model, which can save tremendous computing resources and identify predictors that are most strongly associated with the response. When the predictors can be represented by a Gaussian graphical model, the structure of the predictor graph can be exploited during regularization. Our proposed model exploits this underlying predictor graph structure by decomposing the estimated coefficient vector into a sum of latent variables that correspond to the sum of each node contribution to the coefficient vector. Regularization is then performed on the latent variables rather than on the coefficient vector directly. We use a penalty function that permits a clear user-defined trade-off between the L1 and L2 penalties and propose a novel proximal projection during optimization. Further, our implementation computes the projection operator for the intersection of selected groups, which conserves more computing resources compared to predictor duplication methods, especially for high-dimensional data. Through simulation, we evaluate the performance of our approach under different graph structures and node counts, and present results on real-world data. Results suggest that our method exhibits stable performance relative to other singly or doubly sparse graphical regression models.

</details>


### [121] [Sharp Capacity Thresholds in Linear Associative Memory: From Winner-Take-All to Listwise Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05189)
*Nicholas Barnfield,Juno Kim,Eshaan Nichani,Jason D. Lee,Yue M. Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性记忆模型能存储的键值关联数量，发现该容量取决于检索准则。对于top-1检索，容量为$d^2\asymp n\log n$；对于基于尾部平均边际（TAM）的列表检索，容量为$d^2\asymp n$。作者通过变分原理给出了精确渐近理论，并推测了top-1的尖锐阈值$d^2\sim 2n\log n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究线性记忆能存储多少键值关联，并揭示容量不仅受限于记忆矩阵的自由度，还取决于检索准则。

Method: 采用各向同性高斯模型，分别针对top-1检索（胜者全得）和基于TAM的列表检索建立理论框架，通过变分原理和尾部外推进行分析。

Result: top-1检索下容量为$d^2\asymp n\log n$，列表检索下容量为$d^2\asymp n$；给出了TAM经验风险最小化器的精确渐近理论，预测了临界负荷、分数分布等。

Conclusion: 线性记忆的容量与检索准则密切相关，对数项是胜者全得解码的极端值代价，而列表检索可达到二次方容量尺度。

Abstract: How many key-value associations can a $d\times d$ linear memory store? We show that the answer depends not only on the $d^2$ degrees of freedom in the memory matrix, but also on the retrieval criterion. In an isotropic Gaussian model for the stored pairs, we show that top-1 retrieval, where every signal must beat its largest distractor, requires the logarithmic model-size scale $d^2\asymp n\log n$. We prove that the correlation matrix memory construction, which stores associations by superposing key-target outer products, achieves this scale through a sharp phase transition, and that the same scaling is necessary for any linear memory. Thus the logarithm is the intrinsic extreme-value price of winner-take-all decoding.
  We next consider listwise retrieval, where the correct target need not be the unique top-scoring item but should remain among the strongest candidates. To formalize this regime, we propose the Tail-Average Margin (TAM), a convex upper-tail criterion that certifies inclusion of the correct target in a controlled candidate list. Under this listwise retrieval criterion, the capacity follows the quadratic scale $d^2\asymp n$. At load $n/d^2\toα$, we develop an exact asymptotic theory for the TAM empirical-risk minimizer through a two-parameter scalar variational principle. The theory has a rich phenomenology: in the ridgeless limit it yields a closed-form critical load separating satisfiable and unsatisfiable phases, and it predicts the limiting laws of true scores, competitor scores, margins, and percentile profiles. Finally, a small-tail extrapolation further leads to the conjectural sharp top-1 threshold $d^2\sim 2n\log n$.

</details>

### [122] [Thinned Quantile Shares are Universally Feasible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04300)
*Vishesh Jain,Clayton Mizgerd,Shyam Ravichandran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种参数化的细化分位数份额——c-细化分位数份额，并证明了存在一个通用常数c>0使得c-细化的e^{-c}-分位数份额无条件普遍可行，这是最优的；同时基于彩虹EMC猜想，证明了原始(1/e)-分位数份额的普遍可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分位数份额作为不可分割物品公平分配的一种序数、自我最大化且可解释的基准，其普遍可行性仅依赖于彩虹EMC猜想（条件性结果），且存在一个简单的不可能界限（q>1/e时不可行）。作者希望去除这种条件性依赖，并缩小已知可行与不可行之间的差距。

Method: 引入c-细化分位数份额：通过一个固定常数c∈(0,1]对随机基准束中的包含概率进行"细化"（乘以因子c）。利用这种细化视角，重新分析份额的可行性边界，并结合组合概率方法证明无条件可行性。

Result: 存在通用常数c>0使得c-细化的e^{-c}-分位数份额无条件普遍可行，且对任意c∈(0,1]，c-细化的q-分位数份额在q>e^{-c}时不可行，达到最优。此外，基于彩虹EMC猜想，原始(1/e)-分位数份额是普遍可行的（去除了之前结果中的因子2损失）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在无条件情况下证明了除Feige剩余最大最小份额之外的第二个非平凡普遍可行份额；细化视角还改进了对原始分位数份额的条件性结果。

Abstract: Quantile shares, introduced by Babichenko, Feldman, Holzman, and Narayan [STOC 2024], offer an ordinal, self-maximizing, and interpretable benchmark for fair division of indivisible goods, but their universal feasibility is known only conditional on the rainbow Erdős matching conjecture (EMC). Specifically, Babichenko et al. showed that assuming the rainbow EMC in the near-perfect matching regime, the $(1/2e)$-quantile share is universally feasible. In contrast, a simple argument shows that the $q$-quantile share can be infeasible for any $q > 1/e$. We introduce a one-parameter refinement of quantile shares, the $c$-thinned quantile share, obtained by thinning the inclusion probability in the random benchmark bundle by a factor of $c$ for a fixed constant $c\in(0,1]$. Our main result is that there exists a universal constant $c >0$ for which the $c$-thinned $e^{-c}$-quantile share is unconditionally universally feasible; this is best possible in the sense that for any $c \in (0,1]$, the $c$-thinned $q$-quantile share can be infeasible for any $q > e^{-c}$. Prior to this work, the only nontrivial share known to be universally feasible was Feige's residual maximin share. The thinning viewpoint also lets us remove the factor-two loss in the conditional result for the original quantile share: assuming the rainbow EMC, the $(1/e)$-quantile share is universally feasible.

</details>


### [123] [The Threshold Breakdown Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04317)
*Tianjun Ke,Marco Avella Medina*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限样本稳健性的新方法，定义了阈值崩溃点和有限样本m-敏感性，推导了M-估计量及其标准误和检验统计量的这些度量，并建立了假设检验的决策崩溃点推广，同时提供了推断框架（包括一致性和渐近正态性）以及乘子自助法用于不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统崩溃点分析过于悲观，需要一种更精细的有限样本稳健性度量方法，能够刻画特定污染比例下估计量偏离程度，而非仅给出完全失效的临界点。

Method: 定义了阈值崩溃点（最小污染比例导致预设偏差）和有限样本m-敏感性（m个观测污染后最坏偏差）；推导了M-估计量、标准误和检验统计量的这些度量；扩展了Zhang (1996)的决策崩溃点至假设检验；建立了与He, Simpson和Portnoy (1990)的势和水平崩溃函数的有限样本对应；提出了阈值崩溃和m-敏感性的推断框架，包括一致性和渐近正态性，以及乘子自助法。

Result: 给出了常用M-估计量的阈值崩溃点和m-敏感性的显式表达式；建立了假设检验的通用崩溃特征；证明了推断框架的渐近性质；通过数值例子和血压数据集的二样本检验问题验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的有限样本稳健性度量避免了传统崩溃分析的过度悲观，为实践提供了更灵活的工具，且所开发的推断框架使得这些度量可以进行统计推断和不确定性量化。

Abstract: We introduce a novel approach to finite sample robustness that avoids the pessimism of traditional breakdown analyses. We define the threshold breakdown point, the smallest contamination fraction needed to induce a prescribed deviation, and the finite sample m-sensitivity, the worst-case deviation that an estimator can incur after m observations are contaminated. We derive these measures for commonly used M-estimators, their standard errors and related test statistics. This allows us to extend the decision breakdown point of Zhang (1996) to obtain general breakdown characterizations for hypothesis testing, and show how these notions correspond to finite sample counterparts of the power and level breakdown functions of He, Simpson and Portnoy (1990). We complement our work with an inferential framework for the threshold breakdown and m-sensitivity that yields consistency and asymptotic normality results, as well as a valid multiplier bootstrap for uncertainty quantification. We illustrate the practical utility of our methods in various numerical examples and an application to a two sample testing problem for a blood pressure dataset.

</details>


### [124] [Transversality and Geometric Regularisation in Distributional Statistical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04536)
*R. Labouriau*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出分布统计框架，通过分布-核偶对(T, φ)替代经典概率密度，证明核作为几何正则化器使参数统计模型相对于退化轨迹处于横截位置。利用Whitney、Thom和Mather的横截性定理，证明了有限维弱横截性定理，并验证了位置族、对数正态、Stein差异和图模型等场景的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计框架在处理非可识别性、奇异信息、矩不确定性和表示失效等问题时缺乏统一的几何工具。本文旨在建立分布-核框架，利用横截性理论为这些退化现象提供统一的几何解释。

Method: 采用分布统计框架，引入分布-核偶对(T, φ)，利用Whitney、Thom和Mather的横截性定理证明有限维弱横截性定理，通过特征映射的Jacobian秩条件建立可验证条件。

Result: 证明了对于足够丰富族中的一般核，核诱导的特征映射能够避开足够高余维的退化轨迹。验证了位置族、对数正态分布、Stein差异和图模型等场景的横截性条件。

Conclusion: 可识别性、鲁棒性、矩确定性、Fisher信息正则性、Stein差异、推断分离和Behrens-Fisher问题均可统一解释为特征映射上的横截性条件，为分布统计框架提供了几何基础。

Abstract: The distributional statistical framework replaces classical probability densities by distribution-kernel pairs $(T, \varphi)$, where $T$ is a tempered distribution and $\varphi$ is a rapidly decaying kernel. We develop the thesis that the kernel acts as a geometric regulariser, placing parametric statistical models in generic (transversal) position relative to degeneracy loci encoding non-identifiability, singular information, moment indeterminacy, and representation failure.
  Using the transversality theorems of Whitney, Thom, and Mather, we prove a finite-dimensional weak transversality theorem: for a generic kernel in any sufficiently rich family, the kernel-induced feature map avoids degeneracy strata of sufficiently high codimension. We establish verifiable conditions -- formulated as rank conditions on the Jacobian of the joint feature map -- under which the transversality hypothesis can be checked, and verify them for location families, the log-normal, Stein discrepancies, and graphical models.
  The present results apply to parametric models; extensions to semiparametric and nonparametric settings are discussed. The degeneracy classification includes representation degeneracy (Type 0) for models without closed-form densities and higher-order instabilities (Type IV) in non-chordal graphical models. Identifiability, robustness, moment determinacy, Fisher information regularity, Stein discrepancy, inferential separation, and the Behrens-Fisher problem all admit a unified geometric interpretation as transversality conditions on the feature map. This paper serves as a geometric companion to a series of papers developing the distributional framework.

</details>


### [125] [High-Dimensional Statistics: Reflections on Progress and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05076)
*Arian Maleki,Subhabrata Sen,Sivaraman Balakrishna,Verena Zuber,Chao Gao,Rishabh Dudeja,Christos Thrampoulidis,Anru Zhang,Weijie Su,Jason M. Klusowski,Po-Ling Loh,Ali Shojaie*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文综述了高维统计学在过去二十年中因技术进步（数据采集与存储成本降低）而取得的显著进展，涵盖了生物学、医学、天文学、社会科学和环境科学等领域。现代数据集的复杂特征（如依赖性和异质性）推动了高维统计学向更复杂的估计与推断问题演进，并与优化、测度集中、随机矩阵理论、信息论及理论计算机科学等领域建立了紧密联系。文章旨在综合代表性进展、突出共同主题和开放问题，并为新研究者提供入门指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维统计学领域发展迅速，成果丰富但分散，缺乏系统的梳理与总结。作者旨在通过综述将代表性进展整合，揭示共同的研究主题和尚未解决的问题，为研究者提供一个全面的领域概览和入门指引。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统性地梳理和综合高维统计学领域自过去二十年的代表性研究成果，提炼出核心主题和发展脉络，并指出开放性问题。

Result: 文章识别了高维统计学在多个方向上的代表性进展，阐述了该领域与优化、随机矩阵理论、信息论等交叉学科的深层联系，并指出了当前存在的开放问题。

Conclusion: 高维统计学已发展成为一个与多学科深度交叉的成熟领域，能够应对现代复杂数据带来的挑战。但领域内仍存在许多开放性问题，需要未来的进一步研究。

Abstract: Over the past two decades, the field of high-dimensional statistics has experienced substantial progress, driven largely by technological advances that have dramatically reduced the cost and effort for data collection and storage across a broad range of domains, including biology, medicine, astronomy, and the social and environmental sciences. Modern datasets are increasingly complex, often exhibiting rich dependency, heterogeneity, and other features that challenge traditional statistical methods. In response, high-dimensional statistics has evolved to address more sophisticated estimation and inference problems. This evolution has, in turn, fostered deep connections with and contributions to a wide range of research areas, including optimization, concentration of measure, random matrix theory, information theory, and theoretical computer science. Given the rapid pace of recent developments in high-dimensional statistics, our goal is to synthesize representative advances, highlight common themes and open problems, and point to important works that offer entry points into the field.

</details>


### [126] [Concordance, symmetrization and non-exchangeability for bivariate copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05173)
*Ávaro Rodríguez-García,Manuel Úbeda-Flores*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究二元copula的非交换性度量$μ_p$与经典依赖泛函之间的关系，证明了对称化$C\mapsto (C+C^t)/2$保持Spearman's $ρ$但消除$μ_p$，Blomqvist's $β$不包含非交换性信息，并建立了Schweizer-Wolff依赖度量$σ$的下界$σ(C) \ge 6\,μ_1(C)$，表明不对称性蕴含依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非交换性度量$μ_p$（Durante等人2010年提出）与经典依赖函数（如Spearman's $ρ$、Blomqvist's $β$、Schweizer-Wolff度量$σ$等）之间的理论关系，揭示非对称性与依赖性之间的内在联系。

Method: 理论分析：1) 证明对称化变换对Spearman's $ρ$的不变性和对$μ_p$的消去性；2) 分析Blomqvist's $β$与非交换性的关系；3) 推导$σ$与$μ_1$之间的Sharp下界不等式；4) 对最大非交换族$\{M_θ\}$推导出$τ$、$ρ$和尾部相关系数的闭式表达式。

Result: 1) 对称化操作保持Spearman's $ρ$但消除$μ_p$；2) Blomqvist's $β$不携带非交换性信息；3) 建立了$σ(C) \ge 6\,μ_1(C)$的Sharp下界；4) 给出了最大非交换族$\{M_θ\}$的$τ$、$ρ$和尾部相关系数的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 不对称性（非交换性）在一定程度上蕴含依赖性，不同依赖度量对非交换性的敏感度存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study the relationship between measures of non-exchangeability $μ_p$ ($p\in[1,+\infty]$), in the sense of Durante et al. (2010), and classical dependence functionals for bivariate copulas. We show that the symmetrization $C\mapsto(C+C^t)/2$ preserves Spearman's $ρ$ while annihilating $μ_p$, and that Blomqvist's $β$ carries no information about the degree of non-exchangeability. We also establish the sharp lower bound $σ(C)\ge 6\,μ_1(C)$, where $σ$ is the Schweizer-Wolff dependence measure, showing that asymmetry implies dependence. Closed-form expressions for $τ$, $ρ$, and the tail-dependence coefficients of the maximally non-exchangeable family $\{M_θ\}$ are derived as illustrations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Bayesian Optimization in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00237)
*Jesse Schneider,William J. Welch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于递归二分空间划分的贝叶斯优化方法，通过将建模和采样策略与划分方案协调统一，解决了标准贝叶斯优化中计算复杂度高（三次方）和全局建模不适合局部优化的问题，在6-124维的测试函数上表现优于常用库，且具有线性计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化存在两个主要缺陷：1) 训练集大小增加时，高斯过程模型训练的计算复杂度呈三次方增长，导致计算不可行；2) 目标函数的全局建模对于局部极值搜索并非最优。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 采用灵活递归的二分搜索空间划分，将标准贝叶斯优化的建模部分（高斯过程）和采样获取部分（采集函数）与划分方案协调统一，使得局部建模与局部采集相互配合。

Result: 在7个维度从6到124的挑战性测试函数上，与常用贝叶斯优化库相比，所提方法在所有测试中均获得更优的优化性能，且计算复杂度为线性。

Conclusion: 通过递归二分空间划分，有效降低了贝叶斯优化的计算复杂度（从三次方降至线性），同时由于局部建模更符合优化目标，提升了优化效果。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a sequential method for minimizing objective functions that are expensive to evaluate and about which few assumptions can be made. By using all gathered data to train a Gaussian process model for the function and adaptively employing a mixture of global exploration and local exploitation, this method has been used for optimization in many fields including machine learning, automotive engineering and reinforcement learning. However, the standard method suffers from two problems: 1) with cubic computational complexity in the training-set size it eventually becomes computationally infeasible to train the model, and 2) globally modeling the objective function is not necessarily optimal given the local nature of minimization. Using flexible and recursive binary partitioning of the search space, we adapt both the modeling and acquisitive aspects of standard Bayesian optimization to work harmoniously with the partitioning scheme, thereby ameliorating both standard shortcomings. We compare our method against a commonly used Bayesian optimization library on seven challenging test functions, ranging in dimensionality from $6$ to $124$, and show that our method achieves superior optimization performance in all tests. In addition our method has linear computational complexity.

</details>


### [128] [Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266)
*Etienne Gauthier,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准迭代RLHF因忽略策略对奖励模型的参数引导效应，导致对齐崩溃（策略利用RM盲点产生低质量高奖励输出，形成恶性循环）。本文提出前瞻性策略优化（FPO），通过正则化策略的参数引导效应来恢复缺失的梯度项，防止对齐崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF假设奖励模型是静态的，但在迭代部署中，策略生成的数据会用于重新训练RM，形成反馈循环。这种循环导致策略利用RM的盲点，产生低质量但高奖励的输出，其生成的反馈数据又强化了RM自身的错误，最终导致对齐崩溃。

Method: 基于Stackelberg博弈形式化策略与RM的交互，推导策略真实优化梯度的解析分解：(1) 标准策略梯度 + (2) 参数引导项（策略对未来RM参数的影响）。标准迭代RLHF完全丢弃了参数引导项。FPO通过一阶可微近似恢复该项，对策略的参数引导效应进行正则化约束。

Result: 在受控环境和Llama-3.2-1B大模型对齐流水线上，FPO有效防止了对齐崩溃，相比标准迭代RLHF显著提升了输出质量。

Conclusion: RLHF迭代部署中的反馈循环会导致策略主动利用RM盲点，简单忽略策略对RM参数的影响是导致对齐崩溃的根本原因。通过机制设计恢复参数引导项可以系统性解决此问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) typically assumes a static or non-strategic reward model (RM). In iterative deployment, however, the policy generates the data on which the RM is retrained, creating a feedback loop. Building on the Stackelberg game formulation of this interaction, we derive an analytical decomposition of the policy's true optimization gradient into a standard policy gradient and a parameter-steering term that captures the policy's influence on the RM's future parameters. We show that standard iterative RLHF, which drops this steering term entirely, suffers from alignment collapse: the policy systematically exploits the RM's blind spots, producing low-quality, high-reward outputs whose feedback reinforces the very errors it exploits. To mitigate this, we propose foresighted policy optimization (FPO), a mechanism-design intervention that restores the missing steering term by regularizing the policy's parameter-steering effect on RM updates. We instantiate FPO via a scalable first-order approximation and demonstrate that it prevents alignment collapse on both controlled environments and an LLM alignment pipeline using Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [129] [A Mean Curvature Approach to Boundary Detection: Geometric Insights for Unsupervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04274)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCBP（平均曲率边界点）方法，通过估计数据流形的平均曲率来检测高维数据中的边界点，无需显式流形参数化，并基于曲率进行数据分解以提升聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于密度的边界检测方法在高维、非线性结构及异质密度场景下表现不佳，缺乏对数据流形内在几何结构的建模。

Method: 利用局部k近邻补丁离散近似形状算子，计算逐点平均曲率；采用自适应基于百分比的阈值方案实现多尺度边界提取；提出曲率驱动的数据分解，将样本分为低曲率（平滑）和高曲率（边界）子集。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，MCBP在复杂和高维场景下 consistently 提升了聚类性能。

Conclusion: MCBP是几何机器学习的一个具体贡献，展示了曲率感知分析作为连接微分几何与数据驱动建模的统一范式的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate boundary detection in high-dimensional data remains a central challenge in unsupervised learning, particularly in the presence of non-linear structures and heterogeneous densities. In this work, we introduce Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP), a novel geometric framework grounded in Geometric Machine Learning that departs from traditional density-based approaches by explicitly modeling the intrinsic curvature of the data manifold. The method relies on a discrete approximation of the shape operator, estimated from local k-nearest neighbor patches, to compute pointwise mean curvature without requiring explicit manifold parametrization. The key insight of MCBP is to use mean curvature as a principled descriptor of boundary structure: high-curvature regions naturally correspond to transitions between clusters, geometric irregularities, and low-density interfaces. This yields a unified geometric interpretation of boundary, outlier, and transition points. We further introduce an adaptive percentile-based thresholding scheme that enables multiscale boundary extraction without relying on ad hoc density parameters. Beyond detection, we propose a curvature-driven data decomposition that separates samples into smooth (low-curvature) and boundary (high-curvature) subsets, effectively acting as a non-linear geometric filtering mechanism. This representation enhances cluster separability and improves the robustness of downstream unsupervised algorithms. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that MCBP consistently improves clustering performance, particularly in complex and high-dimensional scenarios. These results position MCBP as a concrete contribution to Geometric Machine Learning, highlighting the potential of curvature-aware analysis as a unifying paradigm bridging differential geometry and data-driven modeling.

</details>


### [130] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FL-Sailer是首个专为scATAC-seq数据设计的联邦学习框架，通过自适应杠杆评分采样（降低80%维度）和不变VAE架构（最小化互信息以分离生物信号与技术噪声），在保护隐私的同时实现了超越集中式方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scATAC-seq数据存在超高维度、极度稀疏和跨机构异质性三大挑战，且受隐私法规和数据规模限制，多机构协作难以实现。现有联邦学习方法无法直接应用于scATAC-seq分析。

Method: 提出FL-Sailer框架，包含两大创新：1）自适应杠杆评分采样（adaptive leverage score sampling）进行特征选择，降低80%维度；2）不变VAE架构（invariant VAE）通过互信息最小化分离生物信号与技术混杂因素。提供了收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在合成和真实表观基因组数据集上，FL-Sailer不仅实现了此前不可行的多机构协作，还通过自适应采样的隐式正则化抑制技术噪声，性能超越集中式方法。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在针对领域特定挑战进行定制后，可以成为协作表观基因组研究的优越范式。

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [131] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明注意力谱方法存在方向盲区，提出非对称系数G作为方向唯一控制参数，并建立基于容量(φ)和方向(G)的双轴诊断框架，在多个模型上验证了极性反转的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉与注意力路由失败有关（过度集中或过度分散），现有谱方法仅分析对称分量，无法捕捉信息流方向，需要建立能同时刻画容量和方向的诊断框架。

Method: 理论证明所有转置不变谱诊断存在方向盲区，推导非对称系数G为方向唯一控制参数；结合闭式二分Cheeger景观，推导均匀因果注意力的n无关下界φ≥1/5（最坏切割t*/n≈0.32），窗口注意力突破下界为O(w/n)；构建(φ, G)双轴诊断框架。

Result: 提出可证伪的极性预测：瓶颈主导与扩散主导基准应呈现相反极性。在最长8B参数模型上，长度控制评估下传输特征保持可解释信号（LC-AUROC 0.62-0.84），HaluEval与MedHallu之间极性反转与预测一致。

Conclusion: 注意力故障模式在形状上可区分（不仅是数值差异），双轴诊断有效区分容量失效与方向失效，为幻觉检测提供了理论基础和可操作指标。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [132] [Regime-Conditioned Evaluation in Multi-Context Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04895)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对40篇迁移贝叶斯优化论文的审计，发现98%的研究未将预算/搜索空间大小作为控制变量。同一基准测试中，改变预算会逆转算法排名。作者提出便携式制度分数(PRS)预测条件效应，并开发RegimePlanner动态切换采集策略，在多个基准上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移BO比较通常估计隐藏制度变量上的平均处理效应，但实践者需要针对其特定先验质量、预算比和度量的条件效应。既有的无条件排名不稳定，需要可预测条件效应的框架。

Method: 提出便携式制度分数PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho)，其中rho是先验秩相关性；构建层次模型分析79种条件；开发RegimePlanner在线估计rho并动态切换采集策略。

Result: 层次模型beta=0.50(p=1.1e-9)，19%的条件落在|优势|<0.01 Hit@1的等价区。RegimePlanner在HPO-B中赢得全部16个搜索空间，在GDSC2上超出匹配的每上下文oracle 18%。预注册预测总体准确率67.5%，EMA先验家族内超90%。

Conclusion: 提出无免费排行榜命题解释无条件排名的不稳定性。实践方案简单：报告B/|A|、rho、K和度量指标即可。

Abstract: Published transfer-BO comparisons often estimate an average treatment effect of acquisition choice over hidden regime variables, while practitioners need the conditional effect for their specific prior quality, budget ratio, and metric. An audit of 40 transfer-BO papers from NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AISTATS, UAI, TMLR, JMLR, and AutoML-Conf (2022-2025) finds that 98% never vary B/|A| as a controlled axis. On the same GDSC2 benchmark, changing only the budget reverses the ranking: at B=50, Greedy outperforms UCB by 0.050 Hit@1, while at B=100, UCB outperforms Greedy by 0.035. We capture this transition with the Portable Regime Score PRS=(B/|A|)(1-rho), where rho is the prior rank correlation and can be estimated from pilot contexts before the main comparison. Across 79 conditions spanning chemistry, drug-response biology, and HPO, a hierarchical model gives beta=0.50 (p=1.1e-9), and 19% of conditions fall in an equivalence zone where |advantage|<0.01 Hit@1. In five published reversal cases, PRS predicts the winner from pre-comparison observables. A No-Free-Leaderboard proposition explains why unconditional rankings are unstable: when CATE changes sign across regimes, the reported ATE becomes a function of benchmark mixture. RegimePlanner, which estimates rho online and switches acquisition accordingly, wins all 16 HPO-B search spaces at B=100 and exceeds the matched {Greedy,UCB} per-context oracle on GDSC2 by 18%. Pre-registered predictions achieve 27/40=67.5% overall accuracy and above 90% within EMA prior families. The practical protocol is simple: report B/|A|, rho, K, and metric alongside any claimed acquisition advantage.

</details>


### [133] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究随机多臂老虎机和回合制强化学习中的遗憾分布，提出了带有特定探索奖励的UCBVI风格算法，推导了通用分布遗憾界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂老虎机分布遗憾界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾分析主要关注期望遗憾，缺乏对遗憾尾部风险的细粒度刻画。本文旨在通过分布遗憾界统一刻画不同置信水平下的遗憾行为，填补期望性能与尾部风险之间的理论空白。

Method: 提出UCBVI-style算法，采用探索奖励$\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$，其中$N$为访问次数，$(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$为用户指定参数。推导了关于参数序列的通用间隙无关与间隙相关分布遗憾界。

Result: 实现了期望遗憾与分布遗憾在极小极大和实例依赖两种机制下的最优权衡。在多臂老虎机设置下，获得了$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/\delta))$阶的分布遗憾界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文为随机MAB和回合制RL提供了统一的分布遗憾分析框架，揭示了探索参数对期望表现、尾部风险和实例依赖行为三者之间的权衡机制，达到了理论上的最优性。

Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

</details>


### [134] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）视角分析Deng等人（2026）提出的生成式漂移模型（GMD），证明其算法对应KL散度WGF的极限点（带Parzen平滑），而实际实现算法类似Sinkhorn散度WGF但缺乏关键性质，并可将该思路扩展到MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN鉴别器等其他WGF极限点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）的理论视角重新审视和理解GMD，澄清其算法与理论之间的关系，并探索该思想的推广可能性。

Method: 将GMD置于WGF的理论框架下进行分析：证明其提出算法对应于KL散度WGF（带Parzen平滑）的极限点；指出实际实现算法与Sinkhorn散度WGF极限点的相似性及差异；并将该思想扩展到MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN鉴别器等其他WGF。

Result: 1) GMD提出算法对应带Parzen平滑的KL散度WGF极限点；2) 实际实现算法类似Sinkhorn散度WGF极限点但缺乏关键性质；3) 该思想可扩展至MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN鉴别器等其他WGF。

Conclusion: GMD可被理解为直接寻找特定WGF的固定点，这一视角不仅提供了理论统一框架，还揭示了其实际实现与理论算法之间的差异，并可自然推广到多种生成模型目标函数。

Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

</details>


### [135] [Transformation Categorization Based on Group Decomposition Theory Using Parameter Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04056)
*Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群论的无监督表征学习方法，通过参数分割和同态约束来分解变换，摒弃了先前方法中的辅助假设，并在旋转、平移、缩放等图像变换任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解耦表示学习假设因子相互独立，但实际中因子常耦合；先前基于Galois理论的方法虽能处理非交换群，却依赖运动约束、等距限制等辅助假设，且消融实验未能分离理论核心与辅助效应的影响。

Method: 提出参数分割方法：将单个变换的参数拆分为多个分量，施加同态约束（将完整变换映射到某个分量），并将正规子群定义为当该分量为恒等时的变换集合，从而在无辅助假设条件下实现群分解。

Result: 在旋转、平移、缩放的图像对实验中，消融研究表明群分解约束确实驱动了正确的类别划分，且去除了辅助假设后方法仍有效。

Conclusion: 所提方法在更广泛条件下实现了无监督的变换分类，无需先前的运动或等距假设，更贴近分解理论本身的要求。

Abstract: Representation learning seeks meaningful sensory representations without supervision and can model aspects of human development. Although many neural networks empirically learn useful features, a principled account of what makes a representation "good" remains elusive. We study unsupervised categorization of transformations between pairs of inputs under algebraic constraints. Classical disentanglement favors mutually independent factors and fails when factors are coupled. Our prior Galois-theoretic approach decomposes a group via normal subgroups by learning a product of two transformations with one factor constrained to a normal subgroup, covering both commutative and non-commutative cases. That method, however, relied on auxiliary assumptions (e.g., motion and isometry restrictions) not required by decomposition theory, and ablations did not separate theory-based from auxiliary effects. We propose parameter division for a single transformation: we split its parameter into components, impose homomorphism constraints mapping the full transformation to one component, and identify the normal subgroup as the set of transformations when that component is fixed to the identity. This formulation drops the previous auxiliary assumptions and applies more broadly. We evaluate on image pairs involving rotation, translation, and scale; ablations show that group-decomposition constraints drive appropriate categorization.

</details>


### [136] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化渐进知识激活（SPARK）方法，通过显式选择并条件化编辑功能因子，解决了大语言模型辅助神经架构搜索中因功能纠缠导致局部修改引发全局性能偏移的问题，在CLRS-DFS上实现了28.1倍样本效率提升和22.9%的OOD准确率相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索面临的关键挑战在于：如何在昂贵评估条件下，既整合既有架构知识又探索新设计。大语言模型有望成为NAS的得力助手，因其能将丰富的架构和编码先验转化为可执行代码编辑。然而实践中，看似局部的修订常因功能纠缠（functional entanglement）——单个编辑意外耦合多个交互功能因子——引发非局部的行为和性能偏移，使得LLM知识难以有效利用。

Method: 提出结构化渐进知识激活（SPARK），通过两步策略解决功能纠缠：首先显式选择待修改的功能因子，然后将编辑操作条件化于所选因子之上。这种因子条件化编辑方式减少了纠缠的副作用，产生更精准、可靠的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS基准上，SPARK实现了28.1倍的样本高效架构演化加速，并带来了22.9%的分布外准确率相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式功能因子选择和条件化编辑，SPARK有效激活了LLM中的相关先验知识，克服了功能纠缠带来的局部编辑全局影响问题，为LLM辅助NAS提供了一种更可控、高效的范式。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"前瞻漂移模型"，通过在每次训练迭代中顺序计算多个漂移项（利用先前计算的漂移项、正样本和模型输出），并将模型输出推向漂移项的加权和方向，以实现比原始漂移模型更好的分布映射性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移模型（drifting model）在单步神经功能评估下取得了SOTA图像生成性能，但其每次训练迭代仅计算单个漂移项，未能充分利用高阶梯度信息。

Method: 在每次训练迭代中顺序计算一组漂移项，每个后续漂移项利用先前计算的漂移项、正样本和当前模型输出进行计算；后续漂移项捕获朝向正样本的高阶梯度信息，最终将模型输出推向这些漂移项的加权和方向。

Result: 在玩具示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于基线漂移模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入前瞻计算多个漂移项并加权求和，能够捕获更高阶的梯度信息，从而提升分布映射的生成质量。

Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

</details>


### [138] [EdgeRazor: A Lightweight Framework for Large Language Models via Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04062)
*Shu-Hao Zhang,Le-Tong Huang,Xiang-Sheng Deng,Xin-Yi Zou,Chen Wu,Nan Li,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeRazor，一个用于大语言模型的轻量级混合精度与极低位权重量化框架，通过混合精度量化感知蒸馏、自适应特征蒸馏和熵感知KL散度三大模块，在1.88bit精度下超越所有3bit竞争方法，比领先的2bit PTQ方法提升11.3个点，训练成本仅为领先QAT方法的1/4到1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重量化方法存在明显局限：PTQ在4bit以下性能严重下降，QAT计算资源需求巨大，量化感知蒸馏则依赖人工选择特征和教师特定数据。亟需一种轻量级、高效且适用于极低位宽（<4bit）的量化框架。

Method: EdgeRazor包含三个核心模块：(1) 混合精度量化感知蒸馏，实现细粒度精度控制；(2) 自适应特征蒸馏，从16位教师模型导出n位学生模型；(3) 熵感知KL散度，同时作用于人工标注和蒸馏数据集，其前向-反向平衡仅由教师输出分布决定。

Result: 1.88bit EdgeRazor超越所有3bit竞争方法，比领先2bit PTQ方法提升11.3点；训练成本仅为领先QAT方法的4-10倍更低；Qwen3-0.6B在1.58bit下存储从1.41GB降至0.28GB，解码速度相比16bit基线加速15.1倍。

Conclusion: EdgeRazor在极低位宽量化上实现了显著的性能-效率权衡，适用于基础、指令微调及多模态LLM，是资源受限设备上部署LLM的有效方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an increasing interest in deploying LLMs on resource-constrained devices, among which quantization has emerged as a promising lightweight technique that converts full-precision model weights and activations into lower-bit formats. Existing weight quantization approaches can be roughly divided into three categories: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) that calibrates quantized parameters on a small dataset without retraining but suffers from severe performance degradation below 4-bit, Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) that searches low-bit parameters using surrogate gradients but demands substantial computational resources, and Quantization-Aware Distillation that integrates QAT with knowledge transfer from a full-precision teacher but manually selects features to distill and relies heavily on teacher-specific data. In this paper, we propose EdgeRazor, a lightweight framework for LLMs with mixed-precision and extremely low-bit weight quantization. The EdgeRazor framework contains three modules: Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation for the fine-grained control of precision, Adaptive Feature Distillation that derives an $n$-bit student from its 16-bit teacher, and Entropy-Aware KL Divergence on both human-annotated and distilled datasets, whose forward-reverse balance is determined solely by the teacher's output distribution. Empirical investigations of EdgeRazor are conducted on base, instruction-tuned, and multimodal LLMs. Notably, EdgeRazor with 1.88-bit surpasses all contenders with the 3-bit precision, especially outperforms the leading 2-bit PTQ methods by 11.3 points, within a 4-10$\times$ lower training budget than the leading QAT approach. EdgeRazor delivers higher compression ratios at all bit width; the 1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B reduces storage from 1.41 GB to 0.28 GB while accelerating decoding by 15.1$\times$ relative to the 16-bit baseline.

</details>


### [139] [Investigating Trustworthiness of Nonparametric Deep Survival Models for Alzheimer's Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04063)
*Jacob Thrasher,Kaitlyn Heintzelman,Peter Martone,David Kotlowski,Binod Bhattarai,Donald Adjeroh,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了阿尔茨海默病进展分析中的公平性问题，提出两个新的公平性度量指标（时间依赖C指数不纯度与Kaplan-Meier公平性），发现深度学习生存模型虽预测能力强但存在显著偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病进展预测对患者护理至关重要，现有深度学习生存模型在AD领域研究较少，且已有研究忽视了模型内部的学习偏差，可能导致对特定边缘群体的不公平和不可靠预测。

Method: 对AD进展分析进行严格的公平性研究及特征重要性分析；提出两种新公平性度量：Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity和Kaplan-Meier Fairness，用于量化非参数生存模型中对性别、种族、教育等敏感属性的偏见。

Result: 深度学习生存模型虽能有效辅助临床决策，但在多个敏感属性上表现出显著偏见。

Conclusion: 现有模型存在公平性问题，未来需进一步研究以消除偏见。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Dementia (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease marked by irreversible decline, making reliable modeling of its progression essential for effective patient care. Progression-aware methods such as survival analysis are therefore crucial tools for the early detection and monitoring of AD. Recent advancements in deep learning have demonstrated remarkable performance in survival tasks, but alarmingly fewer studies have been conducted in the domain of AD. Further, the studies that do exist do not consider learned bias within the model itself, which could result in unfair and unreliable predictions toward certain marginalized groups. As such, we conduct a rigorous study of fairness in AD progression analysis along with a thorough feature importance study to determine the characteristics which are most important for reliable AD predictions. Furthermore, we propose two novel fairness metrics, called Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity and Kaplan-Meier Fairness, to quantify bias with respect to sensitive attributes such as sex, race, and education in nonparametric survival models. Our study demonstrates that while deep learning powered survival models are robust tools which can aid clinicians in AD care decisions, they often exhibit considerable bias, representing important avenues for future research.

</details>


### [140] [Designing a double deep reinforcement learning selection tool for resilient demand prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04068)
*Bilel Abderrahmane Benziane,Benoit Lardeux,Ayoub Mcharek,Maher Jridi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双深度强化学习架构的自动预测模型选择方法，利用平均奖励收敛的早停策略加速训练，在杂货和零食数据集上验证了优于现有方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链预测中AI方法众多，但每个数据集具有独特特征，导致选择合适的预测模型非常困难，这一挑战自80年代以来一直未被充分解决。

Method: 提出一种双深度强化学习智能体架构，在预测时从预测委员会中自动选择模型；同时引入基于平均奖励收敛的新型早停策略以缩短训练时间。

Result: 在杂货销售数据集和零食需求数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法相比现有最先进方法具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双深度强化学习结合平均奖励收敛早停策略可有效提升自动预测模型选择的效率与准确性，为供应链需求预测提供了有前景的新范式。

Abstract: The use of artificial intelligence in supply chain forecasting has attracted many scientific studies for several decades. However, the process of selecting an appropriate forecasting solution becomes a daunting task. This complexity arises due to the distinct features inherent to each dataset. Research to tackle this issue has been performed since the eighties but recent development of demand forecasting has opened new perspectives. This research aims to enhance automatic forecasting model selection by proposing a novel architecture that acts as a double deep reinforcement learning agent, selecting automatically a forecasting model from the forecasting committee at the time of prediction. Moreover, a novel early-stopping approach based on average reward convergence has been introduced to expedite training time. To evaluate the model's performance, an empirical study was conducted utilizing grocery sales datasets and snack demands datasets. The experimental results demonstrate the robustness of the proposed approach when compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [141] [LAWS: Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically -- A Self-Certifying Parametrized Cache Architecture for Neural Inference, Robotics, and Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04069)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAWS自认证推理缓存架构，通过部署观测构建带形式化误差界限的专家函数库，在无真实标签情况下实现可验证的近似推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE和KV缓存方法存在表达能力受限（固定K值）或缺乏理论误差保证的问题，无法在部署时自验证推理质量。

Method: 基于概率语言特里树（PLT）划分输入空间，为每个节点学习带形式化误差界限的专家函数；核心是自认证定理，通过模型Lipschitz常数、嵌入直径和专家训练误差三者在部署时即可计算的总误差界。

Result: 1）自认证定理保证近似误差有界且部署时可验证；2）统一了MoE和KV前缀缓存，表达能力强于固定K MoE或有限缓存；3）命中率单调增长定理；4）专家库增长率O(2^H log N)；5）多单元车队学习收敛加速Omega(K)。

Conclusion: LAWS为在线推理缓存提供了首个可自认证的理论框架，具有单调覆盖率保证和可验证误差界，适用于LLM推理、机器人控制和多智能体边缘部署。

Abstract: We introduce LAWS (Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically), a self-certifying inference caching architecture that builds a growing library of certified expert functions from deployment observations. Each expert covers a region of input space defined by a node in the Probabilistic Language Trie (PLT) of the base model and carries a formal error bound holding uniformly over all inputs. The central result is a self-certification theorem: for any input x, the LAWS approximation error is bounded by epsilon_fit + 2*Lambda(W)*C_E, where Lambda(W) is the model Lipschitz constant, C_E is the maximum embedding diameter, and epsilon_fit is the expert training error -- all checkable at deployment time without ground truth. We prove that LAWS generalizes both Mixture-of-Experts and KV prefix caching as special cases and is strictly more expressive than any fixed-K MoE or finite cache. Further results include a monotone hit rate theorem (any-match routing ensures coverage only increases), an expert library growth rate of O(2^H log N) where H is workload entropy, a fleet learning convergence theorem with Omega(K) speedup for K-unit fleets, and an over-the-air update bandwidth bound. We conjecture that LAWS is acquisition-optimal among stationary online caching algorithms and that the effective Lipschitz constant on the training distribution grows polynomially rather than exponentially in depth. Applications are developed for LLM inference, robotic control, and multi-agent edge deployment.

</details>


### [142] [FlatASCEND: Autoregressive Clinical Sequence Generation with Continuous Time Prediction and Association-Based Pharmacological Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04071)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlatASCEND，一个14.5M参数的自回归临床序列模型，使用扁平复合令牌和零膨胀对数正态时间头。尽管标准分布指标无法区分模型与基线，但模型在患者条件生成中展现了价值。关键发现包括：患者特异性条件作用放大了药理学效应（2.0-2.2倍），而混杂驱动的关联保持不变；在MIMIC-IV上4/10恢复正确机制方向，2个复现治疗-情境关联，4个错误；直接偏好优化破坏了所有正确关联（3/3降至0/3），说明奖励利用问题；生成证据在ICU短期数据中最强，门诊时间保真度较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归模型可以预测临床事件，但在生成患者条件下的多步轨迹以响应干预令牌，并测试这些响应是否保持已知的药理学关联方面，受到的关注有限。

Method: 提出FlatASCEND模型（14.5M参数），采用扁平复合令牌和零膨胀对数正态时间头。通过提示洗牌消融实验、新用户框架评估、直接偏好优化等方法分析模型的条件生成能力。

Result: 1) 标准分布指标（Jaccard 0.889-0.954）无法区分模型与平凡基线；2) 患者特异性条件作用放大药理学效应（2.0-2.2x），不改变混杂驱动关联（0.9x）；3) MIMIC-IV上4/10恢复正确机制方向，2个复现治疗-情境关联，4个错误；4) 直接偏好优化破坏所有正确关联；5) ICU短期数据生成最强，门诊保真度较弱，零样本跨站点迁移需适应。

Conclusion: FlatASCEND在条件生成中展现部分药理学知识恢复能力，但模式表明模型学习的是观察性关联而非因果区分。直接偏好优化中的奖励利用问题导致关联完全破坏。模型在短期ICU数据上表现最佳，但门诊和跨站点泛化能力有限。

Abstract: Autoregressive models can predict clinical events, but generating patient-conditioned multi-step trajectories that respond to intervention tokens and testing whether those responses preserve known pharmacological associations has received limited attention. We present FlatASCEND, a 14.5M-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model using flat composite tokens and a zero-inflated log-normal time head. Standard distributional metrics (Jaccard 0.889-0.954) do not distinguish FlatASCEND from trivial baselines; the model's value lies in conditional generation from patient-specific prefixes. A prompt-shuffle ablation shows patient-specific conditioning amplifies mechanistic pharmacological effects (2.0-2.2x for steroid to glucose, diuretic to potassium) while leaving confounding-driven associations unchanged (0.9x for insulin to glucose). An incident-user framework assesses directional consistency against prior pharmacological knowledge on MIMIC-IV (N=500 per comparison): 4/10 recover correct mechanistic directions, 2 reproduce treatment-context associations, 4 are incorrect (9/10 significant, Wilcoxon p<0.05). This pattern - partial recovery under residual confounding - is consistent with learned observational associations without causal distinction. Direct preference optimisation with surrogate reward destroys all correct associations (3/3 to 0/3), illustrating reward exploitation when reward and evaluation share an outcome domain. Generative evidence is strongest for short-horizon ICU data; outpatient temporal fidelity is weaker (median 10 vs 154 days on INSPECT), and zero-shot cross-site transfer degrades without adaptation.

</details>


### [143] [Sparse Autoencoder Decomposition of Clinical Sequence Model Representations: Feature Complexity, Task Specialisation, and Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04072)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将稀疏自编码器(SAE)系统应用于临床序列基础模型FlatASCEND，发现SAE特征随Transformer深度呈现渐进式抽象（从浅层的单token检测到深层的跨类别多token特征），但在泄漏安全窗口中密集表示在所有临床预测任务上匹配或超越SAE特征，且SAE特征的可重复性仅为21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SAE已被应用于大语言模型和蛋白质语言模型，但尚未被系统地应用于电子健康记录(EHR)基础模型。作者希望填补这一空白，探索SAE在临床序列模型上的表现。

Method: 在FlatASCEND（1450万参数的临床自回归序列模型）的所有10个残差流提取点上训练TopK SAE，使用INSPECT（门诊）和MIMIC-IV（ICU）数据。采用全序列线性探针、泄漏安全窗口评估，以及delta-mode干预方法将SAE扰动噪声降低86倍。

Result: 层0特征为近完美token检测器（45.7%单一token），层6特征跨约30种token类型（0.5%单一token）。全序列线性探针下SAE在离散事件预测（死亡率）上优于密集表示，但密集表示在连续量预测（住院时长）上更优。泄漏安全窗口中密集表示在所有设置下匹配或超越SAE特征（eICU-CRD 48h AUC: 0.880 vs 0.871；MIMIC-IV: 0.914 vs 0.836；INSPECT: 0.800 vs 0.697）。特征可重复性仅为21%。

Conclusion: SAE在临床序列模型中揭示了渐进式抽象的表征结构，但在泄漏安全临床预测任务中密集表示整体优于SAE特征。SAE特征的可重复性低，应被视作说明性而非稳定的特征。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have been applied to large language models and protein language models, but not systematically to electronic health record (EHR) foundation models. We train TopK SAEs on FlatASCEND, a 14.5-million-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model, at all 10 residual stream extraction points on INSPECT (outpatient) and MIMIC-IV (ICU). SAE decomposition reveals progressive abstraction across transformer depth: layer-0 features are near-perfect token detectors (45.7% singleton), while layer-6 features span approximately 30 token types across multiple clinical categories (0.5% singleton). Under full-sequence simple linear probes, SAE features outperform dense representations for discrete event prediction (mortality) while dense representations outperform for continuous magnitude prediction (length of stay) - a probe-level representational phenomenon that does not extend to clinically relevant leakage-safe windows, where dense representations match or exceed SAE features across all tested settings (eICU-CRD 48-hour AUC: SAE 0.871 versus dense 0.880; base model zero-shot, SAE dictionaries trained on eICU activations; MIMIC-IV: 0.836 versus 0.914; INSPECT 1-year/3-year: 0.697 versus 0.800). A delta-mode intervention method reduces SAE perturbation noise by 86x, enabling cleaner feature-level experiments, though the resulting perturbation effects are larger than random controls in 3 of 4 conditions but not formally significant. Feature reproducibility across random seeds is 21%, and individual features should be interpreted as illustrative rather than stable.

</details>


### [144] [Confronting Label Indeterminacy in Automated Bail Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04073)
*Cor Steging,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 保释决策中的标签不确定性（因被拒绝保释者的反事实结果不可观测）给数据驱动决策支持系统带来根本性挑战。本文以宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统为案例，评估了五种处理标签不确定性的方法，发现这些方法对模型预测行为的影响甚至超过模型本身的选择，并通过可解释AI分析揭示了其对模型内部决策过程的影响，最后从法律视角审视了这些方法的合法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保释决策数据中存在结构性标签不确定性：被拒绝保释的被告是否实际会出庭无从观测，这导致历史数据中的决策结果只能部分可知。基于此类数据构建自动化系统可能引入偏见并强化反馈循环，因此亟需探究如何在模型开发中处理这些被拒绝保释的案件。

Method: 以宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统的保释决策为案例，评估五种当代标签不确定性处理方法，涵盖三种机器学习模型，并引入一种受保释决策动态启发的新型标签插补方法。采用可解释AI分析模型内部决策过程，并从法律视角评估各方法的合法性。

Result: 每种方法都依赖于不可验证的假设，但所有方法均影响模型的预测行为，其影响程度有时甚至超过模型本身的选择。可解释AI分析显示这些影响也延伸至模型的内部决策过程。

Conclusion: 保释决策中的标签不确定性是一个根本性挑战，现有处理方法均基于不可验证的假设且显著影响模型行为，在将其应用于司法辅助系统前需审慎评估其合法性与潜在影响。

Abstract: Bail decisions present a fundamental challenge for data-driven decision support systems. When bail is denied, the counterfactual outcome of whether the defendant would have appeared in court remains unobserved. As a result, historical bail data embed structural label indeterminacy: future decisions are influenced by past decisions whose outcomes are only partially knowable. Building automated systems on such data risks introducing bias and reinforcing feedback loops. This raises a core question for machine-learning systems intended to assist judicial actors: how should cases in which bail was denied be treated during model development? In a case study of bail decisions from the Unified Judicial System of Pennsylvania, we evaluate five contemporary approaches to handling label indeterminacy across three machine learning models, including a novel label imputation method motivated by the dynamics of bail decisions. Each method relies on unverifiable assumptions, yet all influence the models' predictive behaviour, sometimes even more so than the choice of model itself. Explainable AI analysis further reveals that these effects extend to the models' internal decision-making processes as well. Finally, we consider the notion of label indeterminacy from a legal perspective and assess the legitimacy of these approaches in the context of bail decision-making.

</details>


### [145] [RetentiveKV: State-Space Memory for Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04075)
*Sihao Liu,YuFan Xiong,Zhonghua Jiang,Zhaode Wang,chengfei lv Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RetentiveKV，一种基于信息熵的KV缓存优化方法，将离散的token剪枝转变为基于状态空间模型的连续记忆演化，在长视觉上下文中实现5倍KV缓存压缩和1.5倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理长视觉上下文时，视觉KV缓存急剧膨胀导致计算效率和内存消耗严重问题。现有基于"重要性持续性"假设的KV缓存压缩方法在多模态场景下存在两个关键缺陷：1) 视觉token具有"延迟重要性"，初始时显著性低但在后续解码中变得关键，导致过早被驱逐；2) 离散剪枝破坏了视觉线索固有的空间连续性。

Method: 提出RetentiveKV方法：利用信息熵量化低注意力token的信息潜力，将计划驱逐的token通过熵引导的状态转换集成到连续状态空间中，使其在后续解码中当语义相关性出现时能够动态重新激活。将KV驱逐从"离散上下文截断"重构为"基于状态空间模型的连续记忆演化"。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上进行大量实验，RetentiveKV实现5.0倍KV缓存压缩和1.5倍解码加速。

Conclusion: RetentiveKV通过熵驱动的连续记忆演化机制，有效解决了多模态场景下视觉token的延迟重要性问题和空间连续性破坏问题，实现了高效的KV缓存压缩和解码加速。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models face severe challenges in computational efficiency and memory consumption due to the substantial expansion of the visual KV cache when processing long visual contexts. Existing KV cache compression methods typically rely on the "persistence of importance" hypothesis to prune tokens. However, this approach proves fragile in multimodal settings due to two key issues: 1) Visual tokens display "deferred importance," initially exhibiting low salience but becoming pivotal during later decoding, which can lead to premature eviction. 2) Discrete pruning disrupts the inherent spatial continuity of visual cues. To address these challenges, we propose RetentiveKV, an entropy-driven KV cache optimization method that reformulates KV eviction from "discrete context truncation" to "continuous memory evolution" based on State Space Models. Our method leverages information entropy to quantify the information potential of low-attention tokens and integrates tokens scheduled for eviction into a continuous state space through entropy-guided state transitions, enabling their dynamic reactivation when semantic relevance arises during subsequent decoding. Extensive experiments on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that RetentiveKV achieves 5.0 times KV cache compression and 1.5 times decoding acceleration.

</details>


### [146] [A Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection in U.S. Banking: Integrating OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN Compliance Requirements for Model Development, Validation, and Monitoring Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04076)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGF-AFFD三级治理架构，将美国四项监管框架（OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB、FinCEN）整合为AI反欺诈模型全生命周期合规蓝图，通过LSTM+XGBoost集成模型（ROC-AUC 0.9289）在近90万笔交易上验证，并引入监管数字孪生元模型实现持续合规监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国金融机构在部署AI反欺诈检测时面临四项监管框架（OCC Bulletin 2011-12、SR 11-7、CFPB AI通告、FinCEN BSA/SAR要求）相互割裂的合规环境，缺乏将这些要求与模型开发、验证和监控实践相连接的集成治理生命周期。

Method: 1) 三级治理架构（RGF-AFFD）；2) 多研究实证程序，使用IEEE-CIS（590,540笔交易）和ULB基准（284,807笔交易）数据集；3) 基准测试六种架构（含LSTM+XGBoost集成）；4) 消融实验、时间漂移分析、SHAP可解释性分析、BISG公平性分析；5) RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型，将指标转化为四项监管特定健康评分及综合监管适配性指数。

Result: LSTM+XGBoost集成模型ROC-AUC达0.9289（F1: 0.6360），效益成本比6:1；XGBoost时间稳定性最强（delta-AUC = -0.0017，LSTM为-0.0626）。通过社区银行实施案例验证，提出四项循证政策建议。

Conclusion: RGF-AFFD是首个同时满足OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN要求的集成部署蓝图，为AI反欺诈检测提供了从模型开发到持续合规监控的端到端治理方案。

Abstract: U.S. financial institutions deploying AI-based fraud detection face a fragmented compliance landscape spanning four regulatory frameworks -- OCC Bulletin 2011-12, SR 11-7, the CFPB AI circular, and FinCEN BSA/SAR requirements -- with no integrated governance life cycle connecting these requirements to model development, validation, and monitoring practice. This paper presents the Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection (RGF-AFFD), a three-tier governance architecture empirically anchored in a multi-study empirical program. Using the IEEE-CIS dataset (590,540 transactions) and ULB benchmark (284,807 transactions), we benchmark six architectures including an LSTM+XGBoost ensemble, and conduct ablation, temporal drift, SHAP interpretability, and BISG fairness analyses. The LSTM+XGBoost ensemble achieves ROC-AUC of 0.9289 (F1: 0.6360) with a benefit-cost ratio of 6:1. XGBoost demonstrates the strongest temporal stability (delta-AUC = -0.0017 versus -0.0626 for LSTM). The RDT-FG Regulatory Digital Twin meta-model translates metrics into four regulator-specific health scores and a composite Regulatory Fitness Index for continuous compliance monitoring. The RGF-AFFD is the first integrated deployment blueprint to simultaneously satisfy OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN requirements, supported by a community bank implementation vignette and four evidence-based policy recommendations.

</details>


### [147] [Balanced Aggregation: Understanding and Fixing Aggregation Bias in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04077)
*Zhiyuan Zeng,Jiameng Huang,Zhangyue Yin,Jiashuo Liu,Ziniu Li,Bingrui Li,Yuhao Wu,Yining Zheng,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡聚合（Balanced Aggregation, BA）方法，解决GRPO风格RLVR中令牌聚合和序列聚合两种策略的优化偏差问题，在多个推理和编码基准上提升训练稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
