### [183] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEXST提出了一种几何对齐的Transformer模型，直接在六边形点阵坐标上操作，通过定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码，结合对比敏感差异目标和单细胞基础模型先验，从H&E染色组织切片中预测空间基因表达，在七个数据集中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量有限，难以大规模部署。现有从H&E图像推断空间基因表达的方法大多假设笛卡尔或几何无关的局部性，忽略了广泛使用的六边形点阵平台的采样结构，且逐点回归目标导致基因表达过度平滑，掩盖了基因特异性的空间异质性。

Method: HEXST直接在六边形点坐标上操作，采用定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码实现高效的局部到全局上下文建模；同时引入对比敏感差异目标和来自预训练单细胞基础模型的转录组先验，增强基因层面的空间对比度。

Result: 在七个空间转录组数据集上，HEXST在准确性和鲁棒性方面持续优于现有最先进模型，同时能够保持基因层面对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST通过几何对齐的Transformer架构有效利用了六边形点阵平台的结构特性，结合对比学习目标和单细胞先验，显著提升了从组织学图像预测空间基因表达的精度和生物学可解释性。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>


### [184] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UE-DPO方法，通过认知不确定性引导的token级探索，解决MLLM中DPO因自参考偏差导致的幻觉对齐不足问题，在偏好学习中主动纠正视觉缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评估的视觉敏感性分配训练权重，但模型仍在训练中，其自我估计存在自参考偏差：强化已学好的视觉线索，忽略难以感知的关键细节，限制了更深入的对齐。

Method: 提出UE-DPO方法。首先量化模型无法将token预测锚定到给定图像的认知不确定性；然后基于不确定性感知的探索强度，对偏好样本的视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力，同时减轻对非偏好样本中有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 通过大量实验证明了方法在缓解幻觉方面的有效性和鲁棒性，并给出了理论证明。

Conclusion: UE-DPO通过认知不确定性引导的token级探索机制，能有效克服自参考偏差，使模型主动发现并纠正认知缺陷，实现更深入的视觉-语言对齐。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [185] [Leveraging Pretrained Language Models as Energy Functions for Glauber Dynamics Text Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04291)
*Tarun Kathuria,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Glauber动力学的离散扩散语言模型，利用预训练因果/掩码语言模型构建能量函数作为平稳分布，显著提升文本生成质量。使用UL2作为预训练模型，该方法在扩散LM中达到最优，与同规模自回归模型性能相当，并在零样本常识推理、Sudoku和Zebra谜题等规划搜索任务中优于或匹敌GPT-2风格的自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散扩散模型使用均匀转移核作为前向过程，生成质量有限。本文旨在利用预训练语言模型构建能量函数，将之作为平稳分布，从而大幅提升离散扩散LMs的文本生成质量。

Method: 基于Glauber动力学构建离散扩散模型，前向过程不采用均匀转移核，而是利用预训练的因果/掩码语言模型定义能量函数，将其作为目标平稳分布进行逆向生成。采用UL2作为预训练模型集成到扩散流程中。

Result: 该方法超越先前的扩散语言模型，与同等规模的自回归模型性能相当；在零样本常识推理、Sudoku和Zebra谜题等规划搜索任务上，优于或匹敌GPT-2风格的自回归模型。

Conclusion: 基于Glauber动力学和预训练语言模型能量函数的离散扩散方法，能够有效弥合扩散LM与自回归模型之间的性能差距，并在多种任务上展现竞争力。

Abstract: We present a discrete diffusion-based language model using Glauber dynamics from statistical physics. Our main insight is that instead of trying to train a discrete state space diffusion model using Glauber dynamics with a uniform transition kernel as the forward process, one can set up an ``energy function'' based on pretrained causal/masked language models. When viewed as the stationary distribution, this energy function allows us to significantly improve the quality of the generated text. Incorporating UL2 as the pretrained model into our diffusion pipeline, we outperform prior diffusion based LMs and perform competitively with autoregressive models of comparable model sizes. Furthermore, our models are competitive with or outperform prior diffusion models and GPT-2 style auto-regressive models on zero-shot common sense reasoning tasks as well as planning and search tasks like Sudoku and Zebra puzzles.

</details>


### [186] [LUCAS-MEGA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Representation Learning in Soil-Environment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04323)
*Kuangdai Leng,Simon Jeffery,Panos Panagos,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了LUCAS-MEGA大规模多模态土壤数据集（7万+样本，1000+特征，融合68个源数据集），并提出SoilFuser多智能体人机协同数据融合流水线，以及基于自监督掩码预训练的SoilFormer多模态表格Transformer，在土壤预测和表征学习上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土壤研究受限于碎片化、异构化的数据集，只能进行小规模预测建模，无法开展高维表示学习，亟需大规模、多模态、统一的机器学习就绪数据集以推动土壤科学数据驱动建模。

Method: 以LUCAS调查为骨干，融合68个来源数据集构建LUCAS-MEGA；提出SoilFuser多智能体人机协同流水线，标准化异构数据格式、解决单位不一致和编码簿不匹配等问题；预处理后训练SoilFormer多模态表格Transformer，采用基于特征掩码的自监督预训练目标。

Result: LUCAS-MEGA数据集包含70,000+样本和1,000+特征，覆盖物理、化学、环境、生物和视觉属性；SoilFuser有效解决数据异构问题；SoilFormer实现稳定训练和强预测性能，学习到的表征支持不确定性感知预测，并恢复了与已知土壤过程一致的关联关系。

Conclusion: LUCAS-MEGA以开放获取方式发布，并提供可组合的、智能体友好的API支持结构化查询和数据驱动工作流，为大规模土壤表示学习和多模态建模奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Understanding soil is fundamental to agriculture, carbon cycling, and environmental sustainability, yet progress is limited by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets that constrain modeling to small-scale predictive settings rather than high-dimensional representation learning. We introduce LUCAS-MEGA, a large-scale multimodal dataset constructed through systematic data fusion of European soil-environment observations, with the LUCAS survey as its backbone. The fused dataset comprises over 70,000 samples and more than 1,000 features spanning physical, chemical, environmental, biological, and visual attributes, aggregated from 68 source datasets. To enable integration at scale, we develop SoilFuser, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop data fusion pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous data formats and measurement protocols, resolves inconsistencies and invalid entries (e.g., unit inconsistencies, codebook mismatches, and erroneous values), incorporates natural language annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes and metadata into a unified, machine learning-ready feature space. The resulting dataset captures key characteristics of real-world soil observations, including multimodality, uneven feature coverage, and heterogeneous uncertainty. To demonstrate the usability of LUCAS-MEGA for data-driven modeling, we pretrain a multimodal tabular transformer (SoilFormer) using a self-supervised objective based on feature masking, achieving stable training, strong predictive performance, and representations that support uncertainty-aware prediction. We further show that the learned representations recover relationships consistent with established soil processes. LUCAS-MEGA is released with open access and is accompanied by composable, agent-friendly APIs that support structured querying and data-driven workflows.

</details>


### [187] [DeFed-GMM-DaDiL: A Decentralized Federated Framework for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04324)
*Rebecca Clain,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Fred Ngole Mboula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFed-GMM-DaDiL，一种完全去中心化的联邦多源域适应方法，通过GMM和Wasserstein重心实现无中心服务器的知识迁移，在处理目标域缺失类时保持稳定的共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决去中心化环境下多源域适应问题，在保护客户端隐私的同时实现无需中央服务器的知识迁移，并处理目标域类别缺失的挑战。

Method: 每个客户端将数据集建模为高斯混合模型（GMM），联邦通过可学习的共享GMM原子的标记Wasserstein重心联合近似这些GMM，实现完全去中心化的域适应。

Result: 在目标域缺失类别的场景下，该方法能保持稳定一致的共享表示，有效重建缺失类别，并在多源域适应基准上达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL在去中心化多源域适应中兼顾了隐私保护、表示稳定性和类别重构能力，验证了基于GMM的联邦学习范式的有效性。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-source domain adaptation seeks to transfer knowledge from multiple heterogeneous and related source domains to an unlabeled target domain in a decentralized setting. We address this challenge through a fully decentralized federated approach, DeFed-GMM-DaDiL, an extension of the GMM-Dataset Dictionary Learning (DaDiL) framework. Each client models its dataset as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and the federation jointly approximates them via labeled Wasserstein barycenters of shared, learnable GMM atoms. This design enables adaptation without a central server while preserving clients' privacy. We empirically study the stability of the learned representations in scenarios where the target domain has missing classes. Empirical results demonstrate that DeFed-GMM-DaDiL maintains stable and consistent shared representations across clients, effectively reconstructs missing classes, and achieves competitive performance on multi-source domain adaptation benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文正式建立了部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟（OD）与动作延迟（AD）之间的等价性，证明二者在任意时间跨度上共享相同的最优解空间，但实际学习动态存在根本差异，并利用结构等价性实现了OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有延迟系统研究主要集中于单智能体无限时域场景，缺乏对部分可观测多智能体协作系统中不同延迟类型（观测延迟与动作延迟）之间关系的正式理论理解，阻碍了统一高效的延迟系统求解方法的发展。

Method: 从理论上证明OD与AD在观测-动作历史下生成相同的联合策略集且诱导的轨迹分布相同，将单智能体无限时域结果推广到任意时域的部分可观测多智能体问题；在TI-MDP中证明观测-动作历史可简化为可处理的局部增广状态；通过数值实验揭示学习动态差异，并提出零样本策略迁移方法。

Result: OD与AD在任意时间跨度的Dec-POMDP中结构等价；但在TI-MDP之外等价性不成立；TD算法中的因果信用分配错误导致不同延迟系统下学习行为不同；成功实现了从OD到AD的多智能体零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 观测延迟与动作延迟在理论上等价，但实际学习困难不同；利用结构等价性可绕过学习挑战实现策略迁移，为复杂延迟系统的统一高效求解方法奠定基础。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [189] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SL(3,Z)子群构造问题的结构化数学推理基准，通过密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性，评估语言模型在开放可判定性边界上的元认知能力。实验发现模型在指数变体上展示了校准的元认知：经过152分钟推理后主动弃权而非提交错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法区分模型是真正理解数学结构还是依赖通用计算模式，需要设计能够暴露模型对代数先验知识（如Aschbacher分类、McLaughlin定理、性质(T)、同余子群性质）内化程度的评估框架。

Method: 构建SL(3,Z)中有限生成子群问题基准，利用构造时间信息(N,K)可在O(1)闭形式确定的不变量（指数、素数上满射、成员关系），而求解者必须通过Aschbacher分类分析或SL(3,Z)中可判定性未知的成员查询来推导。设计四种行为分类（正确承诺、错误承诺、正确弃权、错误弃权）替代标准答案评分。

Result: 两个最先进模型在五种代表性推理轨迹上的实验显示：在指数变体上，一个模型耗时152分钟，明确识别核侧成员关系问题是瓶颈，尝试构造性验证后以"DON'T KNOW"弃权，展示了校准的元认知。基准暴露了标准答案评分混淆的四类行为。

Conclusion: 该基准成功区分了模型的代数推理能力与通用计算能力，四类行为分类比传统评分更能反映模型在数学推理中的真实认知状态，尤其在开放可判定性问题上。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [190] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非随机扰动下边际稳定且部分可观测的线性动态系统，提出了一种通过预测提示稳定的无约束在线最小二乘方法，实现了对数遗憾界，无需有界轨迹假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的在线学习方法通常依赖有界域/梯度，无法处理边际稳定系统中可能出现的无界轨迹问题。本文旨在为非随机扰动下的边际稳定线性系统设计可靠的在线预测算法。

Method: 提出一种无约束在线最小二乘法，通过精心构造的预测提示来稳定学习过程。在已知模型时，利用任何稳定Luenberger预测器构造提示，使提示残差一致有界；在无模型场景下，为对称系统设计通用提示。

Result: 在模型已知时，实现了对数遗憾（logarithmic regret）；在无模型场景下，对称系统同样保持对数遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了与经典固定增益观测器相比自适应且实例最优的在线预测器，适用于非随机扰动下边际稳定线性系统的在线预测问题。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [191] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了p-adic流形学习，给出了一种求解算法，并基于无偏博弈设计了基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形学习方法主要基于实数域，缺乏对p-adic数域（一种非阿基米德结构）下数据内在几何的建模能力，难以处理具有层级结构或非度量拓扑特征的数据。

Method: 定义了p-adic流形学习问题，设计了一种针对p-adic数域结构的流形学习算法，并利用无偏博弈（如取石子游戏等）构造了具有天然p-adic结构的基准任务集。

Result: 在p-adic基准任务上验证了所提算法的有效性，表明该方法能够捕获p-adic空间的层级与树状结构特征。

Conclusion: p-adic流形学习为处理非阿基米德度量空间中的数据降维与结构发现提供了新范式，无偏博弈任务是该方向的标准化评估平台。

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [192] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），通过机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性，证明了完全反事实可识别性，并在CausalInverter框架中实现，在非单调合成数据和MuJoCo机器人任务上取得优越的反事实恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局单调性在具身交互中常被违反（相同的外生扰动在不同接触上下文中引发相反响应），需要探索在放弃全局单调性后仍能保证反事实可识别性的结构条件。

Method: 引入NM-TM-SCM，保留三角递归但以机制可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性；证明等价于外生同构并实现完全反事实可识别性；实例化为CausalInverter框架，包含三角可逆层、定向门和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 合成数据上结构性偏差随非单调性增加带来系统性反事实增益；MuJoCo Door场景实现完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线；MuJoCo Push场景中非单调性较弱时仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 识别出介于全局单调三角模型与无约束黑箱世界模型之间的更宽可识别性区间，为非单调具身交互中的因果推断提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [193] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在表示和最优传输的稀疏反事实社区干预框架，能够从交通调查数据中生成政策可行、稀疏且可解释的群体级干预策略，以推动目标群体向参考群体转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通调查分析多为描述性或预测性，缺乏能生成稀疏、政策可行的干预策略的方法。作者希望从调查数据中挖掘出可控变量调整方案，以实现群体间的分布对齐。

Method: 采用固定基非负潜在表示保持前后可比性；通过Shapley值识别目标相关潜在因子并映射到可控变量；利用熵正则化最优传输最小化目标与参考分布差异，结合加权ℓ₂,₁范数促进策略稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上，所提框架能够生成紧凑、可解释的政策可行干预方案，具备明确的调整幅度，提升了群体级转化率，并保持了干预的稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该研究为从调查数据中自动推导稀疏、可操作的政策干预策略提供了有效途径，兼具理论严谨性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies. We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [194] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WZ-LLM框架，将Wilf-Zeilberger符号方法转化为Lean 4可执行证明草图，结合LLM证明器完成子目标，通过专家验证的引导循环和DAPO强化学习训练专用WZ-Prover，在组合恒等式证明任务上取得34%的成功率，显著优于DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM证明器在处理组合恒等式自动证明时面临长程推理规划困难、搜索空间爆炸的问题，亟需将符号方法的机械化优势与LLM的推理能力相结合来提升证明成功率。

Method: 1) 神经符号框架WZ-LLM：将WZ方法生成的证明计划转化为Lean 4可执行证明草图；2) 专用WZ-Prover：通过Lean内核验证的引导循环（含专家验证迭代）训练，再经DAPO强化学习进一步优化。

Result: 在LCI-Test（100个经典组合恒等式）上达到34%的证明成功率，在CombiBench和PutnamBench-Comb上也取得一致提升，全面超越DeepSeek-V3和Goedel-Prover-V2等强基线。

Conclusion: 该框架提供两种互补优势：一是对WZ方法适用范围外的恒等式提升了直接证明能力，二是当WZ草图引导专用证明器时显著提高了端到端成功率和机器可验证性。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [195] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEPO方法，通过利用历史偏好数据构建不确定性奖励，实现在线RLHF中的高效探索，理论证明其具有数据相关的遗憾界，实验表明相比强基线方法具有更优的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLHF中探索是一个基础性挑战，现有探索策略依赖on-policy期望来推导奖励，但训练过程中有限的历史偏好数据使得这些估计不可靠，导致策略过早降低对未充分探索区域（可能包含高价值行为）的权重。

Method: DEPO方法利用历史数据构建高不确定性区域的额外不确定性奖励，鼓励模型探索潜在的高价值数据；理论上给出了数据相关的遗憾界，证明算法能自适应学习任务的难度。

Result: 理论结果：提出的数据相关遗憾界比最坏情况下的界更紧；实验结果：在多个基准测试中一致优于强基线方法，展现出更优的样本效率。

Conclusion: DEPO是一种简单可扩展的在线RLHF探索方法，通过数据依赖的探索策略有效提升了样本效率，理论和实验均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [196] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Quadrature-TreeSHAP，一种基于高斯-勒让德求积法的路径依赖TreeSHAP重配方，仅需8个固定求积点即可达到机器精度，在CPU和GPU上均显著加速且数值稳定性更强，已集成至XGBoost。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释树集成预测的Shapley值方法存在深度相关运行时、数值不稳定以及不支持高阶交互等权衡问题。

Method: 基于加权Banzhaf交互多项式，将Shapley值和任意阶交互值表示为特征参与概率p的期望积分，再通过Gauss-Legendre求积法（仅需8个固定点）计算该积分，消除了深度依赖性并支持SIMD高效执行。

Result: 在12个XGBoost基准上，CPU端加速1.06x-10.59x，GPU端加速1.84x-6.95x；Shapley成对交互加速3.80x-58.11x，高阶交互加速最高达1200x。

Conclusion: Quadrature-TreeSHAP在数值稳定性、计算速度和交互值支持方面全面优于现有方法，是一种高效且鲁棒的树集成模型解释工具。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [197] [Gradient Scaling Effects in Adaptive Spectral PINNs for Stiff Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04502)
*Isabela M. Yepes,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了PINNs中初始条件（IC）嵌入函数的选择（指数门控 vs. 线性门控）对刚性和振荡动力系统训练优化条件的影响。通过非线性刚性弹簧摆ODE基准实验发现，IC门控函数会引发显式的时间依赖梯度缩放，与频谱表示相互作用。在中等刚度下指数门控更优但异质性高，高刚度下线性门控更优，且存在刚度依赖的优劣反转现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应频谱PINNs中，IC嵌入的优化影响未被充分表征，尽管已有工作强调频谱参数化等表示层面的改进。本文旨在揭示IC门控函数选择对训练优化条件的实质性影响。

Method: 以非线性刚性弹簧摆ODE为受控基准，比较指数门控与线性门控与固定/自适应傅里叶谱主干组合的性能。使用相对L²误差和最大逐点误差作为评价指标，采用配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm校正）进行统计验证。

Result: 中等刚度（k=20）时指数门控通常误差更低但随机种子间行为异质；高刚度（k=60）时线性门控更优，且在更大k值下出现优劣反转。这些趋势在两种误差指标下一致，并经统计检验确认。

Conclusion: IC嵌入在PINNs中并非中性设计选择，其诱导的梯度缩放实质上塑造了刚性 regimes 下的优化条件，且在基线和自适应频谱模型中呈现不同的敏感模式。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) often struggle to train reliably on stiff and oscillatory dynamical systems due to poor optimization conditioning. While prior work has emphasized representational remedies such as spectral parameterizations, the optimization implications of initial-condition (IC) embeddings in adaptive spectral PINNs have not been well characterized. In this work, we show that the choice of IC gating function induces explicit time-dependent gradient scaling, which interacts with spectral representations during training. Using a nonlinear stiff spring-pendulum ODE as a controlled benchmark, we compare exponential and linear IC gates in combination with fixed and adaptive Fourier spectral trunks. We observe stiffness-dependent changes in relative dominance for adaptive PINNs: at moderate stiffness ($k=20$), exponential gating often yields lower error but exhibits heterogeneous behavior across random seeds, whereas at higher stiffness ($k=60$), linear gating becomes preferable, with additional reversals observed at larger $k$. These trends hold for both relative $L^2$ error and maximum pointwise error and are confirmed by paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Overall, our results demonstrate that IC embeddings are not a neutral design choice in PINNs: the induced gradient scaling materially shapes optimization conditioning in stiff regimes, with distinct sensitivity patterns in baseline and adaptive spectral models.

</details>


### [198] [YOTOnet: Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Fault Diagnosis via Domain-Conditioned Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04528)
*Zesen Wang,Zihao Wu,Yue Hu,Yang Gao,Fuzhen Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出YOTOnet，一种针对机械设备跨域故障诊断的"一次训练"架构，通过物理感知不变特征蒸馏器、域条件稀疏专家和双头分类系统，在30个跨数据集协议上优于现有方法，并观察到清晰的数据集规模扩展效应（F1从0.5339提升至0.705）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习故障诊断模型因域偏移问题难以在不同设备和工况间泛化，受基础模型在零样本泛化方面成功的启发，作者希望设计一种能够"一次训练、跨域部署"的故障诊断架构。

Method: YOTOnet包含三个核心组件：(1)物理感知不变特征蒸馏器——使用多尺度膨胀卷积和基于FFT的时频融合提取域无关表示；(2)域条件稀疏专家(DC-MoE)——通过学习的门控机制自适应路由输入至专用处理器，无需外部元数据；(3)双头分类系统与辅助监督。

Result: 在CWRU、MFPT、XJTU、OTTAWA、HUST五个公开轴承数据集上通过30个跨数据集协议验证，YOTOnet优于现有最先进方法。观察到清晰的规模扩展效应：平均测试F1从1个训练集的0.5339提升至4个数据集的0.705。

Conclusion: 这些发现为以下结论提供了实证证据：基础模型原理能够实现工业故障诊断中鲁棒的"一次训练、多次部署"方案。

Abstract: Mechanical equipment forms the critical backbone of modern industrial production, yet domain shift severely limits the generalization of deep learning based fault diagnosis models across different equipment and operating conditions.Inspired by the success of foundation models in achieving zero-shotgeneralization, we propose YOTOnet (You Only Train Once), a novel architecture specifically designed for cross-domain fault diagnosis in mechanical equipment.YOTOnet comprises three core components: (1) a physics-aware Invariant Feature Distiller that extracts domain-agnostic representations using multi-scale dilated convolutions and FFT-based time-frequency fusion,(2) Domain-Conditioned Sparse Experts (DC-MoE) that adaptively route inputs to specialized processors via learned gating without external meta-data, and (3) a dual-head classification system with auxiliary supervision.Extensive validation on five public bearing datasets (CWRU, MFPT, XJTU,OTTAWA, HUST) through 30 cross-dataset protocols demonstrates the superiority of YOTOnet compared with other state-of-the-art methods. Critically, we observe a clear scaling effect-average test F1 improves from 0.5339(1 training dataset) to 0.705 (4 datasets), with a clear gain when moving from 3 to 4 datasets. These findings provide empirical evidence that foundation model principles can enable robust, train-once deployment for industrial fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [199] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了幂分布（power distribution）在采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习和自蒸馏之间的桥梁作用，证明了幂分布是KL正则化RL的闭式优化器，并由此提出幂自蒸馏方法，在推理任务上以更低推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究对RL是否真正负责LLM的强推理能力存在质疑，同时蒸馏和推理时采样（包括幂采样）被证明有效，但RL、蒸馏和采样三者之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在厘清这三者之间的理论联系。

Method: 从理论层面证明幂分布是KL正则化RL（以模型序列级对数概率为奖励）的闭式优化器；基于此识别提出幂自蒸馏方法——将幂采样的成本摊销为对教师样本的监督训练；分析真奖励与自奖励在幂分布下的协方差对改进的约束关系。

Result: 在推理任务上，幂采样提升了自奖励；真奖励的提升取决于与自奖励的对齐程度；幂自蒸馏能以更低的推理成本匹配或超越幂采样的性能。

Conclusion: 幂分布是连接采样、KL正则化RL和自蒸馏的统一框架，幂自蒸馏作为离线蒸馏替代方案可在保持性能的同时大幅降低推理开销。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [200] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的葡萄园病害风险早期预警方法，将传统的每日病害分类任务重新定义为预测未来3-7天内标注的风险期转变事件，并在多年代际气象数据上比较了XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等方法的性能，结果表明事件化公式能有效支持短期预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将病害预测建模为每日二分类任务，这容易偏向利用时间持久性进行预测，无法为可操作的短期预警提供足够支持。

Method: 将病害风险预测重新定义为事件预测任务——预测未来3-7天窗口内进入标注风险期的事件，并引入最小无病间隔以减少二元标签中断造成的碎片化。利用多年代际气象数据构建包含湿度动态、降雨累积、温度变异性及循环时间编码的输入表示，评估XGBoost、LSTM和TCN等模型。

Result: 事件化公式支持实用的短期预警，不同模型在事件召回率、预警提前量和误报行为之间表现出明显的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究强调了问题定义在环境时间序列学习中的重要性，并展示了基于事件的预测方法对葡萄园病害预警系统的价值。

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [201] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAAST提出了一种前向-only的关联适应方法，通过单次解析编译将标注样本转化为快速权重，在消除反向传播训练成本的同时避免了记忆/上下文推理的开销，在图像分类和语言建模任务上匹配或超越现有方法，适应时间减少90%以上，内存节省高达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练模型适应方法存在两难困境：反向传播训练成本高，而基于记忆或上下文学习的方法推理开销大。目标是提出一种既高效训练又轻量推理的适应方法。

Method: 提出FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation），一种前向-only的关联适应方法，通过解析方式将标注样本编译为快速权重（fast weights），只需单次前向传播即可完成适应，无需反向传播，推理时也无需依赖记忆或上下文。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准上，FAAST匹配或超越基于反向传播的适应方法，适应时间减少90%以上；与基于记忆/上下文的适应方法相比，内存使用节省高达95%。

Conclusion: FAAST是一种高效、可扩展的监督任务适应解决方案，特别适用于资源受限的模型，实现了恒定时间推理并将任务适应与预训练表示解耦。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [202] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阈值引导的对齐框架，通过将标量反馈转化为二元决策任务，无需成对偏好数据即可有效对齐视觉生成模型与人类反馈，在扩散和掩码生成范式上均取得了优于先前方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对偏好优化方法依赖于标注好的偏好对，这在反馈以独立标量评分形式收集的场景下限制了可扩展性。作者希望克服这一局限，使对齐能够直接从标量反馈中进行优化。

Method: 重新审视KL正则化对齐目标，证明最优策略隐式地将每个样本的奖励与实例特定的基线进行比较，但该基线通常不可解。提出用数据驱动的全局阈值（从经验分数统计中估计）替代该基线，将对齐转化为对未配对数据的二元决策任务；同时引入置信加权项，强调分数偏离阈值较大的样本以提高样本效率。

Result: 在扩散和掩码生成两种范式上，跨越三个测试集和五个奖励模型的实验表明，该方法在偏好对齐上持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该阈值引导框架为无需成对比较的对齐视觉生成模型提供了一种简单而原则性的替代方案。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [203] [Feature importance analysis for patient management decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04666)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录数据，发现医生的实验室检查和用药决策往往可由少量关键临床特征进行预测，并报告了335项实验室检查和407项用药决策的统计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究临床数据中哪些特征对医生在实验室检查或用药决策中的影响最大，以理解医生决策行为的驱动因素。

Method: 基于4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康记录数据，对335项实验室检查和407项用药决策进行统计分析，识别影响医生决策的关键特征。

Result: 在许多情况下，医生的实验室检查和用药决策可以通过少量特征子集进行良好预测。

Conclusion: 临床决策往往由少数关键特征主导，这为优化临床决策支持系统提供了依据。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to understand what characteristics and features of clinical data influence physician's decision about ordering laboratory tests or prescribing medications the most. We conduct our analysis on data and decisions extracted from electronic health records of 4486 post-surgical cardiac patients. The summary statistics for 335 different lab order decisions and 407 medication decisions are reported. We show that in many cases, physician's lab-order and medication decisions can be well predicted from a small subset of all features.

</details>


### [204] [ITBoost: Information-Theoretic Trust for Robust Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04671)
*Ye Su,Longlong Zhao,Diego Garcia-Gil,Jipeng Guo,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Jinsong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ITBoost方法，通过最小描述长度原则衡量残差轨迹复杂度来评估样本可信度，从而提升梯度提升算法在标签噪声环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升在标签噪声下性能退化，因为算法强调大梯度样本但未区分这些梯度来自困难样本还是不可靠标签。

Method: ITBoost利用最小描述长度（MDL）原则衡量每个样本残差轨迹的复杂度（跨迭代的波动模式），对残差波动不规则的样本降低权重。

Result: 在噪声环境下优于主流提升方法和深度表格模型，且在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能；理论上推导了更紧的泛化界。

Conclusion: 通过追踪样本残差随迭代的演化而非瞬时误差来评估样本可靠性，能有效提升梯度提升在标签噪声下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gradient boosting remains a strong and widely used method for tabular data learning, but its performance often degrades when training labels are noisy. This behavior is largely related to the way boosting algorithms emphasize samples with large gradients, without explicitly accounting for whether such errors originate from informative hard cases or from unreliable labels. We address this issue by reconsidering how sample reliability is evaluated during boosting. Instead of relying on instantaneous error, we examine the evolution of each sample's residuals across iterations. Based on this insight, we propose Information-Theoretic Trust Boosting (ITBoost), which uses the Minimum Description Length principle to measure the complexity of residual trajectories. Samples whose residual patterns fluctuate in an irregular manner are treated as less trustworthy and are down-weighted during learning. Theoretically, we derive a tighter generalization bound for ITBoost under label noise. Empirical results on various tabular benchmarks indicate that ITBoost provides improved robustness in noisy environments over leading boosting and deep tabular models, while retaining best average performance on clean data.

</details>


### [205] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了编程知识追踪（PKT）中的混合模型，发现其性能提升受实现细节（如注意力维度设置、序列构建顺序）影响显著。在统一评估协议下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的差距大幅缩小，复杂的架构并不稳定带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PKT中混合模型（注意力+RNN）虽然报告了强性能，但其可靠性对实现细节和实验设计选择敏感，现有评估缺乏统一标准。

Method: （1）识别注意力维度设置影响性能估计；（2）指出忽略ServerTimestamp导致时序因果性违反；（3）采用单折网格搜索选择超参数并在所有折中固定；（4）分析作业特征和最大序列长度的影响；（5）在CodeWorkout数据集上重新评估。

Result: 在统一评估协议下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT的性能差距显著缩小，增加架构复杂度并不一致带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 为PKT领域提供了可靠可比评估的实践指导，强调统一实验设计的重要性。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [206] [Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04732)
*Sandarbh Yadav,Frederic J Maliakkal,Harshad Khadilkar,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于rollout的仿真规划中使用公共随机数（CRN）对效用估计方差的影响，提出了一种在超过一定深度的rollout策略下可证明降低相对效用方差的简单方法，并在合成任务和两个实际应用（养老金发放任务和Ludo游戏UCT算法）中验证了其性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机环境下的仿真规划中，rollout是一种广泛使用的决策技术，其核心是反复调用采样模型生成轨迹并估计动作效用。然而，不同动作之间的效用估计方差会影响决策质量，如何有效降低方差是提升规划性能的关键问题。

Method: 提出在rollout策略执行超过一定深度后引入公共随机数（Common Random Numbers, CRN）的方法，通过共享随机源来降低不同动作之间相对效用估计的方差，并给出理论上的方差缩减保证。

Result: 合成任务实验证实了该方法能提升任务性能。两个实际应用（养老金发放任务中的单步前瞻规划、Ludo游戏中的UCT算法部署）进一步展示了该创新的实用价值。

Conclusion: 在rollout规划中引入公共随机数是一种简单有效且可证明的方差缩减技术，能显著提升随机环境下基于仿真的决策规划性能，具有广泛的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Simulation-based planning with rollouts is a widely-deployed technique for decision making in stochastic environments. The primary instrument of simulation-based planning is a sampling model, which is repeatedly called to generate trajectories and estimate the utilities of available actions. Among the actions thus explored, one with the maximum estimated utility is then executed. In this paper, we examine the effect of using common random numbers in the simulation process. We obtain a simple recipe for (provably) reducing variance in relative utility when simulations invoke a rollout policy beyond some depth. Experiments on synthetic tasks confirm that our scheme improves task performance. The broader significance of our innovation is apparent from two practical applications: (1) single-step lookahead planning in a pension-disbursement task, and (2) a deployment of the well-known UCT algorithm for the game of Ludo.

</details>


### [207] [OSAQ: Outlier Self-Absorption for Accurate Low-bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04738)
*Zhikai Li,Zhen Dong,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSAQ（异常值自吸收量化）方法，利用Hessian矩阵的低秩一致性识别零空间，通过加法权重变换抑制异常值，实现大语言模型低比特权重量化，无需推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数量巨大导致推理资源消耗高，后训练权重量化虽能缓解内存瓶颈，但权重中的系统异常值严重影响低比特量化性能，现有方法（如缩放、旋转）效果仍不理想。

Method: 观察到Hessian矩阵在不同输入下具有低秩一致性，某些方向曲率始终为零；据此识别稳定的Hessian零空间，在其中线性组合向量构造加法权重变换，抑制权重异常值的同时不改变任务损失；该变换可离线吸收到权重中，无需层间变换或推理开销，且通过闭式解高效实现。

Result: 在2比特量化中，OSAQ集成GPTQ相比原始GPTQ困惑度降低超过40%；广泛实验证明OSAQ有效抑制异常值并提升低比特量化性能。

Conclusion: OSAQ通过利用Hessian低秩零空间实现无推理开销的异常值抑制，为LLM低比特权重量化提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities. However, their massive parameter scale leads to significant resource consumption and latency during inference. Post-training weight-only quantization offers a promising solution by reducing model size and accelerating token generation through alleviating the memory-bound issue. Nevertheless, the presence of inherent systematic outliers in weights continues to be a major obstacle. While existing methods, such as scaling and rotation, attempt to address this issue, the performance remains unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose Outlier Self-Absorption Quantization (OSAQ), which performs additive weight suppression guided by the second-order low-rank property for low-bit weight-only quantization of LLMs. Specifically, we observe that the Hessian exhibits low-rank consistency across different inputs, with certain directions consistently showing vanishing curvature. Leveraging this property, we identify a stable null space of the Hessian and then construct an additive weight transformation by linearly combining the vectors within this null space, thereby suppressing weight outliers without affecting the task loss. This additive transformation can be absorbed into the weights offline, requiring no inter-layer transformations and introducing no inference overhead. Moreover, the construction is efficiently achieved by a closed-form solution, without resource-intensive training or iterative procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OSAQ effectively suppresses outliers and enhances low-bit quantization performance. For instance, in 2-bit quantization, OSAQ, when integrated with GPTQ, achieves over 40% lower perplexity compared to vanilla GPTQ.

</details>


### [208] [Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC for Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04793)
*Matan Pagi,Zohar Sorek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC，通过在潜在动力学中引入控制依赖耦合（低秩双线性结构，参数量增加<1%），突破传统Koopman神经MPC中算符与控制输入条件独立性的限制，使有效算子能自适应当前输入，并在时变条件下实现更优的预测精度、闭环控制鲁棒性及陈旧计划执行的优雅退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Koopman的神经MPC模型从历史数据生成时变动力学，但强制系统算子独立于当前控制输入（条件独立性约束），这限制了模型在单个MPC时域内适应时变动力学的能力，尤其是在陈旧计划执行场景下表现不佳。本文旨在通过最小化扩展引入控制依赖耦合以突破这一限制。

Method: 提出Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC，在潜在动力学中引入控制依赖的耦合（低秩双线性结构），使有效算子能够根据当前输入自适应调整。该模型是标准线性条件独立性公式的严格推广，参数增加<1%，且可得到精确模型Jacobian矩阵，支持高效的序列凸规划（SCP），在标准信赖域假设下具有单调下降和KKT收敛保证。

Result: 在CartPole和RSCP基准测试（时不变和时变场景）中：1）预测精度方面，在训练噪声平均后，所提模型在每个测试单元上匹配或提升预测精度，在控制-状态耦合结构存在的场景中具有显著增益；2）闭环控制方面，RSCP时变任务中迭代SCP显著提升时域内自适应能力并大幅稳定训练，CartPole时变任务中增益适中但一致；3）陈旧计划执行实验中，双线性模型退化更优雅，在CartPole时变任务上保持一致优势，在RSCP时变任务上具有显著更大的鲁棒性裕度。

Conclusion: 控制依赖的潜在动力学为时变条件下鲁棒MPC提供了一种简单而有效的机制，双线性Koopman模型在参数开销极小的情况下实现了对标准线性公式的严格泛化，并在预测精度、闭环适应性和陈旧计划鲁棒性方面均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Koopman-based neural MPC models generate time-varying dynamics from historical data, but preserve convexity by enforcing that the system operator is independent of the current control input. This conditional independence constraint limits adaptation to changing dynamics within a single MPC horizon, particularly under time-varying conditions and under stale-plan execution.
  We propose Bilinear Mamba-Koopman Neural MPC, a minimal extension that introduces control-dependent coupling in the latent dynamics, allowing the effective operator to adapt to the current input. The resulting model is a strict generalization of the standard linear, conditional-independence formulation, adds less than 1% parameters through a low-rank structure, and admits exact model Jacobians that enable efficient Sequential Convex Programming (SCP) with monotone-descent and KKT convergence results under standard trust-region assumptions.
  Across CartPole and RSCP benchmarks in time-invariant and time-varying regimes, the proposed model matches or improves forecasting accuracy on every cell when training noise is averaged out, with strict gains where control-state coupling is structurally present. Its main closed-loop gains appear in the RSCP TV task, where iterative SCP improves adaptation within the horizon and substantially stabilizes training; in CartPole TV, the gains are modest but consistent. In delayed re-planning experiments on the time-varying variants, the bilinear model degrades more gracefully under stale-plan execution, maintaining a consistent advantage on CartPole TV and a substantially larger robustness margin on RSCP TV. These results show that control-dependent latent dynamics provide a simple and effective mechanism for robust MPC under varying conditions.

</details>


### [209] [Improving FMQA via Initial Training Data Design Considering Marginal Bit Coverage in One-Hot Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04825)
*Taiga Hayashi,Yuya Seki,Kotaro Terada,Yosuke Mukasa,Shuta Kikuchi,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对FMQA方法中初始采样导致部分二进制变量未被激活的问题，提出了通过拉丁超立方采样和Sobol序列实现完全边际位覆盖的改进方案，在飞行器翼型优化基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMQA在整数或离散连续变量（通过独热编码）的黑箱优化中，均匀随机初始采样会导致部分二进制变量从未被激活，相应的FM参数无法从观测响应中获得直接梯度更新，影响模型质量。

Method: 设计了两种空间填充采样方法：拉丁超立方采样（LHS）和Sobol序列，确保初始训练数据中每个独热编码得到的二进制变量至少被激活一次，即实现完全边际位覆盖，分别得到LHS-FMQA和Sobol'-FMQA。

Result: 在17变量和32变量的人力飞行器翼型形状优化基准上，两种方法均获得比基线FMQA更高的平均最终巡航速度，且在高维（32变量）问题上优势更明显。

Conclusion: 通过空间填充采样实现完全边际位覆盖能有效改进FMQA在离散/混合变量优化问题上的性能，尤其在高维场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Factorization machine with quadratic-optimization annealing (FMQA) is a black-box optimization method that combines a factorization machine (FM) surrogate with QUBO-based search by an Ising machine. When FMQA is applied to integer or discretized continuous variables via one-hot encoding, uniform random initial sampling can leave many binary variables never active in the initial training data, and the corresponding FM parameters receive no direct gradient updates from the observed responses. We address this by designing the initial training data to achieve complete marginal bit coverage, namely, ensuring that every binary variable obtained by one-hot encoding takes the value one at least once. We use two space-filling sampling methods, Latin hypercube sampling (LHS) and the Sobol' sequence, yielding LHS-FMQA and Sobol'-FMQA. On the human-powered aircraft wing-shape optimization benchmark with 17 and 32 design variables, both proposed methods achieved numerically higher mean final cruising speeds than the baseline FMQA, with the advantage more pronounced on the 32-variable problem.

</details>


### [210] [Trustworthy Federated Label Distribution Learning under Annotation Quality Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04827)
*Junxiang Wu,Zhiqiang Kou,Hongwei Zeng,Wenke Huang,Biao Liu,Hanlin Gu,Yuheng Jia,Di Jiang,Yang Liu,Xin Geng,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQual 是一个面向联邦标签分布学习（Fed-LDL）的质量感知框架，通过全局语义锚点校准低质量客户端、可靠性感知聚合机制重加权客户端贡献，以及四个新构建的基准数据集，有效解决了异构标注质量下的信任困境，并提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签分布学习（LDL）依赖高质量标注分布，但获取成本高且常含噪声；在联邦学习场景下，数据隔离加剧了客户端间标注质量的异质性，导致基于样本量的聚合策略（如FedAvg）失效，亟需一种质量感知的联邦LDL方法。

Method: 提出FedQual框架，包含两个核心机制：(i) 质量自适应客户端训练——利用全局语义锚点校准低质量客户端，同时保持高质量客户端的自主性；(ii) 可靠性感知服务器聚合——基于有效可靠信息而非原始样本量重新加权客户端贡献。此外，构建了四个具有可控标注质量差异的Fed-LDL基准数据集。

Result: 在四个新构建的基准数据集（FER-LDL、FI-LDL、PIPAL-LDL、KADID-LDL）上的大量实验验证了FedQual的有效性。理论证明表明，在异构监督质量下，客户端特定校准严格优于任何统一校准。

Conclusion: FedQual通过质量感知机制有效解决了联邦标签分布学习中因标注质量异质性导致的信任问题，为隐私敏感场景下的联邦LDL提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Label Distribution Learning (LDL) models supervision as an instance-wise probability distribution, enabling fine-grained learning under inherent ambiguity, but its success relies on high-fidelity label distributions that are costly to obtain and thus often noisy. Motivated by privacy-sensitive applications, we study Federated Label Distribution Learning (Fed-LDL), where data isolation further induces heterogeneous annotation quality across clients, making local updates unevenly reliable and breaking sample-size-based aggregation (e.g., FedAvg). To address this trust dilemma, we propose FedQual, a quality-aware Fed-LDL framework with two coupled mechanisms: (i) quality-adaptive client training guided by a global semantic anchor that calibrates low-quality clients while preserving high-quality autonomy, and (ii) reliability-aware server aggregation that reweights client contributions by effective reliable information rather than raw sample size. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct four new Fed-LDL benchmarks (FER-LDL, FI-LDL, PIPAL-LDL, and KADID-LDL) with controlled annotation quality disparity. We further provide a theoretical guarantee showing that under heterogeneous supervision quality, client-specific calibration is strictly better than any uniform calibration. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of FedQual.

</details>


### [211] [Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04830)
*Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型在生成动力学中存在一个临界时间窗口，该窗口内发生相变。研究发现非局域性与对称性破缺这两种相变临界时间几乎同步发生，首次在实践中统一了两种相变概念，为评估模型效率和指导架构设计提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成动力学中存在临界时间窗口，但对称性破缺（轨迹分叉为不同语义极小值）和非局域性（局部去噪失效）两种相变观点尚未被统一研究，缺乏对两者是否同时发生的实证分析。

Method: 通过对现代扩散Transformer生成轨迹的动力学和结果进行定量评估，分别测量对称性破缺临界时间（轨迹分叉为不同语义类别的时间点）和非局域性临界时间（局部去噪失败的时间点），并分析两者在时间上的关联性。

Result: 观察发现非局域性临界时间与对称性破缺临界时间几乎同步发生，两种相变在时间上高度一致。

Conclusion: 首次在实践中统一了扩散模型相变的两种概念，提供了判断扩散模型何时依赖条件信息和全局去噪的具体诊断方法，可用于评估模型效率、指导架构和采样方案设计以避免不必要的计算。

Abstract: Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. The symmetry breaking picture views the critical window as when trajectories bifurcate into different semantic minima of the energy landscape, whereas the nonlocality picture views the critical window as when local denoising fails. We study whether two notions of such phase transitions are concurrent in modern diffusion transformers. By evaluating the dynamics and outcomes of the generation trajectory, we observe a near-simultaneous occurrence of the non-locality and symmetry breaking critical times. Our work is the first to unify the two notions of phase transitions in practice: it provides a concrete diagnostic for when and why diffusion models rely on conditioning and global denoising, enabling principled evaluation of model efficiency and guiding the design of architectures and sampling schemes that avoid unnecessary computation.

</details>


### [212] [Replay-Based Continual Learning for Physics-Informed Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04832)
*Yizheng Wang,Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Yinghua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重放的持续学习策略，用于解决物理信息神经算子在分布外数据上的灾难性遗忘问题，该方法无需标签数据，结合蒸馏约束和LoRA迁移学习，在三个物理问题上验证了其有效性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在分布内数据上表现良好，但在遇到分布外数据时性能严重下降，这一问题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 将持续学习引入基于Transolver架构的物理信息神经算子，提出重放式持续学习策略：使用蒸馏约束保留旧知识，结合LoRA迁移学习快速适应新数据，全程无需标签数据，仅依赖输入场和物理约束进行训练。

Result: 在Darcy流问题、二维超弹性脑肿瘤问题和三维线性弹性TPMS问题上，该方法有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，同时保持了快速适应性，相比传统联合训练策略大幅提升了训练效率，降低了额外内存和计算开销。

Conclusion: 提出的重放式持续学习策略能有效解决物理信息神经算子在分布外数据上的灾难性遗忘问题，兼具高效性和低资源消耗，为神经算子的OOD泛化提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Neural operators generally demonstrate strong predictive performance on in-distribution (ID) problems. However, a critical limitation of existing methods is their significant performance degradation when encountering out-of-distribution (OOD) data. To address this issue, this work introduces continual learning into physics-informed neural operators, with particular emphasis on neural operators built upon the Transolver architecture, and proposes a simple yet effective replay-based continual learning strategy. The proposed method is fully physics-informed and does not require labeled data, relying solely on input fields together with physical constraints for training. When new OOD data become available, a small number of past data are incorporated through a distillation-based constraint to preserve previously acquired knowledge and alleviate catastrophic forgetting. Meanwhile, a transfer learning LoRA is employed to enable rapid adaptation to the new data. The proposed framework is systematically validated on three representative physical problems, including the Darcy flow problem in fluid mechanics, a two-dimensional hyperelastic brain tumor problem in biomechanics, and a three-dimensional linear elastic Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces problem in solid mechanics. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on previously learned data while maintaining fast adaptability to new data. Compared with conventional joint training strategies, the proposed method significantly improves training efficiency while reducing additional memory usage and computational cost.

</details>


### [213] [Bridging Input Feature Spaces Towards Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04834)
*Moshe Eliasof,Krishna Sri Ipsit Mantri,Beatrice Bevilacqua,Bruno Ribeiro,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALL-IN方法，通过将节点特征投影到共享随机空间并利用协方差统计量构建表征，实现跨数据集（不同输入特征）的图模型迁移，无需架构调整或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏统一的输入空间，不同图数据集的输入特征在语义、值范围和维度上不一致，导致图模型无法跨数据集泛化，限制了其作为基础模型的应用。

Method: 将节点特征投影到共享随机空间，基于协方差统计量构建节点表示（节点-协方差算子），从而消除对原始特征空间的依赖；证明了该表示对输入特征排列具有分布不变性，且期望算子对正交变换具有不变性。

Result: 在多种节点级和图级任务上，面对未见数据集的新输入特征，ALL-IN无需架构改变或重新训练即取得了强性能表现。

Conclusion: 提出了一种输入无关、可迁移的图模型构建方向，为图基础模型的发展提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Unlike vision and language domains, graph learning lacks a shared input space, as input features differ across graph datasets not only in semantics, but also in value ranges and dimensionality. This misalignment prevents graph models from generalizing across datasets, limiting their use as foundation models. In this work, we propose ALL-IN, a simple and theoretically grounded method that enables transferability across datasets with different input features. Our approach projects node features into a shared random space and constructs representations via covariance-based statistics, thus eliminating dependence on the original feature space. We show that the computed node-covariance operators and the resulting node representations are invariant in distribution to permutations of the input features. We further demonstrate that the expected operator exhibits invariance to general orthogonal transformations of the input features. Empirically, ALL-IN achieves strong performance across diverse node- and graph-level tasks on unseen datasets with new input features, without requiring architecture changes or retraining. These results point to a promising direction for input-agnostic, transferable graph models.

</details>


### [214] [A geometric relation of the error introduced by sampling a language model's output distribution to its internal state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04899)
*Albert F. Modenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT风格语言模型在预测概率分布分散的多token生成点上对单token变化敏感，并将此敏感性解释为几何属性，推导出仅依赖于token嵌入几何的$\mathfrak{so}(n)$值1-形式。尽管源于纯几何，其曲率在语义上有意义：在象棋推理任务中，曲率与指令微调模型的世界模型耦合，变换按棋盘区域聚类并尊重棋子重要性。这表明token空间几何直接反映模型内部表示问题的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GPT风格语言模型对单token变化敏感性的几何本质，并理解token嵌入空间的几何结构如何反映模型的内部世界模型和问题表示。

Method: 推导了一个$\mathfrak{so}(n)$-值1-形式，该形式仅依赖于token嵌入的几何；通过分析其曲率，在象棋推理任务上研究曲率与指令微调模型世界模型的关系。

Result: 曲率在语义上是有意义的：变换按棋盘区域聚类，并尊重棋子的重要性。

Conclusion: token空间的几何结构直接反映了模型内部如何表示问题。

Abstract: GPT-style language models are sensitive to single-token changes at generation points where the predicted probability distribution is spread across multiple tokens. Viewing this sensitivity as a geometric property, we derive an $\mathfrak{so}(n)$-valued 1-form that depends only on the geometry of the token embeddings. Despite this purely geometric origin, we show that its curvature is semantically meaningful: On chess reasoning tasks, the curvature couples to the world model of an off-the-shelf instruction-tuned model, with transformations clustering by board region and respecting piece importance. Our findings suggest that token space geometry directly reflects how models internally represent problems.

</details>


### [215] [Breaking the Quality-Privacy Tradeoff in Tabular Data Generation via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04911)
*Xinyan Han,Yan Lu,Xiaoyu Lin,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yuanrui Wang,Xuanyue Li,Wenchao Zou,Xingxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffICL，通过将表格数据生成转化为上下文学习问题，利用预训练的结构先验从有限上下文推断数据分布而非记忆样本，在14个真实数据集上同时提升了数据质量和隐私保护，改善了小数据场景下质量-隐私的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成模型在小数据场景下存在质量和隐私的明确权衡：提升数据质量通常以增加训练样本记忆（削弱隐私保护）为代价，因为小训练集难以区分可泛化结构与样本特定模式。

Method: 提出DiffICL，将表格数据生成建模为上下文学习问题；利用从大量数据集中预训练获得的结构先验，从有限上下文中推断数据分布而非记忆单个样本。

Result: 在14个真实数据集上，DiffICL同时提升了合成数据质量和隐私保护，并能作为有效的数据增强手段。

Conclusion: 通过更好的训练范式（上下文学习+预训练先验）可以有效改善表格数据生成中质量与隐私之间的权衡。

Abstract: Tabular data synthesis aims to generate high-quality data while preserving privacy. However, we find that existing tabular generative models exhibit a clear tradeoff in the small-data regime: improving data quality typically comes at the cost of increased memorization of training samples, thereby weakening privacy protection. This tradeoff arises because small training sets make it difficult for dataset-specific generative models to distinguish generalizable structure from sample-specific patterns. To address this, we propose DiffICL, which formulates tabular data generation as an in-context learning problem. Instead of fitting each dataset from scratch,DiffICL leverages pretrained structural priors learned from a large collection of datasets, enabling it to infer data distributions from limited context rather than memorizing individual samples. We evaluate DiffICL on 14 real-world datasets. Results show that DiffICL improves both data quality and privacy, and generate synthetic data that provides effective data augmentation. Our findings suggest that the quality-privacy tradeoff can be improved through better training paradigms.

</details>


### [216] [Koopman Identification of Nonlinear Systems via Reservoir Liftings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04917)
*Weibin Gu,Chen Yang,Lu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-Koopman框架，将储备池计算（RC）解释为有状态的有限维Koopman字典，通过储备池的谱半径显式控制时间记忆深度。利用回声状态性质（ESP）保证提升后的Koopman逼近的适定性和数值条件良好性。提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法，使储备池记忆与主导系统时间尺度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过Koopman算子理论学习非线性动力系统可逆线性表示的方法，常受困于字典选择、时序记忆编码和数值病态问题，亟需一种能同时解决这三方面挑战的框架。

Method: 将储备池计算（RC）重新解释为状态性有限维Koopman字典；利用回声状态性质（ESP）保证适定性和数值条件；提出基于相关性的谱半径选择算法以对齐记忆与系统时间尺度；理论分析储备池有限记忆对Koopman特征函数可观性的影响。

Result: 在合成基准测试上，RC-Koopman在非线性动力学重建精度和动力稳定性之间取得了优于EDMD和Hankel提升方法的平衡。

Conclusion: RC-Koopman通过储备池的受控记忆和ESP的数值保证，有效解决了Koopman逼近中的字典选择、记忆编码和数值病态问题，在实际非线性动力系统分析中具有优越性能。

Abstract: Learning tractable linear representations of nonlinear dynamical systems via Koopman operator theory is often hindered by dictionary selection, temporal memory encoding, and numerical ill-conditioning. Inspired by Reservoir Computing (RC) paradigm, this paper introduces the RC-Koopman framework, which interprets reservoir as a stateful, finite-dimensional Koopman dictionary whose temporal depth is explicitly controlled by its spectral radius. We show that the Echo State Property (ESP) guarantees well-posedness and favorable numerical conditioning of the lifted Koopman approximation. A correlation-based spectral radius selection algorithm aligns reservoir memory with dominant system timescales. Analysis reveals how the finite memory of the reservoir determines which Koopman eigenfunctions remain observable from the lifted features. Evaluation on synthetic benchmarks demonstrates that RC-Koopman achieves a favorable balance between reconstruction accuracy of the underlying nonlinear dynamics and dynamical stability, compared to Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) and Hankel-based lifting approaches. Code available at: https://github.com/NEAR-the-future/RC-Koopman.git

</details>


### [217] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行结果级强化学习，显著提升了模型的组合泛化能力，优于传统的监督微调方法。强化学习通过重塑输出分布，尤其改善了复杂组合类型的泛化，而监督模型则容易过拟合高频训练组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合泛化方法主要依赖监督微调，这种词元级训练范式无法捕获全局组合结构，导致模型难以泛化到未见过的组合。

Method: 采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行结果级强化学习，探索简单二值结果奖励和提供组合反馈的复合奖励两种方案。

Result: 在多个组合泛化基准上，强化学习相比监督微调显著提升了组合泛化性能。监督模型过拟合高频训练组合，而强化学习通过重塑输出分布改善泛化，尤其对复杂组合类型效果更明显。

Conclusion: 结果级强化学习（特别是GRPO框架）能有效提升组合泛化能力，其机制在于重塑输出分布而非简单地模仿训练数据，为突破组合泛化瓶颈提供了新思路。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [218] [Adaptive Inverted-Index Routing for Granular Mixtures-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04952)
*Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Maximilian Mordig,Bernhard Schölkopf,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR-MoE提出了一种基于向量量化的倒排索引路由架构，通过两阶段过程（粗筛+精排）近似top-k路由，在细粒度MoE中提升性能并降低路由计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE（许多小专家而非少数大专家）虽然能提升性能，但会显著增加路由计算开销，甚至可能主导整体计算成本。现有方法对专家参数施加结构约束，限制了灵活性。

Method: 基于向量量化的两阶段路由：第一阶段将token分配给VQ码字以构建候选专家集（粗筛），第二阶段仅对候选集进行精确路由评分（精排）。该方法是标准路由器的即插即用替代方案，无需修改模型架构或损失函数。

Result: 在细粒度MoE设置下，AIR-MoE相比现有路由方法取得了改进的性能。论文还提供了AIR-MoE召回率的理论下界，揭示了其内部工作原理。

Conclusion: AIR-MoE通过倒排索引思想有效解决了细粒度MoE中路由成本过高的问题，在不牺牲性能且无需结构性约束的条件下，实现了高效近似top-k路由。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models enable scalable transformer architectures by activating only a subset of experts per token. Recent evidence suggests that performance improves with increasingly granular experts, i.e., many small experts instead of a few large ones. However, this regime substantially increases routing cost, which can dominate computation. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ). In a first stage, AIR-MoE performs coarse shortlisting by assigning tokens to VQ codewords to construct a candidate set of experts. In a second stage, fine scoring computes exact routing scores restricted to this shortlist. This two-stage procedure approximates true top-k routing while avoiding full expert scoring and, in contrast to prior work, imposing no structural constraints on expert parameters. AIR-MoE serves as a drop-in replacement for standard routers and requires no modifications to the model architecture or loss function. We further provide a lower bound on the mass recall achieved by AIR-MoE that yields insights into its inner workings. Empirically, we demonstrate that AIR-MoE achieves improved performance compared to existing routing approaches in granular MoE settings.

</details>


### [219] [Order-based Rehearsal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04955)
*Yu-Xuan Tao,Tian-Zuo Wang,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于序结构（而非图结构）的避免不良未来（AUF）决策方法，通过信息论方法学习序，将AUF任务转化为可微优化问题，实验表明该方法优于基于学习图的方法，甚至匹配或超越已知真实图的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有避免不良未来（AUF）的排练学习方法依赖于图结构，但从观测数据中学习图结构存在挑战且估计误差大，因此需要探索更简单且充分的序结构是否足以支持AUF决策。

Method: 证明序结构对AUF决策的充分性；提出首个基于序的排练学习方法；开发信息论方法学习序（不限制结构函数形式或噪声分布类型）；构建基于序的采样器近似决策影响；结合替代目标最大化决策后成功概率，将AUF转化为可微优化问题。

Result: 序学习方法优于现有方法；基于序的AUF方法不仅超越基于学习图或学习序的方法，还匹配甚至超越已知真实图的oracle基线。

Conclusion: 学习完整图结构对于AUF决策并非总是必要的，序结构即可充分识别决策影响，从而简化问题并提升性能。

Abstract: When a machine learning (ML) model forecasts an undesired event, one often seeks a decision to avoid it, known as the avoiding undesired future (AUF) problem. Many rehearsal learning methods have been proposed for AUF, but they rely on an underlying graph structure; learning such a graph from observational data is challenging and can incur substantial estimation error. In this work, we demonstrate that the order structure can be sufficient for AUF decision-making, and propose the first order-based rehearsal learning method. Although an order is less informative than a graph, it can be sufficient to identify the influence of decisions from observational data, suggesting that learning the entire graph is not always necessary. To learn the order, we develop an information-theoretic method that imposes no restrictions on the form of structural functions or the type of noise distributions. For AUF decision-making, we construct an order-based sampler to approximate the influence of decisions and, combined with a surrogate objective for maximizing the post-decision success probability, reduce the AUF task to a differentiable optimization problem. Experiments show that our order learning method outperforms existing methods, and that our AUF approach not only surpasses methods relying on learned graphs or learned orders, but also matches or even exceeds oracle baselines that are given the true graph.

</details>


### [220] [KernelBench-X: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04956)
*Han Wang,Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelBench-X通过176个任务15个类别的基准测试，揭示了LLM生成Triton内核的三个关键发现：任务结构比方法设计更能决定正确性（类别解释9.4%方差 vs 方法3.3%）；迭代优化提升编译率但降低性能；正确性不保证效率（46.6%正确内核慢于PyTorch基线）。量化问题完全未解决（0/30成功），表明未来需关注全局协调、数值精度建模和硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的Triton内核生成引起了广泛关注，但一个基本的实证问题仍未得到解答：这种方法在什么情况下会失效，以及为什么会失效？现有研究缺乏对这一能力边界的系统性理解。

Method: 构建KernelBench-X基准，包含176个任务、15个类别，通过类别感知评估对五种代表性方法进行正确性和硬件效率的系统比较。使用解释偏差分析、迭代优化追踪和跨硬件性能对比等方法。

Result: 1)类别解释正确性方差9.4% vs 方法3.3%；2)72%的Fusion任务全部失败；3)迭代中编译率从52.3%升至68.8%，但平均加速比从1.58x降至1.44x；4)46.6%正确内核慢于PyTorch基线；5)跨硬件加速比方差达21.4x；6)量化任务0/30成功。

Conclusion: LLM生成Triton内核的未来进步取决于处理全局协调、显式建模数值精度以及将硬件效率纳入生成过程。当前方法在任务结构理解、数值计算语义和硬件感知方面存在根本性不足。

Abstract: LLM-based Triton kernel generation has attracted significant interest, yet a fundamental empirical question remains unanswered: where does this capability break down, and why? We present KernelBench-X, a benchmark designed to answer this question through category-aware evaluation of correctness and hardware efficiency across 176 tasks in 15 categories. Our systematic comparison of five representative methods yields three main findings. First, task structure determines correctness more than method design. Category explains nearly three times more variance in semantic correctness than method (9.4% vs 3.3% explained deviance), and 72% of Fusion tasks fail across all five methods while Math tasks are solved consistently. Second, iterative refinement improves correctness, but not performance. Across GEAK iterations, compile rate rises from 52.3% to 68.8% while average speedup declines from $1.58\times$ to $1.44\times$; newly rescued kernels consistently underperform persistently correct ones ($1.16\times$ vs $1.58\times$ speedup in round~0$\to$1). Third, correctness does not imply efficiency. 46.6% of correct kernels are slower than the PyTorch eager baseline, and cross-hardware speedup variance reaches $21.4\times$. Besides, quantization remains completely unsolved (0/30 successes) despite non-trivial compilation rates, revealing systematic misunderstanding of numerical computation contracts rather than surface-level syntax errors. These findings suggest that future progress depends on handling global coordination, explicitly modeling numerical precision, and incorporating hardware efficiency into generation. The code is available at https://github.com/BonnieW05/KernelBenchX

</details>


### [221] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EP-GRPO框架，通过挖掘模型内在信息流（熵门控调制、隐式过程信号、累积熵映射）解决GRPO在token级信用分配中的三类失效问题（粒度不均、极性错位、零方差坍缩），在数学推理任务上取得更优的准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO存在三种信用分配失效：均匀token粒度忽略信息价值差异、均匀极性错误奖惩、零方差坍缩导致梯度消失。需系统量化并解决这些问题。

Method: 提出EP-GRPO框架，包含三个组件：1) 熵门控调制，优先处理高熵决策关键点；2) 基于策略散度的隐式过程信号，结合结果优势锚定提供token级方向性反馈；3) 累积熵映射实现进步对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下维持梯度流。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的广泛实验表明，EP-GRPO在准确率和效率上均优于GRPO及其变体。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过无外部奖励模型的密集自监督信号有效解决了GRPO的信用分配问题，为LLM推理提供了更高效的强化学习框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
